================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/classifier/dataset.py
================================================================================

"""
PatchDataset — loads labeled image patches for the 8-class drought classifier.

Handles:
    - Multiple patch sizes (64, 128, 256, 512) from the same class folders.
    - Mixed-format inputs (PNG for patches, JPG also accepted).
    - Class-balanced oversampling (barren class is rare — gets upsampled).
    - 9-channel input (RGB + 5 vegetation indices + V).
    - Heavy, confusion-pair-aware augmentation.
    - MixUp between specific confusion pairs (implemented manually since
      albumentations 2.x does not provide it).
    - CutMix across any pair.
    - A 'hard set' manifest for a curated validation split.

Class folders expected under patches_dir:
    crop/, cotton_ball/, dry_weed/, green_weed/, drought/, soil/, barren/
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import random
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

import albumentations as A
import cv2
import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, WeightedRandomSampler

from src.preprocessing.tensor_builder import build_9channel
from src.utils.constants import (
    CLASS_TO_IDX,
    CONFUSION_PAIRS,
    PATCH_FOLDER_TO_CLASS,
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Augmentation pipelines
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _make_train_aug(size: int = 224) -> A.Compose:
    """
    Strong augmentation designed against the specific confusion pairs:
        drought ↔ dry_weed       (both brown — need texture/structure distinction)
        drought ↔ barren         (both brown — need context distinction)
        drought ↔ crop           (harvest-stage dark cotton ≈ drought)
        soil ↔ cotton_ball       (bright soil glare ≈ white bolls)
        crop ↔ green_weed        (both green — need spatial arrangement)

    Includes:
        - Random shadow synthesis (combat misclassification of shadows as drought)
        - Random specular highlight synthesis (combat soil-glare-as-cotton)
        - Strong HSV jitter
        - Geometric augmentations (flips, rotations, elastic)
    """
    return A.Compose([
        A.Resize(size, size),
        # --- Geometric ---
        A.HorizontalFlip(p=0.5),
        A.VerticalFlip(p=0.5),
        A.RandomRotate90(p=0.5),
        A.ShiftScaleRotate(
            shift_limit=0.05, scale_limit=0.1, rotate_limit=30,
            border_mode=cv2.BORDER_REFLECT_101, p=0.5
        ),
        A.ElasticTransform(alpha=40, sigma=5, p=0.2),
        A.GridDistortion(num_steps=5, distort_limit=0.2, p=0.2),
        # --- Color/appearance  ---
        A.HueSaturationValue(
            hue_shift_limit=10, sat_shift_limit=40, val_shift_limit=30, p=0.8
        ),
        A.RandomBrightnessContrast(brightness_limit=0.3, contrast_limit=0.3, p=0.7),
        A.ColorJitter(brightness=0.2, contrast=0.2, saturation=0.3, hue=0.05, p=0.5),
        # --- Shadow synthesis: random dark elliptical region ---
        # Simulates cast shadow from crop canopy; prevents shadow → drought confusion
        A.RandomShadow(
            shadow_roi=(0, 0, 1, 1),
            num_shadows_limit=(1, 2),
            shadow_dimension=4,
            p=0.3
        ),
        # --- Noise / blur ---
        A.GaussianBlur(blur_limit=(3, 5), p=0.2),
        A.GaussNoise(std_range=(0.01, 0.05), p=0.2),
        # --- Dropout blocks: simulate occlusion by other leaves ---
        A.CoarseDropout(
            num_holes_range=(1, 4),
            hole_height_range=(0.05, 0.15),
            hole_width_range=(0.05, 0.15),
            fill=0,
            p=0.2
        ),
    ])


def _make_val_aug(size: int = 224) -> A.Compose:
    return A.Compose([A.Resize(size, size)])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# MixUp / CutMix helpers (manual since albumentations 2.x removed them)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def mixup_pair(
    img_a: np.ndarray,
    img_b: np.ndarray,
    label_a: int,
    label_b: int,
    alpha: float = 0.1,
    num_classes: int = 8,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """
    Blend two images. Returns mixed image and soft one-hot label vector.
    alpha=0.1 means the weaker image contributes 10% at most.
    """
    lam = np.random.beta(alpha, alpha)
    lam = max(lam, 1.0 - lam)   # ensure dominant class always > 0.5
    mixed = (lam * img_a + (1.0 - lam) * img_b).astype(img_a.dtype)
    label = np.zeros(num_classes, dtype=np.float32)
    label[label_a] = lam
    label[label_b] = 1.0 - lam
    return mixed, label


def cutmix_pair(
    img_a: np.ndarray,
    img_b: np.ndarray,
    label_a: int,
    label_b: int,
    alpha: float = 0.2,
    num_classes: int = 8,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """
    CutMix: paste a random rectangle from img_b onto img_a.
    """
    h, w = img_a.shape[:2]
    lam = np.random.beta(alpha, alpha)

    cut_ratio = np.sqrt(1.0 - lam)
    cut_h = int(h * cut_ratio)
    cut_w = int(w * cut_ratio)

    cx = np.random.randint(w)
    cy = np.random.randint(h)
    x1 = np.clip(cx - cut_w // 2, 0, w)
    x2 = np.clip(cx + cut_w // 2, 0, w)
    y1 = np.clip(cy - cut_h // 2, 0, h)
    y2 = np.clip(cy + cut_h // 2, 0, h)

    mixed = img_a.copy()
    mixed[y1:y2, x1:x2] = img_b[y1:y2, x1:x2]

    actual_lam = 1.0 - (x2 - x1) * (y2 - y1) / (w * h)
    label = np.zeros(num_classes, dtype=np.float32)
    label[label_a] = actual_lam
    label[label_b] = 1.0 - actual_lam
    return mixed, label


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dataset
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class PatchDataset(Dataset):
    """
    Loads all patches from data/patches/ and returns 9-channel tensors + labels.

    Args:
        patches_dir:   Path to the patches/ directory.
        split:         'train', 'val', or 'test'.
        val_split:     Fraction of data for validation.
        test_split:    Fraction of data for test.
        seed:          Random seed for reproducible splits.
        input_size:    All patches resized to this square size for the backbone.
        mixup_prob:    Probability of applying MixUp on a confusion pair.
        cutmix_prob:   Probability of applying CutMix on any pair.
        num_classes:   Total number of classes.
    """

    CONFUSION_PAIR_IDX: list[tuple[int, int]] = [
        (CLASS_TO_IDX[a], CLASS_TO_IDX[b])
        for a, b in CONFUSION_PAIRS
        if a in CLASS_TO_IDX and b in CLASS_TO_IDX
    ]

    def __init__(
        self,
        patches_dir: str | Path,
        split: str = "train",
        val_split: float = 0.15,
        test_split: float = 0.10,
        seed: int = 42,
        input_size: int = 224,
        mixup_prob: float = 0.25,
        cutmix_prob: float = 0.25,
        num_classes: int = 8,
    ) -> None:
        patches_dir = Path(patches_dir)
        assert split in ("train", "val", "test"), f"Invalid split: {split}"
        self.split = split
        self.input_size = input_size
        self.mixup_prob = mixup_prob if split == "train" else 0.0
        self.cutmix_prob = cutmix_prob if split == "train" else 0.0
        self.num_classes = num_classes

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        # Discover all images
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        self.samples: list[tuple[Path, int]] = []  # (path, class_idx)

        for folder_name, class_name in PATCH_FOLDER_TO_CLASS.items():
            folder = patches_dir / folder_name
            if not folder.is_dir():
                continue
            class_idx = CLASS_TO_IDX.get(class_name)
            if class_idx is None:
                continue
            imgs = self._find_images(folder)
            for p in imgs:
                self.samples.append((p, class_idx))

        if not self.samples:
            raise FileNotFoundError(
                f"No images found under {patches_dir}. "
                "Check that the folder names match PATCH_FOLDER_TO_CLASS."
            )

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        # Stratified split
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        rng = random.Random(seed)
        by_class: dict[int, list[tuple[Path, int]]] = defaultdict(list)
        for s in self.samples:
            by_class[s[1]].append(s)

        train_s, val_s, test_s = [], [], []
        for cls_idx, items in by_class.items():
            shuffled = items.copy()
            rng.shuffle(shuffled)
            n = len(shuffled)
            n_test = max(1, int(n * test_split))
            n_val = max(1, int(n * val_split))
            test_s.extend(shuffled[:n_test])
            val_s.extend(shuffled[n_test:n_test + n_val])
            train_s.extend(shuffled[n_test + n_val:])

        split_map = {"train": train_s, "val": val_s, "test": test_s}
        self.samples = split_map[split]

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        # Build lookup: class_idx → list of samples (for MixUp/CutMix)
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        self._by_class: dict[int, list[tuple[Path, int]]] = defaultdict(list)
        for s in self.samples:
            self._by_class[s[1]].append(s)

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        # Augmentation
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        if split == "train":
            self.aug = _make_train_aug(input_size)
        else:
            self.aug = _make_val_aug(input_size)

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        # Class counts for sampler
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        self._class_counts: dict[int, int] = defaultdict(int)
        for _, c in self.samples:
            self._class_counts[c] += 1

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def get_class_counts(self) -> dict[int, int]:
        return dict(self._class_counts)

    def get_weighted_sampler(self, oversample_barren: int = 8) -> WeightedRandomSampler:
        """
        Returns a WeightedRandomSampler for class-balanced training.
        barren class gets extra weight due to small sample count.
        """
        from src.utils.constants import CLASS_TO_IDX
        barren_idx = CLASS_TO_IDX.get("barren", -1)
        weights = []
        for _, cls in self.samples:
            count = self._class_counts[cls]
            w = 1.0 / count
            if cls == barren_idx:
                w *= oversample_barren
            weights.append(w)
        return WeightedRandomSampler(
            weights=weights,
            num_samples=len(weights),
            replacement=True,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Internal
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def _find_images(folder: Path) -> list[Path]:
        exts = {".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".tif", ".tiff", ".bmp"}
        return sorted([p for p in folder.rglob("*") if p.suffix.lower() in exts])

    def _load_rgb(self, path: Path) -> np.ndarray:
        """Load image as uint8 RGB, handling both PNG and JPG."""
        img = cv2.imread(str(path), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
        if img is None:
            raise IOError(f"Could not read image: {path}")
        return cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)

    def _random_confusion_pair_sample(
        self, anchor_class: int
    ) -> tuple[np.ndarray, int] | None:
        """Pick a random sample from a class that forms a confusion pair with anchor."""
        partner_classes = []
        for ca, cb in self.CONFUSION_PAIR_IDX:
            if ca == anchor_class and cb in self._by_class:
                partner_classes.append(cb)
            elif cb == anchor_class and ca in self._by_class:
                partner_classes.append(ca)
        if not partner_classes:
            return None
        partner_cls = random.choice(partner_classes)
        if not self._by_class[partner_cls]:
            return None
        partner_path, _ = random.choice(self._by_class[partner_cls])
        return self._load_rgb(partner_path), partner_cls

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Dataset interface
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self.samples)

    def __getitem__(self, idx: int) -> dict:
        path, label = self.samples[idx]
        img = self._load_rgb(path)

        # Record the original patch size (H, W before resize) as a feature
        orig_h, orig_w = img.shape[:2]
        orig_size = max(orig_h, orig_w)

        # Geometric + color augmentation
        img = self.aug(image=img)["image"]

        soft_label: Optional[np.ndarray] = None

        # MixUp with confusion pair (training only)
        if self.split == "train" and random.random() < self.mixup_prob:
            partner = self._random_confusion_pair_sample(label)
            if partner is not None:
                partner_img, partner_cls = partner
                partner_img = self.aug(image=partner_img)["image"]
                img, soft_label = mixup_pair(
                    img, partner_img, label, partner_cls,
                    alpha=0.15, num_classes=self.num_classes
                )

        # CutMix across any class (training only, but not if already MixUp'd)
        elif self.split == "train" and random.random() < self.cutmix_prob:
            all_classes = list(self._by_class.keys())
            partner_cls = random.choice(all_classes)
            if self._by_class[partner_cls]:
                partner_path, _ = random.choice(self._by_class[partner_cls])
                partner_img = self._load_rgb(partner_path)
                partner_img = self.aug(image=partner_img)["image"]
                img, soft_label = cutmix_pair(
                    img, partner_img, label, partner_cls,
                    alpha=0.3, num_classes=self.num_classes
                )

        # Build 9-channel tensor
        tensor = torch.from_numpy(build_9channel(img))  # (9, H, W)

        # Hard label (int) always returned alongside soft label for logging
        if soft_label is None:
            soft_label_tensor = torch.zeros(self.num_classes, dtype=torch.float32)
            soft_label_tensor[label] = 1.0
        else:
            soft_label_tensor = torch.from_numpy(soft_label)

        return {
            "image": tensor,                                   # (9, 224, 224) float32
            "label": torch.tensor(label, dtype=torch.long),   # scalar int
            "soft_label": soft_label_tensor,                   # (8,) float32
            "orig_size": orig_size,                            # int: largest dim of original patch
            "path": str(path),
        }




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/classifier/losses.py
================================================================================

"""
Loss functions for the patch classifier.

Two losses combined:
    1. SoftCrossEntropyLoss — handles both hard labels and soft labels from
       MixUp/CutMix. Class-weighted to handle barren imbalance.

    2. PairwiseMarginLoss — pulls embeddings of chronic-confusion pairs apart
       in feature space. Applied on the 388-dim embedding (before the head).
       For each confusion pair (A, B) present in the batch, the loss penalizes
       any A-embedding that is closer to a B-embedding than to another A-embedding,
       and vice versa. This directly forces the model to learn discriminative
       features for the pairs it finds hardest.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from src.utils.constants import CLASS_TO_IDX, CONFUSION_PAIRS, NUM_CLASSES


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Soft cross-entropy (handles MixUp/CutMix soft labels)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class SoftCrossEntropyLoss(nn.Module):
    """
    Cross-entropy that accepts soft (one-hot or mixed) label vectors.

    For a hard one-hot label this is identical to nn.CrossEntropyLoss.
    For soft labels from MixUp/CutMix it computes the expected log-prob.

    Class weights are applied to address the barren class imbalance.
    """

    def __init__(self, class_weights: torch.Tensor | None = None) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.register_buffer("class_weights", class_weights)

    def forward(
        self,
        logits: torch.Tensor,      # (B, C)
        soft_labels: torch.Tensor, # (B, C) — each row sums to 1
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        log_probs = F.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)  # (B, C)
        if self.class_weights is not None:
            w = self.class_weights.to(logits.device)
            # Weight each class contribution by its class weight
            weighted_log_probs = log_probs * w.unsqueeze(0)
            loss = -(soft_labels * weighted_log_probs).sum(dim=-1).mean()
        else:
            loss = -(soft_labels * log_probs).sum(dim=-1).mean()
        return loss


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Pairwise margin loss
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class PairwiseMarginLoss(nn.Module):
    """
    For each confusion pair (class_a, class_b) present in the current batch:

        For every sample with hard label = class_a:
            Find its nearest neighbour among class_a embeddings (positive).
            Find its nearest neighbour among class_b embeddings (negative).
            Apply margin loss: max(0, d_pos - d_neg + margin)

    This is a within-batch online triplet mining restricted to the
    confusion pairs defined in CONFUSION_PAIRS.

    Args:
        margin:  Minimum distance gap we want between same-class and cross-class.
        weight:  Scalar multiplier on the total loss.
    """

    PAIR_IDX: list[tuple[int, int]] = [
        (CLASS_TO_IDX[a], CLASS_TO_IDX[b])
        for a, b in CONFUSION_PAIRS
        if a in CLASS_TO_IDX and b in CLASS_TO_IDX
    ]

    def __init__(self, margin: float = 0.3, weight: float = 0.3) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.margin = margin
        self.weight = weight

    def forward(
        self,
        embeddings: torch.Tensor,  # (B, D) L2-normalized
        hard_labels: torch.Tensor, # (B,) int
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        if embeddings.shape[0] < 2:
            return embeddings.sum() * 0.0

        # L2-normalize
        emb = F.normalize(embeddings, p=2, dim=-1)
        # Pairwise L2 distances (B, B)
        dist = torch.cdist(emb, emb, p=2)

        total_loss = torch.tensor(0.0, device=embeddings.device)
        n_terms = 0

        for cls_a, cls_b in self.PAIR_IDX:
            mask_a = hard_labels == cls_a
            mask_b = hard_labels == cls_b
            if mask_a.sum() < 1 or mask_b.sum() < 1:
                continue

            # For each anchor in cls_a:
            #   positive = nearest cls_a sample (excluding self)
            #   negative = nearest cls_b sample
            a_indices = mask_a.nonzero(as_tuple=True)[0]
            for anc_idx in a_indices:
                # Positive distances (same class, excluding self)
                pos_dists = dist[anc_idx][mask_a]
                # Exclude self (distance = 0)
                pos_dists = pos_dists[pos_dists > 1e-6]
                if pos_dists.numel() == 0:
                    continue
                d_pos = pos_dists.min()

                # Negative distances (confusion partner class)
                neg_dists = dist[anc_idx][mask_b]
                d_neg = neg_dists.min()

                loss = F.relu(d_pos - d_neg + self.margin)
                total_loss = total_loss + loss
                n_terms += 1

            # Symmetric: for each anchor in cls_b
            b_indices = mask_b.nonzero(as_tuple=True)[0]
            for anc_idx in b_indices:
                pos_dists = dist[anc_idx][mask_b]
                pos_dists = pos_dists[pos_dists > 1e-6]
                if pos_dists.numel() == 0:
                    continue
                d_pos = pos_dists.min()
                neg_dists = dist[anc_idx][mask_a]
                d_neg = neg_dists.min()
                loss = F.relu(d_pos - d_neg + self.margin)
                total_loss = total_loss + loss
                n_terms += 1

        if n_terms == 0:
            return embeddings.sum() * 0.0

        return self.weight * total_loss / n_terms


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Combined loss
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class ClassifierLoss(nn.Module):
    """
    Combined loss = SoftCE + PairwiseMargin.

    Args:
        class_counts: dict mapping class_idx → sample count (used for weighting).
        barren_oversample: extra multiplier for barren weight (rare class).
        margin_weight: weight on the pairwise margin loss.
        margin: margin value for PairwiseMarginLoss.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        class_counts: dict[int, int] | None = None,
        barren_oversample: int = 8,
        margin_weight: float = 0.3,
        margin: float = 0.3,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()

        # Build class weights (inverse frequency, normalized to mean=1)
        if class_counts is not None:
            barren_idx = CLASS_TO_IDX.get("barren", -1)
            weights = torch.ones(NUM_CLASSES)
            for idx, count in class_counts.items():
                if count > 0:
                    weights[idx] = 1.0 / count
                    if idx == barren_idx:
                        weights[idx] *= barren_oversample
            weights = weights / weights.mean()
        else:
            weights = None

        self.ce_loss = SoftCrossEntropyLoss(class_weights=weights)
        self.margin_loss = PairwiseMarginLoss(margin=margin, weight=margin_weight)

    def forward(
        self,
        logits: torch.Tensor,      # (B, C)
        embeddings: torch.Tensor,  # (B, D) — pre-head features
        soft_labels: torch.Tensor, # (B, C)
        hard_labels: torch.Tensor, # (B,)
    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        """Returns a dict with 'loss', 'ce', 'margin' for logging."""
        ce = self.ce_loss(logits, soft_labels)
        margin = self.margin_loss(embeddings, hard_labels)
        total = ce + margin
        return {"loss": total, "ce": ce, "margin": margin}




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/classifier/model.py
================================================================================

"""
MultiscalePatchClassifier

Architecture:
    9-channel input (RGB + ExG + ExGR + CIVE + GLI + NGRDI + V)
        ↓
    DINOv2 ViT-S/14 backbone (ImageNet-pretrained, 9-channel via weight surgery)
        ↓
    CLS token → 384-dim embedding
        ↓
    Concatenate with 4-dim patch scale embedding (learned, not one-hot)
        ↓
    MLP head [388 → 256 → 8 classes]

Scale embedding:
    The network receives a learned embedding of the original patch size bucket
    (64 / 128 / 256 / 512 / other→256). This tells the model at what physical
    resolution it is looking, which is critical because drought vs. dry_weed
    look identical at 64px but diverge strongly at 512px.

Weight surgery for 9-channel input:
    The pretrained 3-channel patch_embed.proj conv is loaded, its weights are
    copied to the first 3 channels of a new 9-channel conv (divided by 3 to
    preserve approximate activation magnitude), and channels 3-8 are zero-init.
    The zero init is intentional: the vegetation index channels start silent and
    learn to activate only when they provide signal beyond RGB.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import timm
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from src.utils.constants import NUM_CLASSES

# Backbone model name in timm
_BACKBONE_NAME = "vit_base_patch14_dinov2.lvd142m"
_EMBED_DIM = 768   # ViT-B embed dim

# Map patch sizes to bucket indices for the scale embedding
_SIZE_BUCKETS = [64, 128, 256, 512]


def _size_to_bucket(orig_size: int) -> int:
    """Map the largest patch dimension to a bucket index 0-3."""
    for i, threshold in enumerate([96, 192, 384, 999999]):
        if orig_size <= threshold:
            return i
    return 3  # 512+


class PatchScaleEmbedding(nn.Module):
    """Learned embedding for the 4 patch-size buckets."""
    def __init__(self, num_buckets: int = 4, embed_dim: int = 4) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.emb = nn.Embedding(num_buckets, embed_dim)
        nn.init.normal_(self.emb.weight, std=0.02)

    def forward(self, orig_sizes: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Args:
            orig_sizes: (B,) int tensor of original patch sizes (px).
        Returns:
            (B, embed_dim) float tensor.
        """
        buckets = torch.tensor(
            [_size_to_bucket(s.item()) for s in orig_sizes],
            dtype=torch.long,
            device=self.emb.weight.device,
        )
        return self.emb(buckets)


class MultiscalePatchClassifier(nn.Module):
    """
    Args:
        num_classes:  Number of output classes (default 8).
        pretrained:   Load ImageNet weights for the backbone (default True).
        freeze_backbone_epochs: How many epochs to freeze the backbone.
                      Managed externally by the training loop; this flag is
                      read by the trainer, not used internally here.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        num_classes: int = NUM_CLASSES,
        pretrained: bool = True,
        pretrained_weights_path: str | None = None,
        img_size: int = 224,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.num_classes = num_classes
        self.img_size = img_size

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        # Backbone: DINOv2 ViT-B/14, loaded with 9-channel input
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        # Step 1: Load 3-channel pretrained weights (HuggingFace or local .pt/.pth file)
        w3 = None
        if pretrained:
            try:
                backbone_3ch = timm.create_model(
                    _BACKBONE_NAME,
                    pretrained=True,
                    num_classes=0,
                    img_size=img_size,
                    in_chans=3,
                )
                w3 = backbone_3ch.patch_embed.proj.weight.data.clone()  # (768, 3, 14, 14)
                del backbone_3ch
            except Exception:
                pass  # fall through to local weights path

        # Local weights override: pass pretrained_weights_path to __init__
        if w3 is None and pretrained_weights_path is not None:
            import os
            if os.path.isfile(pretrained_weights_path):
                sd = torch.load(pretrained_weights_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
                # Handle different save formats
                sd = sd.get("model", sd.get("state_dict", sd))
                tmp_backbone = timm.create_model(
                    _BACKBONE_NAME, pretrained=False, num_classes=0,
                    img_size=img_size, in_chans=3,
                )
                missing, unexpected = tmp_backbone.load_state_dict(sd, strict=False)
                if len(missing) < 10:  # tolerate head keys missing
                    w3 = tmp_backbone.patch_embed.proj.weight.data.clone()
                    print(f"[INFO] Loaded local DINOv2 weights from {pretrained_weights_path}")
                del tmp_backbone
            else:
                print(f"[WARNING] pretrained_weights_path not found: {pretrained_weights_path}")

        if w3 is None and pretrained:
            print("[WARNING] Could not load pretrained weights — using random init.")

        # Step 2: Create 9-channel model
        self.backbone = timm.create_model(
            _BACKBONE_NAME,
            pretrained=False,
            num_classes=0,
            img_size=img_size,
            in_chans=9,
        )

        # Step 3: Weight surgery — copy RGB weights, zero-init index channels
        if pretrained and w3 is not None:
            with torch.no_grad():
                w9 = self.backbone.patch_embed.proj.weight.data
                # First 3 channels: copy from pretrained, scale to preserve magnitude
                # (9-ch input has 3x more channels contributing to each feature)
                w9[:, :3, :, :] = w3 / 3.0
                # Channels 3-8: zero-init (learn from scratch, don't interfere with RGB signal)
                w9[:, 3:, :, :] = 0.0

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        # Scale embedding
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        self.scale_emb = PatchScaleEmbedding(num_buckets=4, embed_dim=4)

        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        # MLP head: backbone_dim + scale_dim → classes
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------
        head_in = _EMBED_DIM + 4   # 384 + 4 = 388
        self.head = nn.Sequential(
            nn.LayerNorm(head_in),
            nn.Linear(head_in, 256),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Dropout(0.3),
            nn.Linear(256, num_classes),
        )
        self._init_head()

    def _init_head(self) -> None:
        for m in self.head.modules():
            if isinstance(m, nn.Linear):
                nn.init.trunc_normal_(m.weight, std=0.02)
                if m.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def forward(
        self,
        images: torch.Tensor,
        orig_sizes: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Args:
            images:     (B, 9, H, W) float32
            orig_sizes: (B,) int64 — largest original patch dimension
        Returns:
            (B, num_classes) logits (unnormalized)
        """
        feat = self.backbone(images)           # (B, 384)
        scale = self.scale_emb(orig_sizes)     # (B, 4)
        combined = torch.cat([feat, scale], dim=1)  # (B, 388)
        return self.head(combined)             # (B, num_classes)

    def get_embeddings(
        self,
        images: torch.Tensor,
        orig_sizes: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Return the 388-dim embedding before the head (used for feature bank)."""
        feat = self.backbone(images)
        scale = self.scale_emb(orig_sizes)
        return torch.cat([feat, scale], dim=1)

    def freeze_backbone(self) -> None:
        """Freeze all backbone parameters. Head and scale_emb remain trainable."""
        for param in self.backbone.parameters():
            param.requires_grad = False

    def unfreeze_backbone(self) -> None:
        """Unfreeze backbone for fine-tuning."""
        for param in self.backbone.parameters():
            param.requires_grad = True

    def get_optimizer_param_groups(
        self,
        lr_head: float = 3e-4,
        lr_backbone: float = 3e-5,
    ) -> list[dict]:
        """
        Returns param groups with separate LRs for head/scale_emb vs backbone.
        Called by the trainer to set up AdamW.
        """
        head_params = list(self.head.parameters()) + list(self.scale_emb.parameters())
        backbone_params = list(self.backbone.parameters())
        return [
            {"params": head_params, "lr": lr_head, "name": "head"},
            {"params": backbone_params, "lr": lr_backbone, "name": "backbone"},
        ]




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/classifier/train.py
================================================================================

"""
Patch classifier training loop.

Usage:
    python -m src.classifier.train --config configs/config.yaml

What it does:
    1. Loads PatchDataset for train/val/test splits.
    2. Instantiates MultiscalePatchClassifier (DINOv2 ViT-S/14, 9-channel).
    3. Trains with:
        - Backbone frozen for first `warmup_epochs` epochs.
        - AdamW with separate LRs for backbone and head.
        - Cosine LR schedule.
        - SoftCE + PairwiseMargin loss.
        - Class-balanced WeightedRandomSampler.
    4. Validates each epoch: macro-F1, per-class F1, per-confusion-pair error.
    5. Saves best checkpoint to runs/classifier/best.pt.
    6. Writes training_report.json + confusion matrix PNG on completion.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
from torch.utils.tensorboard import SummaryWriter
from tqdm import tqdm

from src.classifier.dataset import PatchDataset
from src.classifier.losses import ClassifierLoss
from src.classifier.model import MultiscalePatchClassifier
from src.utils.constants import CLASS_NAMES, CONFUSION_PAIRS, NUM_CLASSES


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Metrics helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def compute_metrics(
    all_preds: list[int],
    all_labels: list[int],
) -> dict:
    """
    Compute:
        - Per-class precision, recall, F1.
        - Macro-F1.
        - Per-confusion-pair error rate.
        - Overall accuracy.
    """
    from collections import defaultdict
    n = NUM_CLASSES
    tp = np.zeros(n)
    fp = np.zeros(n)
    fn = np.zeros(n)
    correct = 0

    for pred, label in zip(all_preds, all_labels):
        if pred == label:
            tp[label] += 1
            correct += 1
        else:
            fp[pred] += 1
            fn[label] += 1

    precision = np.where(tp + fp > 0, tp / (tp + fp), 0.0)
    recall    = np.where(tp + fn > 0, tp / (tp + fn), 0.0)
    f1        = np.where(precision + recall > 0,
                         2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall), 0.0)
    macro_f1  = f1.mean()
    accuracy  = correct / max(len(all_labels), 1)

    from src.utils.constants import CLASS_TO_IDX
    confusion_pair_errors = {}
    for cls_a, cls_b in CONFUSION_PAIRS:
        idx_a = CLASS_TO_IDX.get(cls_a, -1)
        idx_b = CLASS_TO_IDX.get(cls_b, -1)
        if idx_a < 0 or idx_b < 0:
            continue
        # Error rate: fraction of class_a samples predicted as class_b + vice versa
        total_ab = sum(1 for l in all_labels if l in (idx_a, idx_b))
        confused  = sum(
            1 for p, l in zip(all_preds, all_labels)
            if (l == idx_a and p == idx_b) or (l == idx_b and p == idx_a)
        )
        err = confused / max(total_ab, 1)
        confusion_pair_errors[f"{cls_a}↔{cls_b}"] = round(err, 4)

    per_class = {
        CLASS_NAMES[i]: {
            "precision": round(float(precision[i]), 4),
            "recall":    round(float(recall[i]), 4),
            "f1":        round(float(f1[i]), 4),
        }
        for i in range(n)
    }

    return {
        "accuracy":             round(accuracy, 4),
        "macro_f1":             round(float(macro_f1), 4),
        "per_class":            per_class,
        "confusion_pair_errors": confusion_pair_errors,
    }


def save_confusion_matrix(
    all_preds: list[int],
    all_labels: list[int],
    out_path: Path,
) -> None:
    """Save a confusion matrix PNG using matplotlib."""
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    cm = np.zeros((NUM_CLASSES, NUM_CLASSES), dtype=int)
    for p, l in zip(all_preds, all_labels):
        cm[l, p] += 1

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 8))
    im = ax.imshow(cm, interpolation="nearest", cmap="Blues")
    plt.colorbar(im)
    ax.set(
        xticks=range(NUM_CLASSES),
        yticks=range(NUM_CLASSES),
        xticklabels=CLASS_NAMES,
        yticklabels=CLASS_NAMES,
        xlabel="Predicted",
        ylabel="True",
        title="Confusion Matrix",
    )
    plt.setp(ax.get_xticklabels(), rotation=45, ha="right")

    for i in range(NUM_CLASSES):
        for j in range(NUM_CLASSES):
            ax.text(j, i, str(cm[i, j]), ha="center", va="center",
                    color="white" if cm[i, j] > cm.max() * 0.5 else "black",
                    fontsize=8)

    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(out_path, dpi=120, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Training loop
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def train(config: dict) -> None:
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print(f"Device: {device}")
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        print(f"GPU: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")

    cfg = config["classifier"]
    paths = config["paths"]
    out_dir = Path(paths["runs_dir"]) / "classifier"
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    writer = SummaryWriter(log_dir=out_dir / "tb_logs")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Datasets
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    print("\nLoading datasets...")
    patches_dir = paths["patches_dir"]

    ds_train = PatchDataset(
        patches_dir=patches_dir,
        split="train",
        val_split=cfg["val_split"],
        test_split=cfg["test_split"],
        seed=cfg["seed"],
        input_size=cfg["input_size"],
        mixup_prob=0.25,
        cutmix_prob=0.25,
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
    )
    ds_val = PatchDataset(
        patches_dir=patches_dir,
        split="val",
        val_split=cfg["val_split"],
        test_split=cfg["test_split"],
        seed=cfg["seed"],
        input_size=cfg["input_size"],
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
    )
    ds_test = PatchDataset(
        patches_dir=patches_dir,
        split="test",
        val_split=cfg["val_split"],
        test_split=cfg["test_split"],
        seed=cfg["seed"],
        input_size=cfg["input_size"],
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
    )

    print(f"  train: {len(ds_train)}  val: {len(ds_val)}  test: {len(ds_test)}")
    class_counts = ds_train.get_class_counts()
    print("  class counts (train):")
    for i, name in enumerate(CLASS_NAMES):
        print(f"    {name:15} {class_counts.get(i, 0):>4}")

    sampler = ds_train.get_weighted_sampler(
        oversample_barren=cfg.get("oversample_barren", 8)
    )
    dl_train = DataLoader(
        ds_train,
        batch_size=cfg["batch_size"],
        sampler=sampler,
        num_workers=4,
        pin_memory=True,
        drop_last=True,
    )
    dl_val = DataLoader(
        ds_val,
        batch_size=cfg["batch_size"],
        shuffle=False,
        num_workers=4,
        pin_memory=True,
    )
    dl_test = DataLoader(
        ds_test,
        batch_size=cfg["batch_size"],
        shuffle=False,
        num_workers=4,
        pin_memory=True,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Model
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    print("\nBuilding model...")
    weights_path = cfg.get("pretrained_weights_path")
    model = MultiscalePatchClassifier(
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
        pretrained=True,
        pretrained_weights_path=weights_path,
        img_size=cfg["input_size"],
    ).to(device)

    total_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()) / 1e6
    trainable_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad) / 1e6
    print(f"  Total: {total_params:.1f}M  Trainable: {trainable_params:.1f}M")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Loss
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    criterion = ClassifierLoss(
        class_counts=class_counts,
        barren_oversample=cfg.get("oversample_barren", 8),
        margin_weight=cfg.get("margin_loss_weight", 0.3),
        margin=cfg.get("margin_loss_alpha", 0.3),
    ).to(device)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Optimizer & scheduler
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    warmup_epochs = cfg.get("warmup_epochs", 5)
    model.freeze_backbone()
    print(f"  Backbone frozen for first {warmup_epochs} epochs.")

    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        model.get_optimizer_param_groups(
            lr_head=cfg["lr_head"],
            lr_backbone=cfg["lr_backbone"],
        ),
        weight_decay=cfg["weight_decay"],
    )

    total_epochs = cfg["epochs"]
    scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(
        optimizer, T_max=total_epochs - warmup_epochs, eta_min=1e-6
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Training loop
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    best_val_f1 = 0.0
    best_ckpt_path = out_dir / "best.pt"
    history = []

    for epoch in range(1, total_epochs + 1):
        # Unfreeze backbone after warmup
        if epoch == warmup_epochs + 1:
            model.unfreeze_backbone()
            print(f"\nEpoch {epoch}: backbone unfrozen.")
            # Reset optimizer to apply backbone LR properly
            optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                model.get_optimizer_param_groups(
                    lr_head=cfg["lr_head"],
                    lr_backbone=cfg["lr_backbone"],
                ),
                weight_decay=cfg["weight_decay"],
            )
            scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(
                optimizer,
                T_max=total_epochs - epoch,
                eta_min=1e-6,
            )

        # ---- Train ----
        model.train()
        train_losses = {"loss": 0.0, "ce": 0.0, "margin": 0.0}
        t0 = time.time()

        for batch in tqdm(dl_train, desc=f"Epoch {epoch:3d}/{total_epochs} [train]",
                          leave=False):
            images      = batch["image"].to(device)       # (B, 9, H, W)
            soft_labels = batch["soft_label"].to(device)  # (B, C)
            hard_labels = batch["label"].to(device)       # (B,)
            orig_sizes  = batch["orig_size"]               # (B,) int — stays CPU

            optimizer.zero_grad()
            logits = model(images, orig_sizes)
            embeddings = model.get_embeddings(images, orig_sizes)
            loss_dict = criterion(logits, embeddings, soft_labels, hard_labels)
            loss_dict["loss"].backward()

            nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
            optimizer.step()

            for k in train_losses:
                train_losses[k] += loss_dict[k].item()

        if epoch > warmup_epochs:
            scheduler.step()

        n_batches = len(dl_train)
        for k in train_losses:
            train_losses[k] /= n_batches

        # ---- Validate ----
        model.eval()
        all_preds, all_labels_list = [], []
        val_loss = 0.0

        with torch.no_grad():
            for batch in tqdm(dl_val, desc=f"Epoch {epoch:3d}/{total_epochs} [val]  ",
                              leave=False):
                images      = batch["image"].to(device)
                soft_labels = batch["soft_label"].to(device)
                hard_labels = batch["label"].to(device)
                orig_sizes  = batch["orig_size"]

                logits = model(images, orig_sizes)
                embeddings = model.get_embeddings(images, orig_sizes)
                ld = criterion(logits, embeddings, soft_labels, hard_labels)
                val_loss += ld["loss"].item()

                preds = logits.argmax(dim=1).cpu().tolist()
                all_preds.extend(preds)
                all_labels_list.extend(hard_labels.cpu().tolist())

        val_loss /= max(len(dl_val), 1)
        metrics = compute_metrics(all_preds, all_labels_list)
        val_f1 = metrics["macro_f1"]
        elapsed = time.time() - t0

        # ---- Logging ----
        print(
            f"Epoch {epoch:3d}/{total_epochs} | "
            f"train_loss={train_losses['loss']:.4f} "
            f"(ce={train_losses['ce']:.4f} margin={train_losses['margin']:.4f}) | "
            f"val_loss={val_loss:.4f} | val_macro_f1={val_f1:.4f} | "
            f"acc={metrics['accuracy']:.4f} | {elapsed:.0f}s"
        )
        print("  confusion pairs:", metrics["confusion_pair_errors"])

        writer.add_scalar("Loss/train", train_losses["loss"], epoch)
        writer.add_scalar("Loss/val",   val_loss, epoch)
        writer.add_scalar("F1/val_macro", val_f1, epoch)
        for name, m in metrics["per_class"].items():
            writer.add_scalar(f"F1_per_class/val_{name}", m["f1"], epoch)

        # ---- Checkpoint ----
        epoch_record = {
            "epoch": epoch,
            "train_loss": train_losses["loss"],
            "val_loss": val_loss,
            **metrics,
        }
        history.append(epoch_record)

        if val_f1 >= best_val_f1:
            best_val_f1 = val_f1
            torch.save(
                {
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "model_state_dict": model.state_dict(),
                    "val_macro_f1": val_f1,
                    "metrics": metrics,
                    "config": config,
                },
                best_ckpt_path,
            )
            print(f"  ✓ Saved best checkpoint (val_macro_f1={val_f1:.4f})")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final test evaluation on best checkpoint
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    print(f"\nLoading best checkpoint for test evaluation...")
    ckpt = torch.load(best_ckpt_path, map_location=device)
    model.load_state_dict(ckpt["model_state_dict"])
    model.eval()

    all_preds, all_labels_list = [], []
    with torch.no_grad():
        for batch in tqdm(dl_test, desc="Test eval"):
            images     = batch["image"].to(device)
            hard_labels = batch["label"].to(device)
            orig_sizes  = batch["orig_size"]
            logits = model(images, orig_sizes)
            all_preds.extend(logits.argmax(dim=1).cpu().tolist())
            all_labels_list.extend(hard_labels.cpu().tolist())

    test_metrics = compute_metrics(all_preds, all_labels_list)
    print(f"\nTest Results:")
    print(f"  macro_f1 = {test_metrics['macro_f1']:.4f}")
    print(f"  accuracy = {test_metrics['accuracy']:.4f}")
    print("  per-class F1:")
    for cls, m in test_metrics["per_class"].items():
        print(f"    {cls:15} {m['f1']:.4f}")
    print("  confusion pair errors:")
    for pair, err in test_metrics["confusion_pair_errors"].items():
        flag = "⚠" if err > 0.07 else "✓"
        print(f"    {flag} {pair}: {err:.4f}")

    # Save confusion matrix PNG
    cm_path = out_dir / "confusion_matrix_test.png"
    save_confusion_matrix(all_preds, all_labels_list, cm_path)
    print(f"  Saved: {cm_path}")

    # Save full report
    report = {
        "best_val_macro_f1": best_val_f1,
        "test_metrics": test_metrics,
        "history": history,
        "config": config,
    }
    report_path = out_dir / "training_report.json"
    with open(report_path, "w") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)
    print(f"  Saved: {report_path}")

    writer.close()
    print(f"\nDone. Best val macro-F1: {best_val_f1:.4f}")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--config", type=Path, default=Path("configs/config.yaml"))
    args = ap.parse_args()

    import yaml
    with open(args.config) as f:
        config = yaml.safe_load(f)

    train(config)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Orthomosaic inference pipeline."""
from .predict import predict

           'compute_areas', 'drought_decision']




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/inference/memory_safe_postprocess.py
================================================================================


"""Block-wise postprocessing with global connectivity."""

import cv2
import numpy as np
import tempfile

from src.preprocessing import masks as original_masks
import importlib
original_post = importlib.import_module("src.inference.postprocess")
from src.preprocessing.disk_components import (
    strip_components,
    component_mask
)


def blocks(height, rows, halo=0):

    for y in range(0, height, rows):

        end = min(y + rows, height)
        lo = max(0, y - halo)
        hi = min(height, end + halo)

        yield y, end, lo, hi


def select_components(binary, selector, rows=256):

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
        prefix="drought_boundary_",
        dir="/content"
    ) as work:

        result = boundary_cc.analyze(
            binary,
            strip_rows=rows,
            work_dir=work
        )

        return boundary_cc.selected_mask(
            result, selector
        )


def valid_field_mask(
    rgb,
    dark_threshold=10,
    saturation_threshold=15,
    min_border_component_area=500,
    rows=256
):

    H, W = rgb.shape[:2]

    candidate = np.empty(
        (H, W), dtype=np.uint8
    )

    for y, end, _, _ in blocks(H, rows):

        block = rgb[y:end]

        maximum = block.max(axis=2)
        minimum = block.min(axis=2)

        saturation = (
            maximum.astype(np.int16)
            - minimum.astype(np.int16)
        )

        dark = (
            (maximum <= dark_threshold)
            & (saturation <= saturation_threshold)
        )

        white = (
            (maximum >= 248)
            & (saturation <= 8)
        )

        candidate[y:end] = dark | white

    bg = select_components(
        candidate,
        lambda areas, borders: (
            borders
            & (areas >= min_border_component_area)
        ),
        rows
    )

    del candidate

    result = np.empty(
        (H, W), dtype=bool
    )

    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(
        cv2.MORPH_RECT, (5, 5)
    )

    for y, end, lo, hi in blocks(H, rows, 4):

        dilated = cv2.dilate(
            bg[lo:hi].astype(np.uint8),
            kernel,
            iterations=2
        )

        result[y:end] = ~dilated[
            y-lo:end-lo
        ].astype(bool)

    return result


def compute_aux_masks(
    rgb,
    *,
    glare_v=0.95,
    glare_s=0.10,
    shadow_v=0.15,
    shadow_s=0.20,
    field_dark=10,
    field_sat=15,
    field_min_area=500,
    rows=256
):

    H, W = rgb.shape[:2]

    valid = valid_field_mask(
        rgb,
        field_dark,
        field_sat,
        field_min_area,
        rows
    )

    glare = np.empty((H, W), dtype=bool)
    shadow = np.empty((H, W), dtype=bool)

    for y, end, _, _ in blocks(H, rows):

        glare[y:end] = original_masks.glare_mask(
            rgb[y:end], glare_v, glare_s
        )

        shadow[y:end] = original_masks.shadow_mask(
            rgb[y:end], shadow_v, shadow_s
        )

    return {
        "valid_field": valid,
        "glare": glare,
        "shadow": shadow
    }


def soften_problematic_pixels(
    class_map,
    rgb,
    aux_masks=None,
    rows=256
):

    masks = (
        compute_aux_masks(rgb, rows=rows)
        if aux_masks is None
        else aux_masks
    )

    H, W = class_map.shape

    result = class_map.copy()

    for y, end, lo, hi in blocks(H, rows, 4):

        problem = (
            masks["glare"][y:end]
            | masks["shadow"][y:end]
        )

        if not problem.any():
            continue

        best = np.zeros(
            (end-y, W), dtype=np.uint8
        )

        target = result[y:end]

        for cls in range(original_post.NUM_CLASSES):

            indicator = (
                class_map[lo:hi] == cls
            ).astype(np.uint8)

            count = cv2.boxFilter(
                indicator,
                ddepth=-1,
                ksize=(9, 9),
                normalize=False
            )[y-lo:end-lo]

            dominates = (
                (count > best) & problem
            )

            target[dominates] = cls

            np.maximum(
                best, count, out=best
            )

    return result


def morphological_cleanup(class_map, rows=256):

    H, W = class_map.shape

    result = class_map.copy()

    noise = np.zeros(
        (H, W), dtype=bool
    )

    for name, minimum in (
        original_post.MIN_COMPONENT_SIZES.items()
    ):

        if minimum <= 0:
            continue

        cls = original_post.CLASS_TO_IDX.get(name)

        if cls is None:
            continue

        binary = (class_map == cls)

        if not binary.any():
            continue

        small = select_components(
            binary,
            lambda areas, borders: (
                (areas > 0)
                & (areas < minimum)
            ),
            rows
        )

        noise |= small

        del binary, small

    if not noise.any():
        return result

    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(
        cv2.MORPH_RECT, (15, 15)
    )

    for y, end, lo, hi in blocks(H, rows, 7):

        target = result[y:end]

        for cls in range(
            1, original_post.NUM_CLASSES
        ):

            valid = (
                (class_map[lo:hi] == cls)
                & ~noise[lo:hi]
            ).astype(np.uint8)

            if not valid.any():
                continue

            dilated = cv2.dilate(
                valid, kernel
            )[y-lo:end-lo]

            fill = (
                noise[y:end]
                & dilated.astype(bool)
            )

            target[fill] = cls

    return result


def tag_harvest_zones(class_map, rows=256):

    H, W = class_map.shape

    cotton_idx = original_post.CLASS_TO_IDX.get(
        "cotton_ball", 3
    )

    window = original_post.HARVEST_WINDOW

    threshold = (
        original_post.HARVEST_COTTON_DENSITY
    )

    result = np.empty(
        (H, W), dtype=bool
    )

    for y, end, lo, hi in blocks(
        H, rows, window // 2
    ):

        cotton = (
            class_map[lo:hi] == cotton_idx
        ).astype(np.float32)

        density = cv2.blur(
            cotton, (window, window)
        )

        result[y:end] = (
            density[y-lo:end-lo] > threshold
        )

    return result


def postprocess(
    class_map,
    rgb,
    skip_morph=False,
    rows=256
):

    masks = compute_aux_masks(
        rgb, rows=rows
    )

    result = class_map.copy()

    for y, end, _, _ in blocks(
        result.shape[0], rows
    ):

        result[y:end][
            ~masks["valid_field"][y:end]
        ] = 0

    result = soften_problematic_pixels(
        result, rgb, masks, rows
    )

    del masks

    if not skip_morph:

        result = morphological_cleanup(
            result, rows
        )

    harvest = tag_harvest_zones(
        result, rows
    )

    return result, harvest


# BOUNDARY_COMPONENTS_V1

from src.preprocessing import boundary_components as boundary_cc




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/inference/memory_safe_report.py
================================================================================


"""Memory-efficient drought reporting."""

import numpy as np
import tempfile
import importlib

reference = importlib.import_module(
    "src.inference.report"
)

CLASS_TO_IDX = reference.CLASS_TO_IDX

from src.preprocessing.disk_components import (
    strip_components
)

def drought_decision(
    area_stats: dict,
    harvest_mask: np.ndarray,
    class_map: np.ndarray,
    tau_fraction: float    = 0.10,
    tau_non_harvest: float = 0.08,
    tau_cc_share: float    = 0.20,
) -> dict:
    """
    Three-signal drought decision.

    Signal 1 — Drought fraction > tau_fraction (10%)
        Fraction of the agricultural area (crop+cotton+drought+dry_weed)
        classified as drought.

    Signal 2 — Non-harvest drought fraction > tau_non_harvest (8%)
        Same as Signal 1 but excludes harvest zones (high cotton_ball density)
        to avoid false positives from senescent cotton leaves around open bolls.

    Signal 3 — Largest Connected Component (CC) share > tau_cc_share (20%)
        Fraction of ALL drought pixels belonging to the single largest
        spatially connected drought region. High = coherent stress zone.
        Low = scattered noise / misclassification.

    Decision: DROUGHT if Signal 1 alone (high fraction) OR (Signal 2 AND Signal 3).
    """

    drought_idx = CLASS_TO_IDX.get("drought", 6)

    H, W = class_map.shape

    drought_binary = np.empty(
        (H, W), dtype=np.uint8
    )

    n_drought_px = 0

    for y in range(0, H, 256):

        end = min(y + 256, H)

        block = (
            class_map[y:end] == drought_idx
        )

        drought_binary[y:end] = block

        n_drought_px += int(
            np.count_nonzero(block)
        )

    largest_cc = 0
    cc_share = 0.0

    if n_drought_px > 0:

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
            prefix="drought_decision_",
            dir="/content"
        ) as work:

            result = boundary_cc.analyze(
                drought_binary,
                strip_rows=256,
                work_dir=work
            )

            largest_cc = int(result["largest"])

            cc_share = (
                largest_cc / n_drought_px
            )

            del result

    del drought_binary

    df   = area_stats['drought_fraction']
    nhdf = area_stats['non_harvest_drought_fraction']
    sig1 = df   > tau_fraction
    sig2 = nhdf > tau_non_harvest
    sig3 = cc_share > tau_cc_share

    # Updated rule: Signal 1 alone is sufficient (handles distributed row drought)
    is_drought = sig1 or (sig2 and sig3)
    confidence = round(min(1.0,
        (df   / max(tau_fraction,    1e-6)) * 0.5 +
        (nhdf / max(tau_non_harvest, 1e-6)) * 0.3 +
        float(sig3) * 0.2
    ), 3)

    return {
        'drought':                         is_drought,
        'confidence':                      confidence,
        'stress_score': confidence,
        'score_is_calibrated_probability': False,
        'decision_type': 'visual_stress_heuristic',
        'field_confirmation_required': True,
        'drought_fraction':                df,
        'non_harvest_drought_fraction':    nhdf,
        'largest_cc_share':                round(cc_share, 4),
        'signal_1_fraction':               sig1,
        'signal_2_non_harvest':            sig2,
        'signal_3_coherent':               sig3,
        'tau_fraction':                    tau_fraction,
        'tau_non_harvest':                 tau_non_harvest,
        'tau_cc_share':                    tau_cc_share,
    }


# BOUNDARY_COMPONENTS_V1

from src.preprocessing import boundary_components as boundary_cc




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/inference/postprocess.py
================================================================================

"""
Post-processing for orthomosaic segmentation predictions.
Optimised for very large images (25 000+ pixels).

All scipy.ndimage calls replaced with cv2 equivalents which are:
  - 50-100x faster for connected-component labeling
  - Use 2x less RAM (int32 vs int64 label arrays)
  - SIMD-accelerated morphological operations

Expected time on 25 000 × 25 000 image:
  Before fix  (scipy): 15-40 minutes
  After fix   (cv2):   60-90 seconds
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import cv2
import numpy as np

from src.preprocessing.masks import compute_aux_masks
from src.utils.constants import CLASS_TO_IDX, NUM_CLASSES


# Minimum pixel count for a connected component to survive per class
MIN_COMPONENT_SIZES: dict[str, int] = {
    'drought':      200,
    'dry_weed':      80,
    'green_weed':    80,
    'cotton_ball':   10,
    'crop':           0,
    'soil':           0,
    'background':     0,
}

HARVEST_COTTON_DENSITY = 0.05
HARVEST_WINDOW         = 256


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Step 1: field mask
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def apply_field_mask(
    class_map: np.ndarray,
    img_rgb:   np.ndarray,
    aux_masks=None,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Force black-border (no-data) pixels to background class 0."""
    masks = (compute_aux_masks(img_rgb) if aux_masks is None else aux_masks)
    result = class_map.copy()
    result[~masks['valid_field']] = 0
    return result


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Step 2: soften glare and shadow pixels
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def soften_problematic_pixels(
    class_map: np.ndarray,
    img_rgb:   np.ndarray,
    aux_masks=None,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Replace glare / shadow pixels with the modal class of their neighbourhood.
    Uses cv2.boxFilter (SIMD-accelerated) instead of per-pixel iteration.
    """
    masks = (compute_aux_masks(img_rgb) if aux_masks is None else aux_masks)
    problem = (masks['glare'] | masks['shadow']).astype(bool)
    if not problem.any():
        return class_map

    result = class_map.copy()
    best_count = np.zeros_like(class_map, dtype=np.uint8)

    for cls in range(NUM_CLASSES):
        indicator  = (class_map == cls).view(np.uint8)
        # Count how many neighbours belong to this class (9×9 window)
        count = cv2.boxFilter(indicator, ddepth=-1,
                               ksize=(9, 9), normalize=False)
        # Where this class dominates AND pixel is problematic → relabel
        dominates = (count > best_count) & problem
        result[dominates] = cls
        best_count = np.maximum(best_count, count)

    return result


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Step 3: morphological cleanup (cv2 version — fast)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def morphological_cleanup(class_map: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Remove small isolated components per class.

    Uses cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats (50-100x faster than scipy.ndimage.label)
    and cv2.morphologyEx for dilation (SIMD-accelerated).

    Memory: one int32 label array per class at a time = 2.5 GB for 25k×25k.
    Peak is 2.5 GB regardless of class count (arrays are reused).
    Compare to original: 7 × 5 GB (int64) = 35 GB peak.
    """
    result     = class_map.copy()
    H, W       = class_map.shape
    noise_mask = np.zeros((H, W), dtype=np.uint8)

    for cls_name, min_size in MIN_COMPONENT_SIZES.items():
        if min_size <= 0:
            continue
        cls_idx = CLASS_TO_IDX.get(cls_name)
        if cls_idx is None:
            continue

        binary = (class_map == cls_idx).view(np.uint8).copy()
        if not binary.any():
            continue

        # cv2 connected components — int32, ~2.5 GB for 25k×25k
        n_labels, labels, stats, _ = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(
            binary, connectivity=8
        )
        if n_labels <= 1:
            continue   # no foreground components

        # stats columns: LEFT, TOP, WIDTH, HEIGHT, AREA
        small_components = (
            stats[:, cv2.CC_STAT_AREA] < min_size
        ).astype(np.uint8)
        small_components[0] = 0
        
        # Process in strips to limit temporary memory.
        for row_start in range(0, H, 512):
            row_end = min(row_start + 512, H)
            noise_mask[row_start:row_end] |= (
                small_components[labels[row_start:row_end]]
            )

        # Immediately free the large label array
        del labels, stats
        import gc; gc.collect()

    if not noise_mask.any():
        return result

    # Fill noise pixels with the nearest valid class using dilation
    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT, (15, 15))
    for cls_idx in range(NUM_CLASSES):
        if cls_idx == 0:
            continue
        cls_mask    = ((class_map == cls_idx) & ~noise_mask.astype(bool)).view(np.uint8)
        if not cls_mask.any():
            continue
        dilated     = cv2.dilate(cls_mask, kernel)
        fill_region = noise_mask & dilated
        result[fill_region.astype(bool)] = cls_idx

    return result


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Step 4: harvest zone tagging
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def tag_harvest_zones(class_map: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Returns bool mask where cotton_ball density > HARVEST_COTTON_DENSITY.
    Uses cv2.blur (fast, SIMD).
    """
    cotton_idx = CLASS_TO_IDX.get('cotton_ball', 3)
    cotton     = (class_map == cotton_idx).view(np.uint8).astype(np.float32)
    density    = cv2.blur(cotton, (HARVEST_WINDOW, HARVEST_WINDOW))
    return density > HARVEST_COTTON_DENSITY


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Full pipeline
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def postprocess(
    class_map:  np.ndarray,
    img_rgb:    np.ndarray,
    skip_morph: bool = False,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """
    Full post-processing.

    Returns:
        cleaned_map:  (H, W) uint8 final class predictions
        harvest_mask: (H, W) bool harvest zone indicator
    """
    import time, gc
    t0 = time.time()

    print('    [4/6] Step 1/4: field mask ...')
    aux_masks = compute_aux_masks(img_rgb)
    class_map = apply_field_mask(class_map, img_rgb, aux_masks)
    gc.collect()
    print(f'          done ({time.time()-t0:.1f}s)')

    print('    [4/6] Step 2/4: glare/shadow softening ...')
    t1 = time.time()
    class_map = soften_problematic_pixels(class_map, img_rgb, aux_masks)
    del aux_masks
    gc.collect()
    print(f'          done ({time.time()-t1:.1f}s)')

    if not skip_morph:
        print('    [4/6] Step 3/4: morphological cleanup ...')
        t1 = time.time()
        class_map = morphological_cleanup(class_map)
        gc.collect()
        print(f'          done ({time.time()-t1:.1f}s)')
    else:
        print('    [4/6] Step 3/4: morphological cleanup SKIPPED (--skip-morph)')

    print('    [4/6] Step 4/4: harvest zone tagging ...')
    t1 = time.time()
    harvest_mask = tag_harvest_zones(class_map)
    print(f'          done ({time.time()-t1:.1f}s)')

    print(f'    [4/6] Post-processing total: {time.time()-t0:.1f}s')
    return class_map, harvest_mask




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/inference/predict.py
================================================================================

"""
Orthomosaic inference — batch 5.

Runs the trained SegFormer model over a full orthomosaic image, produces
a color-coded segmentation mask, area estimates and a drought decision.

Usage
-----
    python -m src.inference.predict \\
        --image  data/orthomosaic/field_01.jpg \\
        --model  runs/segmentation/best.pt \\
        --altitude 13.8 \\
        --out    runs/results/field_01/

Outputs in --out directory:
    mask.png        Full-resolution color-coded segmentation mask
    overlay.jpg     50% alpha overlay on original (resized to ≤8192 px wide)
    report.json     Per-class areas, drought fraction, decision
    report.pdf      One-page human-readable summary

Notes:
    - --altitude defaults to 13.8 m (from the attached Metashape report).
      Pass the actual altitude for each orthomosaic for accurate m² values.
    - --model can point to best.pt (batch4) or best_v2.pt (batch4.1).
      The pipeline is identical regardless of which model file is used.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import gc
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path

# --- MUST be set before cv2 is imported anywhere in the process ---
# Removes OpenCV's hard pixel-count limit that rejects large orthomosaics.
os.environ.setdefault('OPENCV_IO_MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS', str(2 ** 40))

import cv2
import numpy as np
import torch
import yaml
from PIL import Image

from src.inference.postprocess import postprocess
from src.inference.report import (
    compute_areas,
    drought_decision,
    save_json_report,
    save_mask_png,
    save_overlay_jpg,
    save_pdf_report,
)
from src.inference.tile import TileStitcher
from src.segmentation.model import SegFormerB2
from src.utils.constants import NUM_CLASSES


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Image loading
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def load_orthomosaic(path: Path) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Load a (potentially huge) orthomosaic JPG/TIF as uint8 RGB.

    Tries three backends in order:
        1. cv2  — fastest; works after OPENCV_IO_MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS is set.
        2. PIL  — fallback; handles images cv2 still refuses.
        3. PIL with explicit LANCZOS pre-downscale if the image is so large
           that even PIL chokes on memory (>500 Mpx).

    The env var OPENCV_IO_MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS is set at module import time above,
    so cv2 should succeed for all normally-sized orthomosaics.
    """
    Image.MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS = None   # disable PIL decompression-bomb check

    # --- Backend 1: cv2 ---
    img = cv2.imread(str(path), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
    if img is not None:
        rgb = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        del img
        gc.collect()
        print(f'  Loaded (cv2): {path.name}  {rgb.shape[1]}×{rgb.shape[0]} px')
        return rgb

    # --- Backend 2: PIL ---
    print(f'  cv2 failed for {path.name}, trying PIL ...')
    try:
        pil_img = Image.open(str(path)).convert('RGB')
        rgb = np.array(pil_img, dtype=np.uint8)
        del pil_img
        gc.collect()
        print(f'  Loaded (PIL): {path.name}  {rgb.shape[1]}×{rgb.shape[0]} px')
        return rgb
    except Exception as e:
        raise IOError(
            f'Could not read {path}. Tried cv2 and PIL. Last error: {e}'
        ) from e


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Model loading
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def load_model(
    checkpoint_path: Path,
    device: torch.device,
    config: dict,
) -> torch.nn.Module:
    """Load SegFormerB2 from a training checkpoint."""
    cfg_seg = config.get('segmentation', {})
    model   = SegFormerB2(
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
        input_size=cfg_seg.get('input_size', 512),
        pretrained_id=None,          # no download needed — we load from checkpoint
        in_channels=9,
    ).to(device)

    ckpt = torch.load(checkpoint_path, map_location=device, weights_only=False)
    if 'model_state_dict' in ckpt:
        model.load_state_dict(ckpt['model_state_dict'])
        val_miou = ckpt.get('val_mIoU', 'unknown')
        print(f'  Loaded checkpoint: {checkpoint_path.name}'
              f'  (val mIoU: {val_miou})')
    else:
        # Raw state dict
        model.load_state_dict(ckpt)
        print(f'  Loaded checkpoint: {checkpoint_path.name}')

    model.eval()
    return model


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main inference function (callable from other modules / web app)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def predict(
    image_path: str | Path,
    model_path: str | Path,
    altitude_m: float,
    out_dir: str | Path,
    config_path: str | Path = 'configs/config.yaml',
    tile_size: int = 1024,
    tile_stride: int = 768,
    batch_size: int = 8,
    skip_pdf: bool = False,
    skip_morph: bool = False,
) -> dict:
    """
    Run full inference on one orthomosaic.

    Args:
        image_path:  Path to the orthomosaic JPG.
        model_path:  Path to best.pt or best_v2.pt.
        altitude_m:  Flight altitude in metres (used for GSD calculation).
        out_dir:     Directory where outputs are saved.
        config_path: Path to configs/config.yaml.
        tile_size:   Inference tile size (1024 recommended).
        tile_stride: Stride between tiles (768 = 256 px overlap).
        batch_size:  Tiles per GPU batch.
        skip_pdf:    If True, skip PDF generation.
        skip_morph:  If True, skip morphological cleanup (faster).

    Returns:
        dict with keys: mask_path, overlay_path, json_path, pdf_path,
                        area_stats, decision.
    """
    image_path = Path(image_path)
    model_path = Path(model_path)
    out_dir    = Path(out_dir)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    with open(config_path) as f:
        config = yaml.safe_load(f)

    device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
    print(f'Device: {device}')

    # Guard against null/None altitude passed from upstream
    if altitude_m is None or altitude_m <= 0:
        print(f'[WARNING] altitude_m={altitude_m!r} is invalid — '
              f'using default 13.8 m')
        altitude_m = 13.8

    t_total = time.time()

    # 1. Load image
    print('\n[1/6] Loading orthomosaic ...')
    img_rgb = load_orthomosaic(image_path)

    # 2. Load model
    print('[2/6] Loading model ...')
    model = load_model(model_path, device, config)

    # 3. Tile inference
    print(f'[3/6] Running tiled inference (tile={tile_size}, stride={tile_stride}) ...')
    t0 = time.time()
    stitcher  = TileStitcher(
        model, device,
        tile_size=tile_size,
        stride=tile_stride,
        batch_size=batch_size,
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
    )
    class_map = stitcher.run(img_rgb)
    if device.type == 'cuda':
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    gc.collect()
    print(f'  Inference: {time.time()-t0:.1f}s')

    # 4. Post-processing
    print('[4/6] Post-processing ...')
    t0 = time.time()
    class_map, harvest_mask = postprocess(class_map, img_rgb,
                                          skip_morph=skip_morph)
    print(f'  Post-process: {time.time()-t0:.1f}s')

    # 5. Report
    print('[5/6] Computing areas and drought decision ...')
    area_stats = compute_areas(class_map, harvest_mask, altitude_m)
    decision   = drought_decision(area_stats, harvest_mask, class_map)

    print(f'\n  === DROUGHT DECISION: {"⚠  DROUGHT DETECTED" if decision["drought"] else "✓  No Drought"} ===')
    print(f'  confidence:          {decision["confidence"]:.0%}')
    print(f'  drought fraction:    {decision["drought_fraction"]:.1%} of agri area')
    print(f'  non-harvest drought: {decision["non_harvest_drought_fraction"]:.1%}')
    print()
    print('  Per-class areas:')
    for cls_name, stats in area_stats['per_class'].items():
        if stats['pixels'] == 0:
            continue
        pct = stats['area_m2'] / max(area_stats['valid_field_area_m2'], 1) * 100
        print(f'    {cls_name:14} {stats["area_m2"]:>12,.1f} m²  ({pct:.1f}%)')

    # 6. Save outputs
    print('\n[6/6] Saving outputs ...')
    mask_path    = out_dir / 'mask.png'
    overlay_path = out_dir / 'overlay.jpg'
    json_path    = out_dir / 'report.json'
    pdf_path     = out_dir / 'report.pdf'

    print('  Saving mask.png ...')
    save_mask_png(class_map, mask_path)

    print('  Saving overlay.jpg ...')
    save_overlay_jpg(img_rgb, class_map, overlay_path)

    print('  Saving report.json ...')
    save_json_report(area_stats, decision, str(image_path), json_path)

    if not skip_pdf:
        print('  Saving report.pdf ...')
        save_pdf_report(img_rgb, class_map, area_stats, decision,
                        image_path.name, pdf_path)

    elapsed = time.time() - t_total
    # Release large arrays now that all outputs are saved
    del class_map, harvest_mask, img_rgb
    gc.collect()
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

    print(f'\nDone in {elapsed:.1f}s. Outputs in {out_dir}')

    return {
        'mask_path':    str(mask_path),
        'overlay_path': str(overlay_path),
        'json_path':    str(json_path),
        'pdf_path':     str(pdf_path) if not skip_pdf else None,
        'area_stats':   area_stats,
        'decision':     decision,
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CLI
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Orthomosaic drought inference')
    ap.add_argument('--image',    type=Path, required=True,
                    help='Path to orthomosaic JPG')
    ap.add_argument('--model',    type=Path,
                    default=Path('runs/segmentation/best.pt'),
                    help='Path to model checkpoint (default: best.pt)')
    ap.add_argument('--altitude', type=float, default=13.8,
                    help='Flight altitude in metres (default: 13.8)')
    ap.add_argument('--out',      type=Path, default=None,
                    help='Output directory (default: runs/results/<image_stem>/)')
    ap.add_argument('--config',   type=Path,
                    default=Path('configs/config.yaml'))
    ap.add_argument('--tile-size',   type=int, default=1024)
    ap.add_argument('--tile-stride', type=int, default=768)
    ap.add_argument('--batch-size',  type=int, default=8)
    ap.add_argument('--skip-pdf',  action='store_true')
    ap.add_argument('--skip-morph', action='store_true')
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if args.out is None:
        args.out = Path('runs/results') / args.image.stem

    predict(
        image_path=args.image,
        model_path=args.model,
        altitude_m=args.altitude,
        out_dir=args.out,
        config_path=args.config,
        tile_size=args.tile_size,
        tile_stride=args.tile_stride,
        batch_size=args.batch_size,
        skip_pdf=args.skip_pdf,
        skip_morph=args.skip_morph,
    )


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/inference/predict_v11.py
================================================================================

"""
Orthomosaic inference — batch 5.

Runs the trained SegFormer model over a full orthomosaic image, produces
a color-coded segmentation mask, area estimates and a drought decision.

Usage
-----
    python -m src.inference.predict \\
        --image  data/orthomosaic/field_01.jpg \\
        --model  runs/segmentation/best.pt \\
        --altitude 13.8 \\
        --out    runs/results/field_01/

Outputs in --out directory:
    mask.png        Full-resolution color-coded segmentation mask
    overlay.jpg     50% alpha overlay on original (resized to ≤8192 px wide)
    report.json     Per-class areas, drought fraction, decision
    report.pdf      One-page human-readable summary

Notes:
    - --altitude defaults to 13.8 m (from the attached Metashape report).
