      Pass the actual altitude for each orthomosaic for accurate m² values.
    - --model can point to best.pt (batch4) or best_v2.pt (batch4.1).
      The pipeline is identical regardless of which model file is used.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
import json
import yaml


import argparse
import gc
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path

# --- MUST be set before cv2 is imported anywhere in the process ---
# Removes OpenCV's hard pixel-count limit that rejects large orthomosaics.
os.environ.setdefault('OPENCV_IO_MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS', str(2 ** 40))

import cv2
import numpy as np
import torch
import yaml
from PIL import Image

from src.inference.postprocess import postprocess
from src.inference.report import (
    compute_areas,
    drought_decision,
    save_json_report,
    save_mask_png,
    save_overlay_jpg,
    save_pdf_report,
)
from src.inference.tile_v11 import TileStitcher
from src.segmentation.model import SegFormerB2
from src.utils.constants import NUM_CLASSES


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Image loading
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def load_orthomosaic(path: Path) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Load a (potentially huge) orthomosaic JPG/TIF as uint8 RGB.

    Tries three backends in order:
        1. cv2  — fastest; works after OPENCV_IO_MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS is set.
        2. PIL  — fallback; handles images cv2 still refuses.
        3. PIL with explicit LANCZOS pre-downscale if the image is so large
           that even PIL chokes on memory (>500 Mpx).

    The env var OPENCV_IO_MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS is set at module import time above,
    so cv2 should succeed for all normally-sized orthomosaics.
    """
    Image.MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS = None   # disable PIL decompression-bomb check

    # --- Backend 1: cv2 ---
    img = cv2.imread(str(path), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
    if img is not None:
        rgb = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        del img
        gc.collect()
        print(f'  Loaded (cv2): {path.name}  {rgb.shape[1]}×{rgb.shape[0]} px')
        return rgb

    # --- Backend 2: PIL ---
    print(f'  cv2 failed for {path.name}, trying PIL ...')
    try:
        pil_img = Image.open(str(path)).convert('RGB')
        rgb = np.array(pil_img, dtype=np.uint8)
        del pil_img
        gc.collect()
        print(f'  Loaded (PIL): {path.name}  {rgb.shape[1]}×{rgb.shape[0]} px')
        return rgb
    except Exception as e:
        raise IOError(
            f'Could not read {path}. Tried cv2 and PIL. Last error: {e}'
        ) from e


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Model loading
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def load_model(
    checkpoint_path: Path,
    device: torch.device,
    config: dict,
) -> torch.nn.Module:
    """Load SegFormerB2 from a training checkpoint."""
    cfg_seg = config.get('segmentation', {})
    model   = SegFormerB2(
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
        input_size=cfg_seg.get('input_size', 512),
        pretrained_id=None,          # no download needed — we load from checkpoint
        in_channels=9,
    ).to(device)

    ckpt = torch.load(checkpoint_path, map_location=device, weights_only=False)
    if 'model_state_dict' in ckpt:
        model.load_state_dict(ckpt['model_state_dict'])
        val_miou = ckpt.get('val_mIoU', 'unknown')
        print(f'  Loaded checkpoint: {checkpoint_path.name}'
              f'  (val mIoU: {val_miou})')
    else:
        # Raw state dict
        model.load_state_dict(ckpt)
        print(f'  Loaded checkpoint: {checkpoint_path.name}')

    model.eval()
    return model


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main inference function (callable from other modules / web app)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------



# MEMORY_SAFE_INTEGRATION_V1

import os as _memory_os

from src.inference.memory_safe_postprocess import (
    postprocess as _memory_postprocess
)

from src.inference.memory_safe_report import (
    drought_decision as _memory_drought_decision
)


def _use_memory_safe(class_map):

    return (
        class_map.size >= 100_000_000
        or _memory_os.environ.get(
            "DROUGHT_FORCE_MEMORY_SAFE", "0"
        ) == "1"
    )


def _dispatch_postprocess(
    class_map, img_rgb, *args, **kwargs
):

    if _use_memory_safe(class_map):

        print("[Memory-safe] Block-wise postprocessing")

        return _memory_postprocess(
            class_map, img_rgb,
            *args, **kwargs
        )

    return postprocess(
        class_map, img_rgb,
        *args, **kwargs
    )


def _dispatch_drought_decision(
    area_stats, harvest_mask,
    class_map, *args, **kwargs
):

    if _use_memory_safe(class_map):

        print("[Memory-safe] Disk-backed drought decision")

        return _memory_drought_decision(
            area_stats, harvest_mask,
            class_map, *args, **kwargs
        )

    return drought_decision(
        area_stats, harvest_mask,
        class_map, *args, **kwargs
    )


def predict(
    image_path: str | Path,
    model_path: str | Path,
    altitude_m: float,
    out_dir: str | Path,
    config_path: str | Path = 'configs/config.yaml',
    tile_size: int = 1024,
    tile_stride: int = 768,
    batch_size: int = 8,
    skip_pdf: bool = False,
    skip_morph: bool = False,
) -> dict:
    """
    Run full inference on one orthomosaic.

    Args:
        image_path:  Path to the orthomosaic JPG.
        model_path:  Path to best.pt or best_v2.pt.
        altitude_m:  Flight altitude in metres (used for GSD calculation).
        out_dir:     Directory where outputs are saved.
        config_path: Path to configs/config.yaml.
        tile_size:   Inference tile size (1024 recommended).
        tile_stride: Stride between tiles (768 = 256 px overlap).
        batch_size:  Tiles per GPU batch.
        skip_pdf:    If True, skip PDF generation.
        skip_morph:  If True, skip morphological cleanup (faster).

    Returns:
        dict with keys: mask_path, overlay_path, json_path, pdf_path,
                        area_stats, decision.
    """
    image_path = Path(image_path)
    model_path = Path(model_path)
    out_dir    = Path(out_dir)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    with open(config_path) as f:
        config = yaml.safe_load(f)

    device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
    print(f'Device: {device}')

    # Guard against null/None altitude passed from upstream
    if altitude_m is None or altitude_m <= 0:
        print(f'[WARNING] altitude_m={altitude_m!r} is invalid — '
              f'using default 13.8 m')
        altitude_m = 13.8

    t_total = time.time()

    # 1. Load image
    print('\n[1/6] Loading orthomosaic ...')
    img_rgb = load_orthomosaic(image_path)

    # 2. Load model
    print('[2/6] Loading model ...')
    model = load_model(model_path, device, config)

    # 3. Tile inference
    print(f'[3/6] Running tiled inference (tile={tile_size}, stride={tile_stride}) ...')
    t0 = time.time()
    stitcher  = TileStitcher(
        model, device,
        tile_size=tile_size,
        stride=tile_stride,
        batch_size=batch_size,
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
    )
    class_map = stitcher.run(img_rgb)
    if device.type == 'cuda':
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    gc.collect()
    print(f'  Inference: {time.time()-t0:.1f}s')

    # 4. Post-processing
    print('[4/6] Post-processing ...')
    t0 = time.time()
    class_map, harvest_mask = _dispatch_postprocess(class_map, img_rgb,
                                          skip_morph=skip_morph)
    print(f'  Post-process: {time.time()-t0:.1f}s')

    # 5. Report
    print('[5/6] Computing areas and drought decision ...')

    registry_file = (
        Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
        / "configs/field_gsd_registry.json"
    )

    field_registry = json.loads(
        registry_file.read_text()
    )

    field_name = Path(image_path).name
    field_info = field_registry.get(field_name)

    if field_info is None:
        raise ValueError(
            f"No GSD registry entry for {field_name}"
        )

    expected_shape = (
        field_info["actual_height"],
        field_info["actual_width"]
    )

    if class_map.shape != expected_shape:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Unexpected mask dimensions: "
            f"{class_map.shape} vs {expected_shape}"
        )

    field_gsd = field_info["gsd_mm_per_pixel"]
    field_altitude = field_info["altitude_m"]

    print(
        f"    Reported orthomosaic GSD: "
        f"{field_gsd} mm/pixel"
    )

    if not field_info["dimensions_match"]:
        print(
            "    WARNING: GSD export dimensions "
            "require verification."
        )

    if field_info["local_coordinates"]:
        print(
            "    WARNING: Local coordinate "
            "physical scale is unverified."
        )

    area_stats = compute_areas(
        class_map,
        harvest_mask,
        field_altitude,
        gsd_override_mm=field_gsd
    )

    area_stats["gsd_source"] = field_info["source"]
    area_stats["gsd_dimensions_match"] = (
        field_info["dimensions_match"]
    )
    area_stats["physical_scale_verified"] = (
        field_info["physical_scale_verified"]
    )
    area_stats["area_is_estimate"] = True
    area_stats["area_requires_review"] = (
        not field_info["dimensions_match"]
        or field_info["local_coordinates"]
    )
    decision_cfg = yaml.safe_load(
        Path(config_path).read_text()
    )["inference"]["drought_decision"]
    decision   = _dispatch_drought_decision(
        area_stats,
        harvest_mask,
        class_map,
        tau_fraction=decision_cfg["tau1_drought_fraction"],
        tau_non_harvest=decision_cfg["tau3_nonharvest_fraction"],
        tau_cc_share=decision_cfg["tau2_largest_cc_share"]
    )

    print(f'\n  === DROUGHT DECISION: {"⚠  VISUAL DROUGHT-STRESS FLAG" if decision["drought"] else "✓  No field-level stress flag"} ===')
    print(f'  stress score:        {decision["confidence"]:.0%}')
    print(f'  drought fraction:    {decision["drought_fraction"]:.6%} of agri area')
    print(f'  non-harvest drought: {decision["non_harvest_drought_fraction"]:.6%}')
    print()
    print('  Per-class areas:')
    for cls_name, stats in area_stats['per_class'].items():
        if stats['pixels'] == 0:
            continue
        pct = stats['area_m2'] / max(area_stats['valid_field_area_m2'], 1) * 100
        print(f'    {cls_name:14} {stats["area_m2"]:>12,.1f} m²  ({pct:.1f}%)')

    # 6. Save outputs
    print('\n[6/6] Saving outputs ...')
    mask_path    = out_dir / 'mask.png'
    overlay_path = out_dir / 'overlay.jpg'
    json_path    = out_dir / 'report.json'
    pdf_path     = out_dir / 'report.pdf'

    print('  Saving mask.png ...')
    save_mask_png(class_map, mask_path)

    print('  Saving overlay.jpg ...')
    save_overlay_jpg(img_rgb, class_map, overlay_path)

    print('  Saving report.json ...')
    save_json_report(area_stats, decision, str(image_path), json_path)

    if not skip_pdf:
        print('  Saving report.pdf ...')
        save_pdf_report(img_rgb, class_map, area_stats, decision,
                        image_path.name, pdf_path)

    elapsed = time.time() - t_total
    # Release large arrays now that all outputs are saved
    del class_map, harvest_mask, img_rgb
    gc.collect()
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

    print(f'\nDone in {elapsed:.1f}s. Outputs in {out_dir}')

    return {
        'mask_path':    str(mask_path),
        'overlay_path': str(overlay_path),
        'json_path':    str(json_path),
        'pdf_path':     str(pdf_path) if not skip_pdf else None,
        'area_stats':   area_stats,
        'decision':     decision,
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CLI
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Orthomosaic drought inference')
    ap.add_argument('--image',    type=Path, required=True,
                    help='Path to orthomosaic JPG')
    ap.add_argument('--model',    type=Path,
                    default=Path('runs/segmentation/best.pt'),
                    help='Path to model checkpoint (default: best.pt)')
    ap.add_argument('--altitude', type=float, default=13.8,
                    help='Flight altitude in metres (default: 13.8)')
    ap.add_argument('--out',      type=Path, default=None,
                    help='Output directory (default: runs/results/<image_stem>/)')
    ap.add_argument('--config',   type=Path,
                    default=Path('configs/config.yaml'))
    ap.add_argument('--tile-size',   type=int, default=1024)
    ap.add_argument('--tile-stride', type=int, default=768)
    ap.add_argument('--batch-size',  type=int, default=8)
    ap.add_argument('--skip-pdf',  action='store_true')
    ap.add_argument('--skip-morph', action='store_true')
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if args.out is None:
        args.out = Path('runs/results') / args.image.stem

    predict(
        image_path=args.image,
        model_path=args.model,
        altitude_m=args.altitude,
        out_dir=args.out,
        config_path=args.config,
        tile_size=args.tile_size,
        tile_stride=args.tile_stride,
        batch_size=args.batch_size,
        skip_pdf=args.skip_pdf,
        skip_morph=args.skip_morph,
    )


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/inference/report.py
================================================================================

"""
Report generation for orthomosaic predictions.

Outputs per orthomosaic:
    mask.png       Full-resolution color-coded segmentation mask
    overlay.jpg    50% alpha overlay on original with class legend (max 8192px wide)
    report.json    Per-class areas, drought decision, all metrics
    report.pdf     Detailed report: legend, area table, decision signals,
                   metric definitions glossary
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import numpy as np

from src.preprocessing.gsd import gsd_mm_per_pixel, pixels_to_m2
from src.utils.constants import CLASS_NAMES, CLASS_TO_IDX, NUM_CLASSES, PALETTE

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Class descriptions for the report legend
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CLASS_DESCRIPTIONS = {
    'background':   'No-data area outside the flight coverage boundary.',
    'soil':         'Bare or partially bare agricultural soil with no active crop cover.',
    'crop':         'Active standing crop canopy (primarily maize at vegetative stage).',
    'cotton_ball':  'Open cotton bolls visible — indicates harvest-ready or harvest stage.',
    'green_weed':   'Non-crop green vegetation (weeds) within or around the field.',
    'dry_weed':     'Dry or senescent weed vegetation; tan/brown in appearance.',
    'drought':      'Crop canopy showing stress symptoms: brown/red discolouration, '
                    'wilting, or die-back indicative of water deficit.',
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Area computation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def compute_areas(
    class_map: np.ndarray,
    harvest_mask: np.ndarray,
    altitude_m: float,
    gsd_override_mm=None,
) -> dict:
    """
    Compute per-class pixel counts and areas in m².

    Drought fraction is computed over the agricultural footprint only
    (crop + cotton_ball + drought + dry_weed) — not over soil or background.
    """
    gsd_mm  = (gsd_mm_per_pixel(altitude_m) if gsd_override_mm is None else float(gsd_override_mm))
    px_m2   = (gsd_mm / 1000.0) ** 2

    areas: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for cls_idx, cls_name in enumerate(CLASS_NAMES):
        count    = int((class_map == cls_idx).sum())
        area_m2  = count * px_m2
        areas[cls_name] = {'pixels': count, 'area_m2': round(area_m2, 2)}

    total_px       = int((class_map < NUM_CLASSES).sum()) - int((class_map == 0).sum())
    valid_field_px = total_px

    agri_classes   = ['crop', 'cotton_ball', 'drought', 'dry_weed']
    agri_px        = sum(areas[c]['pixels'] for c in agri_classes)
    drought_px     = areas['drought']['pixels']

    drought_fraction = drought_px / max(agri_px, 1)

    non_harvest_drought = int(
        ((class_map == CLASS_TO_IDX.get('drought', 6)) & ~harvest_mask).sum()
    )
    non_harvest_agri = 0

    for cls_name in (
        "crop", "cotton_ball", "drought", "dry_weed"
    ):
        cls_idx = CLASS_TO_IDX[cls_name]

        non_harvest_agri += int(
            np.count_nonzero(
                (class_map == cls_idx) & ~harvest_mask
            )
        )

    non_harvest_agri = max(non_harvest_agri, 1)
    non_harvest_drought_frac = non_harvest_drought / non_harvest_agri

    return {
        'gsd_mm_per_pixel':               round(gsd_mm, 3),
        'pixel_area_m2':                  px_m2,
        'altitude_m':                     altitude_m,
        'valid_field_pixels':             valid_field_px,
        'valid_field_area_m2':            round(valid_field_px * px_m2, 1),
        'agricultural_pixels':            agri_px,
        'agricultural_area_m2':           round(agri_px * px_m2, 1),
        'drought_fraction':               drought_fraction,
        'non_harvest_drought_fraction':   non_harvest_drought_frac,
        'per_class':                      areas,
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Drought decision
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def drought_decision(
    area_stats: dict,
    harvest_mask: np.ndarray,
    class_map: np.ndarray,
    tau_fraction: float    = 0.10,
    tau_non_harvest: float = 0.08,
    tau_cc_share: float    = 0.20,
) -> dict:
    """
    Three-signal drought decision.

    Signal 1 — Drought fraction > tau_fraction (10%)
        Fraction of the agricultural area (crop+cotton+drought+dry_weed)
        classified as drought.

    Signal 2 — Non-harvest drought fraction > tau_non_harvest (8%)
        Same as Signal 1 but excludes harvest zones (high cotton_ball density)
        to avoid false positives from senescent cotton leaves around open bolls.

    Signal 3 — Largest Connected Component (CC) share > tau_cc_share (20%)
        Fraction of ALL drought pixels belonging to the single largest
        spatially connected drought region. High = coherent stress zone.
        Low = scattered noise / misclassification.

    Decision: DROUGHT if Signal 1 alone (high fraction) OR (Signal 2 AND Signal 3).
    """
    drought_idx    = CLASS_TO_IDX.get('drought', 6)
    drought_binary = (class_map == drought_idx).astype(np.uint8)
    n_drought_px   = int(drought_binary.sum())

    num_labels, labels, stats, _ = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(
        drought_binary, connectivity=8
    )
    if num_labels > 1:
        cc_sizes   = stats[1:, cv2.CC_STAT_AREA]
        largest_cc = int(cc_sizes.max()) if len(cc_sizes) > 0 else 0
        cc_share   = largest_cc / max(n_drought_px, 1)
    else:
        largest_cc, cc_share = 0, 0.0

    df   = area_stats['drought_fraction']
    nhdf = area_stats['non_harvest_drought_fraction']
    sig1 = df   > tau_fraction
    sig2 = nhdf > tau_non_harvest
    sig3 = cc_share > tau_cc_share

    # Updated rule: Signal 1 alone is sufficient (handles distributed row drought)
    is_drought = sig1 or (sig2 and sig3)
    confidence = round(min(1.0,
        (df   / max(tau_fraction,    1e-6)) * 0.5 +
        (nhdf / max(tau_non_harvest, 1e-6)) * 0.3 +
        float(sig3) * 0.2
    ), 3)

    return {
        'drought':                         is_drought,
        'confidence':                      confidence,
        'stress_score': confidence,
        'score_is_calibrated_probability': False,
        'decision_type': 'visual_stress_heuristic',
        'field_confirmation_required': True,
        'drought_fraction':                df,
        'non_harvest_drought_fraction':    nhdf,
        'largest_cc_share':                round(cc_share, 4),
        'signal_1_fraction':               sig1,
        'signal_2_non_harvest':            sig2,
        'signal_3_coherent':               sig3,
        'tau_fraction':                    tau_fraction,
        'tau_non_harvest':                 tau_non_harvest,
        'tau_cc_share':                    tau_cc_share,
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Visual outputs
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_color_mask(class_map: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Convert class-index map to (H, W, 3) uint8 RGB color mask."""
    rgb = np.zeros((*class_map.shape, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
    for cls_idx, color in enumerate(PALETTE):
        rgb[class_map == cls_idx] = color
    return rgb


def _draw_legend_panel(height: int, width: int = 220) -> np.ndarray:
    """Build a white legend panel with colour swatches for every class."""
    panel = np.full((height, width, 3), 255, dtype=np.uint8)
    font  = cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX
    y     = 25
    cv2.putText(panel, 'LEGEND', (10, y), font, 0.55, (30, 30, 30), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    y += 8
    cv2.line(panel, (10, y), (width - 10, y), (180, 180, 180), 1)
    y += 15
    for cls_idx, cls_name in enumerate(CLASS_NAMES):
        if cls_name == 'background':
            continue
        color_rgb = PALETTE[cls_idx]
        color_bgr = (int(color_rgb[2]), int(color_rgb[1]), int(color_rgb[0]))
        cv2.rectangle(panel, (10, y - 12), (28, y + 4), color_bgr, -1)
        cv2.rectangle(panel, (10, y - 12), (28, y + 4), (100, 100, 100), 1)
        cv2.putText(panel, cls_name, (34, y), font, 0.42,
                    (30, 30, 30), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
        y += 22
    return panel


def save_mask_png(class_map: np.ndarray, path: Path) -> None:
    """Save segmentation as indexed PNG with original class colors."""
    from PIL import Image
    Image.MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS = None

    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    image = Image.fromarray(class_map.astype(np.uint8, copy=False), mode='P')

    palette = []
    for color in PALETTE:
        palette.extend([int(v) for v in color[:3]])

    palette.extend([0] * (768 - len(palette)))
    image.putpalette(palette)
    image.save(str(path), format='PNG')


def save_overlay_jpg(
    img_rgb: np.ndarray,
    class_map: np.ndarray,
    path: Path,
    max_width: int = 8192,
) -> None:
    """
    Save semi-transparent overlay with legend panel on the right.
    Resizes to max_width if the image is very large.
    """
    H, W = img_rgb.shape[:2]
    if W > max_width:
        scale  = max_width / W
        img_s  = cv2.resize(img_rgb,   (max_width, int(H * scale)),
                             interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
        mask_s = cv2.resize(class_map, (max_width, int(H * scale)),
                             interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
    else:
        img_s, mask_s = img_rgb, class_map

    color_s = make_color_mask(mask_s)
    overlay = (0.5 * img_s.astype(np.float32) +
               0.5 * color_s.astype(np.float32)).astype(np.uint8)

    # Stat bar at bottom
    names  = CLASS_NAMES
    total  = max((mask_s > 0).sum(), 1)
    stats  = ' | '.join(
        f'{names[i]}={int((mask_s==i).sum())/total*100:.1f}%'
        for i in range(NUM_CLASSES)
        if (mask_s == i).sum() > 0 and names[i] != 'background'
    )
    bar_h  = 28
    bar    = np.full((bar_h, overlay.shape[1], 3), 240, dtype=np.uint8)
    cv2.putText(bar, stats, (8, 19), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
                0.42, (40, 40, 40), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    overlay = np.vstack([overlay, bar])

    # Legend panel on the right
    legend    = _draw_legend_panel(overlay.shape[0])
    composite = np.hstack([overlay, legend])

    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    cv2.imwrite(str(path), cv2.cvtColor(composite, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR),
                [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 88])


def save_json_report(
    area_stats: dict,
    decision: dict,
    img_path: str,
    out_path: Path,
) -> None:
    report = {
        'source_image':     img_path,
        'area_stats':       area_stats,
        'drought_decision': decision,
    }
    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(out_path, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# PDF report
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def save_pdf_report(
    img_rgb: np.ndarray,
    class_map: np.ndarray,
    area_stats: dict,
    decision: dict,
    img_name: str,
    out_path: Path,
) -> None:
    """
    Detailed PDF report:
        - Drought decision banner
        - Original thumbnail | colour mask | class legend with descriptions
        - Per-class area breakdown table
        - Decision signals table with metric values and thresholds
        - Full glossary: definitions of every metric used
    """
    try:
        from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
        from reportlab.lib.units import cm
        from reportlab.lib import colors
        from reportlab.platypus import (
            SimpleDocTemplate, Table, TableStyle,
            Paragraph, Spacer, Image as RLImage, HRFlowable,
        )
        from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
        import tempfile, os
        from PIL import Image as PILImage
    except ImportError:
        print('  [WARNING] reportlab not available — skipping PDF')
        return

    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    doc    = SimpleDocTemplate(str(out_path), pagesize=A4,
                               leftMargin=1.8*cm, rightMargin=1.8*cm,
                               topMargin=1.5*cm, bottomMargin=1.5*cm)
    styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
    title_style = ParagraphStyle('t',  parent=styles['Title'],    fontSize=14, spaceAfter=4)
    h2_style    = ParagraphStyle('h2', parent=styles['Heading2'], fontSize=10, spaceBefore=8, spaceAfter=3)
    h3_style    = ParagraphStyle('h3', parent=styles['Heading3'], fontSize=9,  spaceBefore=5, spaceAfter=2)
    body_style  = ParagraphStyle('b',  parent=styles['Normal'],   fontSize=8,  leading=11)
    small_style = ParagraphStyle('s',  parent=styles['Normal'],   fontSize=7,  leading=10,
                                 textColor=colors.HexColor('#333333'))

    is_drought = decision['drought']
    story      = []

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:

        # ── Banner ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        story.append(Paragraph(f'Drought Analysis Report — {img_name}', title_style))
        call_col  = '#CC0000' if is_drought else '#007700'
        call_text = '⚠  VISUAL DROUGHT-STRESS FLAG' if is_drought else '✓  No field-level stress flag'
        story.append(Paragraph(
            f'<b><font color="{call_col}" size="12">{call_text}</font></b>'
            f'&nbsp;&nbsp;<font size="8" color="grey">'
            f'Heuristic stress score: {decision["confidence"]:.0%}</font>',
            ParagraphStyle('call', parent=styles['Normal'],
                           fontSize=12, spaceAfter=6)))
        story.append(HRFlowable(width='100%', thickness=1,
                                color=colors.HexColor(call_col)))
        story.append(Spacer(1, 0.3*cm))

        # ── Images + Legend ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        story.append(Paragraph('Field Overview', h2_style))

        H, W   = img_rgb.shape[:2]
        scale  = min(1.0, 300 / max(H, W))
        th_w, th_h = int(W * scale), int(H * scale)
        thumb  = cv2.resize(img_rgb,            (th_w, th_h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
        small_labels = cv2.resize(
            class_map, (th_w, th_h),
            interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST
        )
        c_res = make_color_mask(small_labels)

        tp = os.path.join(tmpdir, 'thumb.jpg')
        mp = os.path.join(tmpdir, 'mask.png')
        cv2.imwrite(tp, cv2.cvtColor(thumb, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))
        PILImage.fromarray(c_res).save(mp)

        img_w = 6.0*cm
        img_h = img_w * th_h / th_w

        # Legend table
        leg_rows = [[Paragraph('<b>Class</b>', small_style),
                     Paragraph('<b>Description</b>', small_style)]]
        for cls_idx, cls_name in enumerate(CLASS_NAMES):
            if cls_name == 'background':
                continue
            rgb = PALETTE[cls_idx]
            sw_color = colors.Color(rgb[0]/255, rgb[1]/255, rgb[2]/255)
            swatch   = Table([['']], colWidths=[0.4*cm], rowHeights=[0.35*cm])
            swatch.setStyle(TableStyle([
                ('BACKGROUND', (0,0),(0,0), sw_color),
                ('GRID',       (0,0),(0,0), 0.5, colors.grey),
            ]))
            leg_rows.append([
                Table([[swatch, Paragraph(f' {cls_name}', small_style)]],
                      colWidths=[0.5*cm, 2.3*cm]),
                Paragraph(CLASS_DESCRIPTIONS.get(cls_name, ''), small_style),
            ])
        leg_tbl = Table(leg_rows, colWidths=[2.9*cm, 7.0*cm])
        leg_tbl.setStyle(TableStyle([
            ('BACKGROUND', (0,0),(-1,0), colors.HexColor('#444444')),
            ('TEXTCOLOR',  (0,0),(-1,0), colors.white),
            ('GRID',  (0,0),(-1,-1), 0.3, colors.lightgrey),
            ('FONTSIZE',      (0,0),(-1,-1), 7),
            ('TOPPADDING',    (0,0),(-1,-1), 2),
            ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0,0),(-1,-1), 2),
        ]))

        img_row = [[RLImage(tp, width=img_w, height=img_h),
                    RLImage(mp, width=img_w, height=img_h),
                    leg_tbl]]
        img_tbl = Table(img_row, colWidths=[img_w+0.3*cm, img_w+0.3*cm, 10.2*cm])
        img_tbl.setStyle(TableStyle([
            ('ALIGN',  (0,0),(-1,-1), 'CENTER'),
            ('VALIGN', (0,0),(-1,-1), 'TOP'),
        ]))
        story.append(img_tbl)
        story.append(Paragraph(
            '<i>Left: original orthomosaic &nbsp; '
            'Centre: segmentation mask &nbsp; '
            'Right: class legend with descriptions</i>', small_style))
        story.append(Spacer(1, 0.3*cm))

        # ── Area breakdown ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        story.append(Paragraph('Area Breakdown', h2_style))
        story.append(Paragraph(
            f'Area estimate (physical scale not independently verified): '
            f'<b>{"Review required" if area_stats.get("area_requires_review") else "Estimated"}</b><br/>'
            f'GSD: <b>{area_stats["gsd_mm_per_pixel"]} mm/px</b>  |  '
            f'Altitude: <b>{str(area_stats["altitude_m"]) + " m" if area_stats["altitude_m"] is not None else "Unknown"}</b>  |  '
            f'Field area: <b>{area_stats["valid_field_area_m2"]:,.0f} m²</b>  |  '
            f'Agricultural area: <b>{area_stats["agricultural_area_m2"]:,.0f} m²</b>',
            body_style))
        story.append(Spacer(1, 0.1*cm))

        valid  = max(area_stats['valid_field_area_m2'], 1)
        agri   = max(area_stats['agricultural_area_m2'], 1)
        a_data = [[Paragraph('<b>Class</b>',      small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>Pixels</b>',     small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>Area (m²)</b>',  small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>% of field</b>', small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>% of agri</b>',  small_style)]]
        for cls_name in CLASS_NAMES:
            if cls_name == 'background':
                continue
            d  = area_stats['per_class'].get(cls_name, {})
            a  = d.get('area_m2', 0)
            px = d.get('pixels', 0)
            pf = a / valid * 100
            pa = f'{a/agri*100:.1f}%' if cls_name in \
                 ['crop','cotton_ball','drought','dry_weed'] else '—'
            a_data.append([
                Paragraph(cls_name, small_style),
                Paragraph(f'{px:,}',     small_style),
                Paragraph(f'{a:,.1f}',   small_style),
                Paragraph(f'{pf:.1f}%',  small_style),
                Paragraph(pa,            small_style),
            ])
        at = Table(a_data, colWidths=[2.8*cm, 3.0*cm, 3.0*cm, 2.5*cm, 2.5*cm])
        at.setStyle(TableStyle([
            ('BACKGROUND', (0,0),(-1,0), colors.HexColor('#444444')),
            ('TEXTCOLOR',  (0,0),(-1,0), colors.white),
            ('GRID',  (0,0),(-1,-1), 0.3, colors.lightgrey),
            ('ROWBACKGROUNDS', (0,1),(-1,-1),
             [colors.white, colors.HexColor('#f5f5f5')]),
            ('FONTSIZE',      (0,0),(-1,-1), 7),
            ('TOPPADDING',    (0,0),(-1,-1), 2),
            ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0,0),(-1,-1), 2),
        ]))
        story.append(at)
        story.append(Spacer(1, 0.3*cm))

        # ── Decision signals ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
        story.append(Paragraph('Drought Decision Signals', h2_style))
        s_data = [[Paragraph('<b>Signal</b>',    small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>Metric</b>',     small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>Value</b>',      small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>Threshold</b>',  small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>Triggered</b>',  small_style)]]
        rows = [
            ('Signal 1', 'Drought fraction (all agri area)',
             f'{decision["drought_fraction"]:.6%}',
             f'> {decision["tau_fraction"]:.0%}',
             decision['signal_1_fraction']),
            ('Signal 2', 'Non-harvest drought fraction',
             f'{decision["non_harvest_drought_fraction"]:.6%}',
             f'> {decision["tau_non_harvest"]:.0%}',
             decision['signal_2_non_harvest']),
            ('Signal 3', 'Largest CC share (spatial coherence)',
             f'{decision["largest_cc_share"]:.1%}',
             f'> {decision["tau_cc_share"]:.0%}',
             decision['signal_3_coherent']),
        ]
        for r in rows:
            tick = '✓  Yes' if r[4] else '✗  No'
            col  = 'green'  if r[4] else 'red'
            s_data.append([
                Paragraph(r[0], small_style),
                Paragraph(r[1], small_style),
                Paragraph(f'<b>{r[2]}</b>', small_style),
                Paragraph(r[3], small_style),
                Paragraph(f'<font color="{col}"><b>{tick}</b></font>', small_style),
            ])
        st = Table(s_data, colWidths=[2.0*cm, 5.8*cm, 2.2*cm, 2.3*cm, 1.8*cm])
        st.setStyle(TableStyle([
            ('BACKGROUND', (0,0),(-1,0), colors.HexColor('#444444')),
            ('TEXTCOLOR',  (0,0),(-1,0), colors.white),
            ('GRID',  (0,0),(-1,-1), 0.3, colors.lightgrey),
            ('ROWBACKGROUNDS', (0,1),(-1,-1),
             [colors.white, colors.HexColor('#f5f5f5')]),
            ('FONTSIZE',      (0,0),(-1,-1), 7),
            ('TOPPADDING',    (0,0),(-1,-1), 3),
            ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0,0),(-1,-1), 3),
        ]))
        story.append(st)

        rule = ('Signal 1 alone (drought fraction exceeds threshold).'
                if decision['signal_1_fraction'] else
                'Signal 2 AND Signal 3 (moderate fraction + spatial coherence).')
        story.append(Paragraph(
            f'<i>Decision rule applied: {rule}</i>', small_style))
        story.append(Spacer(1, 0.3*cm))

        # ── Glossary ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        story.append(HRFlowable(width='100%', thickness=0.5,
                                color=colors.lightgrey))
        story.append(Paragraph('Metric Definitions', h3_style))

        glossary = [
            ('<b>GSD (Ground Sampling Distance)</b>',
             'Real-world size of one image pixel. '
             'Area uses the reported orthomosaic pixel resolution when available. '
             'Used to convert pixel counts to m².'),
            ('<b>Agricultural Area</b>',
             'Total pixels of crop + cotton_ball + drought + dry_weed. '
             'Excludes background and bare soil. Drought fractions are computed '
             'over this area so soil-dominated fields do not falsely dilute the signal.'),
            ('<b>Drought Fraction (Signal 1)</b>',
             f'Proportion of agricultural area classified as drought. '
             f'Threshold: {decision["tau_fraction"]:.0%}. '
             'Exceeding this threshold alone is sufficient to raise a visual stress flag '
             'because it means significant crop area is visibly stressed.'),
            ('<b>Non-Harvest Drought Fraction (Signal 2)</b>',
             'Drought fraction after removing pixels inside harvest zones '
             '(regions where cotton_ball density is high). '
             'In cotton fields at harvest, senescent leaves mimic drought colour; '
             f'this metric discounts those zones. Threshold: {decision["tau_non_harvest"]:.0%}.'),
            ('<b>CC (Connected Component)</b>',
             'A group of spatially adjacent pixels all classified as drought. '
             'Pixels are connected if they share an edge or corner (8-connectivity). '
             'Many small CCs = scattered noise. One large CC = coherent stress patch.'),
            ('<b>Largest CC Share (Signal 3)</b>',
             f'Fraction of all drought pixels in the single largest connected drought region. '
             f'Threshold: {decision["tau_cc_share"]:.0%}. '
             'Above threshold = spatially coherent predicted stress. '
             'Below threshold = scattered pixels (likely misclassification noise). '
             'Signal 3 is used as a coherence gate alongside Signal 2 but NOT Signal 1.'),
            ('<b>Heuristic Stress Score</b>',
             'Composite index (0–100%) computed as: '
             '0.5 × (drought_frac / threshold) + '
             '0.3 × (non_harvest_frac / threshold) + '
             '0.2 × coherence_signal. '
             'Capped at 100%. This score is not a calibrated probability or confirmed drought diagnosis.'),
        ]
        for term, defn in glossary:
            story.append(Paragraph(f'{term}: {defn}', body_style))
            story.append(Spacer(1, 0.07*cm))

        doc.build(story)




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/inference/report_before_indexed_png.py
================================================================================

"""
Report generation for orthomosaic predictions.

Outputs per orthomosaic:
    mask.png       Full-resolution color-coded segmentation mask
    overlay.jpg    50% alpha overlay on original with class legend (max 8192px wide)
    report.json    Per-class areas, drought decision, all metrics
    report.pdf     Detailed report: legend, area table, decision signals,
                   metric definitions glossary
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import numpy as np

from src.preprocessing.gsd import gsd_mm_per_pixel, pixels_to_m2
from src.utils.constants import CLASS_NAMES, CLASS_TO_IDX, NUM_CLASSES, PALETTE

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Class descriptions for the report legend
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CLASS_DESCRIPTIONS = {
    'background':   'No-data area outside the flight coverage boundary.',
    'soil':         'Bare or partially bare agricultural soil with no active crop cover.',
    'crop':         'Active standing crop canopy (primarily maize at vegetative stage).',
    'cotton_ball':  'Open cotton bolls visible — indicates harvest-ready or harvest stage.',
    'green_weed':   'Non-crop green vegetation (weeds) within or around the field.',
    'dry_weed':     'Dry or senescent weed vegetation; tan/brown in appearance.',
    'drought':      'Crop canopy showing stress symptoms: brown/red discolouration, '
                    'wilting, or die-back indicative of water deficit.',
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Area computation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def compute_areas(
    class_map: np.ndarray,
    harvest_mask: np.ndarray,
    altitude_m: float,
) -> dict:
    """
    Compute per-class pixel counts and areas in m².

    Drought fraction is computed over the agricultural footprint only
    (crop + cotton_ball + drought + dry_weed) — not over soil or background.
    """
    gsd_mm  = gsd_mm_per_pixel(altitude_m)
    px_m2   = pixels_to_m2(1, altitude_m)

    areas: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for cls_idx, cls_name in enumerate(CLASS_NAMES):
        count    = int((class_map == cls_idx).sum())
        area_m2  = count * px_m2
        areas[cls_name] = {'pixels': count, 'area_m2': round(area_m2, 2)}

    total_px       = int((class_map < NUM_CLASSES).sum()) - int((class_map == 0).sum())
    valid_field_px = total_px

    agri_classes   = ['crop', 'cotton_ball', 'drought', 'dry_weed']
    agri_px        = sum(areas[c]['pixels'] for c in agri_classes)
    drought_px     = areas['drought']['pixels']

    drought_fraction = drought_px / max(agri_px, 1)

    non_harvest_drought = int(
        ((class_map == CLASS_TO_IDX.get('drought', 6)) & ~harvest_mask).sum()
    )
    non_harvest_agri = max(
        agri_px - int(((class_map == CLASS_TO_IDX.get('cotton_ball', 3))
                       & harvest_mask).sum()), 1,
    )
    non_harvest_drought_frac = non_harvest_drought / non_harvest_agri

    return {
        'gsd_mm_per_pixel':               round(gsd_mm, 3),
        'pixel_area_m2':                  round(px_m2, 6),
        'altitude_m':                     altitude_m,
        'valid_field_pixels':             valid_field_px,
        'valid_field_area_m2':            round(valid_field_px * px_m2, 1),
        'agricultural_pixels':            agri_px,
        'agricultural_area_m2':           round(agri_px * px_m2, 1),
        'drought_fraction':               round(drought_fraction, 4),
        'non_harvest_drought_fraction':   round(non_harvest_drought_frac, 4),
        'per_class':                      areas,
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Drought decision
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def drought_decision(
    area_stats: dict,
    harvest_mask: np.ndarray,
    class_map: np.ndarray,
    tau_fraction: float    = 0.10,
    tau_non_harvest: float = 0.08,
    tau_cc_share: float    = 0.20,
) -> dict:
    """
    Three-signal drought decision.

    Signal 1 — Drought fraction > tau_fraction (10%)
        Fraction of the agricultural area (crop+cotton+drought+dry_weed)
        classified as drought.

    Signal 2 — Non-harvest drought fraction > tau_non_harvest (8%)
        Same as Signal 1 but excludes harvest zones (high cotton_ball density)
        to avoid false positives from senescent cotton leaves around open bolls.

    Signal 3 — Largest Connected Component (CC) share > tau_cc_share (20%)
        Fraction of ALL drought pixels belonging to the single largest
        spatially connected drought region. High = coherent stress zone.
        Low = scattered noise / misclassification.

    Decision: DROUGHT if Signal 1 alone (high fraction) OR (Signal 2 AND Signal 3).
    """
    drought_idx    = CLASS_TO_IDX.get('drought', 6)
    drought_binary = (class_map == drought_idx).astype(np.uint8)
    n_drought_px   = int(drought_binary.sum())

    num_labels, labels, stats, _ = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(
        drought_binary, connectivity=8
    )
    if num_labels > 1:
        cc_sizes   = stats[1:, cv2.CC_STAT_AREA]
        largest_cc = int(cc_sizes.max()) if len(cc_sizes) > 0 else 0
        cc_share   = largest_cc / max(n_drought_px, 1)
    else:
        largest_cc, cc_share = 0, 0.0

    df   = area_stats['drought_fraction']
    nhdf = area_stats['non_harvest_drought_fraction']
    sig1 = df   > tau_fraction
    sig2 = nhdf > tau_non_harvest
    sig3 = cc_share > tau_cc_share

    # Updated rule: Signal 1 alone is sufficient (handles distributed row drought)
    is_drought = sig1 or (sig2 and sig3)
    confidence = round(min(1.0,
        (df   / max(tau_fraction,    1e-6)) * 0.5 +
        (nhdf / max(tau_non_harvest, 1e-6)) * 0.3 +
        float(sig3) * 0.2
    ), 3)

    return {
        'drought':                         is_drought,
        'confidence':                      confidence,
        'drought_fraction':                round(df, 4),
        'non_harvest_drought_fraction':    round(nhdf, 4),
        'largest_cc_share':                round(cc_share, 4),
        'signal_1_fraction':               sig1,
        'signal_2_non_harvest':            sig2,
        'signal_3_coherent':               sig3,
        'tau_fraction':                    tau_fraction,
        'tau_non_harvest':                 tau_non_harvest,
        'tau_cc_share':                    tau_cc_share,
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Visual outputs
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_color_mask(class_map: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Convert class-index map to (H, W, 3) uint8 RGB color mask."""
    rgb = np.zeros((*class_map.shape, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
    for cls_idx, color in enumerate(PALETTE):
        rgb[class_map == cls_idx] = color
    return rgb


def _draw_legend_panel(height: int, width: int = 220) -> np.ndarray:
    """Build a white legend panel with colour swatches for every class."""
    panel = np.full((height, width, 3), 255, dtype=np.uint8)
    font  = cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX
    y     = 25
    cv2.putText(panel, 'LEGEND', (10, y), font, 0.55, (30, 30, 30), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    y += 8
    cv2.line(panel, (10, y), (width - 10, y), (180, 180, 180), 1)
    y += 15
    for cls_idx, cls_name in enumerate(CLASS_NAMES):
        if cls_name == 'background':
            continue
        color_rgb = PALETTE[cls_idx]
        color_bgr = (int(color_rgb[2]), int(color_rgb[1]), int(color_rgb[0]))
        cv2.rectangle(panel, (10, y - 12), (28, y + 4), color_bgr, -1)
        cv2.rectangle(panel, (10, y - 12), (28, y + 4), (100, 100, 100), 1)
        cv2.putText(panel, cls_name, (34, y), font, 0.42,
                    (30, 30, 30), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
        y += 22
    return panel


def save_mask_png(class_map: np.ndarray, path: Path) -> None:
    """Save the full-resolution color mask as PNG."""
    from PIL import Image
    Image.MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS = None
    Image.fromarray(make_color_mask(class_map)).save(str(path))


def save_overlay_jpg(
    img_rgb: np.ndarray,
    class_map: np.ndarray,
    path: Path,
    max_width: int = 8192,
) -> None:
    """
    Save semi-transparent overlay with legend panel on the right.
    Resizes to max_width if the image is very large.
    """
    H, W = img_rgb.shape[:2]
    if W > max_width:
        scale  = max_width / W
        img_s  = cv2.resize(img_rgb,   (max_width, int(H * scale)),
                             interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
        mask_s = cv2.resize(class_map, (max_width, int(H * scale)),
                             interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)
    else:
        img_s, mask_s = img_rgb, class_map

    color_s = make_color_mask(mask_s)
    overlay = (0.5 * img_s.astype(np.float32) +
               0.5 * color_s.astype(np.float32)).astype(np.uint8)

    # Stat bar at bottom
    names  = CLASS_NAMES
    total  = max((mask_s > 0).sum(), 1)
    stats  = ' | '.join(
        f'{names[i]}={int((mask_s==i).sum())/total*100:.1f}%'
        for i in range(NUM_CLASSES)
        if (mask_s == i).sum() > 0 and names[i] != 'background'
    )
    bar_h  = 28
    bar    = np.full((bar_h, overlay.shape[1], 3), 240, dtype=np.uint8)
    cv2.putText(bar, stats, (8, 19), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
                0.42, (40, 40, 40), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    overlay = np.vstack([overlay, bar])

    # Legend panel on the right
    legend    = _draw_legend_panel(overlay.shape[0])
    composite = np.hstack([overlay, legend])

    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    cv2.imwrite(str(path), cv2.cvtColor(composite, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR),
                [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 88])


def save_json_report(
    area_stats: dict,
    decision: dict,
    img_path: str,
    out_path: Path,
) -> None:
    report = {
        'source_image':     img_path,
        'area_stats':       area_stats,
        'drought_decision': decision,
    }
    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(out_path, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# PDF report
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def save_pdf_report(
    img_rgb: np.ndarray,
    class_map: np.ndarray,
    area_stats: dict,
    decision: dict,
    img_name: str,
    out_path: Path,
) -> None:
    """
    Detailed PDF report:
        - Drought decision banner
        - Original thumbnail | colour mask | class legend with descriptions
        - Per-class area breakdown table
        - Decision signals table with metric values and thresholds
        - Full glossary: definitions of every metric used
    """
    try:
        from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
        from reportlab.lib.units import cm
        from reportlab.lib import colors
        from reportlab.platypus import (
            SimpleDocTemplate, Table, TableStyle,
            Paragraph, Spacer, Image as RLImage, HRFlowable,
        )
        from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
        import tempfile, os
        from PIL import Image as PILImage
    except ImportError:
        print('  [WARNING] reportlab not available — skipping PDF')
        return

    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    doc    = SimpleDocTemplate(str(out_path), pagesize=A4,
                               leftMargin=1.8*cm, rightMargin=1.8*cm,
                               topMargin=1.5*cm, bottomMargin=1.5*cm)
    styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
    title_style = ParagraphStyle('t',  parent=styles['Title'],    fontSize=14, spaceAfter=4)
    h2_style    = ParagraphStyle('h2', parent=styles['Heading2'], fontSize=10, spaceBefore=8, spaceAfter=3)
    h3_style    = ParagraphStyle('h3', parent=styles['Heading3'], fontSize=9,  spaceBefore=5, spaceAfter=2)
    body_style  = ParagraphStyle('b',  parent=styles['Normal'],   fontSize=8,  leading=11)
    small_style = ParagraphStyle('s',  parent=styles['Normal'],   fontSize=7,  leading=10,
                                 textColor=colors.HexColor('#333333'))

    is_drought = decision['drought']
    story      = []

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:

        # ── Banner ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        story.append(Paragraph(f'Drought Analysis Report — {img_name}', title_style))
        call_col  = '#CC0000' if is_drought else '#007700'
        call_text = '⚠  DROUGHT DETECTED' if is_drought else '✓  No Drought Detected'
        story.append(Paragraph(
            f'<b><font color="{call_col}" size="12">{call_text}</font></b>'
            f'&nbsp;&nbsp;<font size="8" color="grey">'
            f'Confidence: {decision["confidence"]:.0%}</font>',
            ParagraphStyle('call', parent=styles['Normal'],
                           fontSize=12, spaceAfter=6)))
        story.append(HRFlowable(width='100%', thickness=1,
                                color=colors.HexColor(call_col)))
        story.append(Spacer(1, 0.3*cm))

        # ── Images + Legend ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        story.append(Paragraph('Field Overview', h2_style))

        H, W   = img_rgb.shape[:2]
        scale  = min(1.0, 300 / max(H, W))
        th_w, th_h = int(W * scale), int(H * scale)
        thumb  = cv2.resize(img_rgb,            (th_w, th_h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
        c_mask = make_color_mask(class_map)
        c_res  = cv2.resize(c_mask,             (th_w, th_h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST)

        tp = os.path.join(tmpdir, 'thumb.jpg')
        mp = os.path.join(tmpdir, 'mask.png')
        cv2.imwrite(tp, cv2.cvtColor(thumb, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))
        PILImage.fromarray(c_res).save(mp)

        img_w = 6.0*cm
        img_h = img_w * th_h / th_w

        # Legend table
        leg_rows = [[Paragraph('<b>Class</b>', small_style),
                     Paragraph('<b>Description</b>', small_style)]]
        for cls_idx, cls_name in enumerate(CLASS_NAMES):
            if cls_name == 'background':
                continue
            rgb = PALETTE[cls_idx]
            sw_color = colors.Color(rgb[0]/255, rgb[1]/255, rgb[2]/255)
            swatch   = Table([['']], colWidths=[0.4*cm], rowHeights=[0.35*cm])
            swatch.setStyle(TableStyle([
                ('BACKGROUND', (0,0),(0,0), sw_color),
                ('GRID',       (0,0),(0,0), 0.5, colors.grey),
            ]))
            leg_rows.append([
                Table([[swatch, Paragraph(f' {cls_name}', small_style)]],
                      colWidths=[0.5*cm, 2.3*cm]),
                Paragraph(CLASS_DESCRIPTIONS.get(cls_name, ''), small_style),
            ])
        leg_tbl = Table(leg_rows, colWidths=[2.9*cm, 7.0*cm])
        leg_tbl.setStyle(TableStyle([
            ('BACKGROUND', (0,0),(-1,0), colors.HexColor('#444444')),
            ('TEXTCOLOR',  (0,0),(-1,0), colors.white),
            ('GRID',  (0,0),(-1,-1), 0.3, colors.lightgrey),
            ('FONTSIZE',      (0,0),(-1,-1), 7),
            ('TOPPADDING',    (0,0),(-1,-1), 2),
            ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0,0),(-1,-1), 2),
        ]))

        img_row = [[RLImage(tp, width=img_w, height=img_h),
                    RLImage(mp, width=img_w, height=img_h),
                    leg_tbl]]
        img_tbl = Table(img_row, colWidths=[img_w+0.3*cm, img_w+0.3*cm, 10.2*cm])
        img_tbl.setStyle(TableStyle([
            ('ALIGN',  (0,0),(-1,-1), 'CENTER'),
            ('VALIGN', (0,0),(-1,-1), 'TOP'),
        ]))
        story.append(img_tbl)
        story.append(Paragraph(
            '<i>Left: original orthomosaic &nbsp; '
            'Centre: segmentation mask &nbsp; '
            'Right: class legend with descriptions</i>', small_style))
        story.append(Spacer(1, 0.3*cm))

        # ── Area breakdown ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        story.append(Paragraph('Area Breakdown', h2_style))
        story.append(Paragraph(
            f'GSD: <b>{area_stats["gsd_mm_per_pixel"]} mm/px</b>  |  '
            f'Altitude: <b>{area_stats["altitude_m"]} m</b>  |  '
            f'Field area: <b>{area_stats["valid_field_area_m2"]:,.0f} m²</b>  |  '
            f'Agricultural area: <b>{area_stats["agricultural_area_m2"]:,.0f} m²</b>',
            body_style))
        story.append(Spacer(1, 0.1*cm))

        valid  = max(area_stats['valid_field_area_m2'], 1)
        agri   = max(area_stats['agricultural_area_m2'], 1)
        a_data = [[Paragraph('<b>Class</b>',      small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>Pixels</b>',     small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>Area (m²)</b>',  small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>% of field</b>', small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>% of agri</b>',  small_style)]]
        for cls_name in CLASS_NAMES:
            if cls_name == 'background':
                continue
            d  = area_stats['per_class'].get(cls_name, {})
            a  = d.get('area_m2', 0)
            px = d.get('pixels', 0)
            pf = a / valid * 100
            pa = f'{a/agri*100:.1f}%' if cls_name in \
                 ['crop','cotton_ball','drought','dry_weed'] else '—'
            a_data.append([
                Paragraph(cls_name, small_style),
                Paragraph(f'{px:,}',     small_style),
                Paragraph(f'{a:,.1f}',   small_style),
                Paragraph(f'{pf:.1f}%',  small_style),
                Paragraph(pa,            small_style),
            ])
        at = Table(a_data, colWidths=[2.8*cm, 3.0*cm, 3.0*cm, 2.5*cm, 2.5*cm])
        at.setStyle(TableStyle([
            ('BACKGROUND', (0,0),(-1,0), colors.HexColor('#444444')),
            ('TEXTCOLOR',  (0,0),(-1,0), colors.white),
            ('GRID',  (0,0),(-1,-1), 0.3, colors.lightgrey),
            ('ROWBACKGROUNDS', (0,1),(-1,-1),
             [colors.white, colors.HexColor('#f5f5f5')]),
            ('FONTSIZE',      (0,0),(-1,-1), 7),
            ('TOPPADDING',    (0,0),(-1,-1), 2),
            ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0,0),(-1,-1), 2),
        ]))
        story.append(at)
        story.append(Spacer(1, 0.3*cm))

        # ── Decision signals ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
        story.append(Paragraph('Drought Decision Signals', h2_style))
        s_data = [[Paragraph('<b>Signal</b>',    small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>Metric</b>',     small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>Value</b>',      small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>Threshold</b>',  small_style),
                   Paragraph('<b>Triggered</b>',  small_style)]]
        rows = [
            ('Signal 1', 'Drought fraction (all agri area)',
             f'{decision["drought_fraction"]:.1%}',
             f'> {decision["tau_fraction"]:.0%}',
             decision['signal_1_fraction']),
            ('Signal 2', 'Non-harvest drought fraction',
             f'{decision["non_harvest_drought_fraction"]:.1%}',
             f'> {decision["tau_non_harvest"]:.0%}',
             decision['signal_2_non_harvest']),
            ('Signal 3', 'Largest CC share (spatial coherence)',
             f'{decision["largest_cc_share"]:.1%}',
             f'> {decision["tau_cc_share"]:.0%}',
             decision['signal_3_coherent']),
        ]
        for r in rows:
            tick = '✓  Yes' if r[4] else '✗  No'
            col  = 'green'  if r[4] else 'red'
            s_data.append([
                Paragraph(r[0], small_style),
                Paragraph(r[1], small_style),
                Paragraph(f'<b>{r[2]}</b>', small_style),
                Paragraph(r[3], small_style),
                Paragraph(f'<font color="{col}"><b>{tick}</b></font>', small_style),
            ])
        st = Table(s_data, colWidths=[2.0*cm, 5.8*cm, 2.2*cm, 2.3*cm, 1.8*cm])
        st.setStyle(TableStyle([
            ('BACKGROUND', (0,0),(-1,0), colors.HexColor('#444444')),
            ('TEXTCOLOR',  (0,0),(-1,0), colors.white),
            ('GRID',  (0,0),(-1,-1), 0.3, colors.lightgrey),
            ('ROWBACKGROUNDS', (0,1),(-1,-1),
             [colors.white, colors.HexColor('#f5f5f5')]),
            ('FONTSIZE',      (0,0),(-1,-1), 7),
            ('TOPPADDING',    (0,0),(-1,-1), 3),
            ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0,0),(-1,-1), 3),
        ]))
        story.append(st)

        rule = ('Signal 1 alone (drought fraction exceeds threshold).'
                if decision['signal_1_fraction'] else
                'Signal 2 AND Signal 3 (moderate fraction + spatial coherence).')
        story.append(Paragraph(
            f'<i>Decision rule applied: {rule}</i>', small_style))
        story.append(Spacer(1, 0.3*cm))

        # ── Glossary ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        story.append(HRFlowable(width='100%', thickness=0.5,
                                color=colors.lightgrey))
        story.append(Paragraph('Metric Definitions', h3_style))

        glossary = [
            ('<b>GSD (Ground Sampling Distance)</b>',
             'Real-world size of one image pixel. '
             'Formula: GSD = (pixel_size_mm × altitude_m) / focal_length_mm. '
             'Used to convert pixel counts to m².'),
            ('<b>Agricultural Area</b>',
             'Total pixels of crop + cotton_ball + drought + dry_weed. '
             'Excludes background and bare soil. Drought fractions are computed '
             'over this area so soil-dominated fields do not falsely dilute the signal.'),
            ('<b>Drought Fraction (Signal 1)</b>',
             f'Proportion of agricultural area classified as drought. '
             f'Threshold: {decision["tau_fraction"]:.0%}. '
             'Exceeding this threshold alone is sufficient to declare drought '
             'because it means significant crop area is visibly stressed.'),
            ('<b>Non-Harvest Drought Fraction (Signal 2)</b>',
             'Drought fraction after removing pixels inside harvest zones '
             '(regions where cotton_ball density is high). '
             'In cotton fields at harvest, senescent leaves mimic drought colour; '
             f'this metric discounts those zones. Threshold: {decision["tau_non_harvest"]:.0%}.'),
            ('<b>CC (Connected Component)</b>',
             'A group of spatially adjacent pixels all classified as drought. '
             'Pixels are connected if they share an edge or corner (8-connectivity). '
             'Many small CCs = scattered noise. One large CC = coherent stress patch.'),
            ('<b>Largest CC Share (Signal 3)</b>',
             f'Fraction of all drought pixels in the single largest connected drought region. '
             f'Threshold: {decision["tau_cc_share"]:.0%}. '
             'Above threshold = spatially coherent drought (genuine stress). '
             'Below threshold = scattered pixels (likely misclassification noise). '
             'Signal 3 is used as a coherence gate alongside Signal 2 but NOT Signal 1.'),
            ('<b>Confidence Score</b>',
             'Composite index (0–100%) computed as: '
             '0.5 × (drought_frac / threshold) + '
             '0.3 × (non_harvest_frac / threshold) + '
             '0.2 × coherence_signal. '
             'Capped at 100%. High confidence + DROUGHT = clear, widespread, coherent stress.'),
        ]
        for term, defn in glossary:
            story.append(Paragraph(f'{term}: {defn}', body_style))
            story.append(Spacer(1, 0.07*cm))

        doc.build(story)




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/inference/tile.py
================================================================================

"""
Tile-and-stitch inference for large orthomosaic images.

Production-hardened for very large remote sensing imagery (25k+ pixels).

Fixes applied
-------------
1. All accumulators (prob_acc, weight_acc) live on CPU float16.
   Nothing is accumulated on the GPU — only one batch at a time sits there.

2. Dynamic safe batch size: computed from image pixel count to avoid OOM
   before the first forward pass. Can be overridden by the caller.

3. OOM retry loop: if a batch still OOMs (e.g. due to fragmentation), the
   batch is automatically re-tried with half the size, down to minimum 1.

4. Explicit GPU cleanup after every batch: del + empty_cache + gc.collect.
   Prevents VRAM fragmentation across the hundreds of batches in a large image.

5. No model-side changes required — fixes are purely in data movement.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import gc
import math

import cv2
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from tqdm import tqdm

from src.preprocessing.tensor_builder import build_9channel


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dynamic safe batch size
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _safe_batch_size(
    H: int,
    W: int,
    tile_size: int,
    requested: int,
    vram_budget_gb: float = 12.0,
) -> int:
    """
    Compute a safe batch size that is unlikely to OOM on the current GPU.

    Heuristic based on measured GPU usage for SegFormer-B2 at tile_size=1024:
        ~0.22 GB per tile in a batch (input + activations + logits)
    This is conservative — actual usage depends on batch size due to
    batched attention patterns in SegFormer.

    Args:
        H, W:          Image dimensions in pixels.
        tile_size:     Inference tile size.
        requested:     Caller-requested batch size.
        vram_budget_gb: How many GB to budget for one forward pass.
                        Default 12 GB leaves headroom for the model weights
                        (~0.5 GB) on a 70 GB H200.
    """
    pixels_per_tile = tile_size * tile_size
    # Measured ~0.22 GB per tile for SegFormer-B2 at 1024×1024
    # Measured SegFormer-B2 peak VRAM per tile in a batch:
    # ~0.75 GB at 1024×1024 — accounts for input, activations, attention maps,
    # gradients (even with no_grad, PyTorch keeps intermediate tensors).
    gb_per_tile = 9 * pixels_per_tile * 4 / 1e9 * 20.0  # 20× overhead factor

    safe = max(1, int(vram_budget_gb / gb_per_tile))
    result = min(requested, safe)

    total_tiles = math.ceil(H / tile_size) * math.ceil(W / tile_size)
    if result < requested:
        print(
            f'  [tile] Image {W}×{H} ({total_tiles} tiles): '
            f'reducing batch {requested}→{result} to stay within '
            f'{vram_budget_gb:.0f} GB VRAM budget.'
        )
    return result


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Hann window
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _hann2d(size: int) -> torch.Tensor:
    """2D Hann window as CPU float16. Clipped to min 0.01 so corners contribute."""
    h = torch.hann_window(size, periodic=False)
    h = h.clamp(min=0.1)
    return torch.outer(h, h).half()   # (size, size) float16


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TileStitcher
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TileStitcher:
    """
    Production-grade tiled inference over large orthomosaic images.

    All GPU memory is released after every batch.
    Automatically retries with smaller batch on CUDA OOM.
    All accumulator tensors live on CPU to avoid VRAM pressure.

    Args:
        model:         Trained SegFormerB2 on `device`, already in eval mode.
        device:        torch.device
        tile_size:     Tile size in pixels (default 1024).
        stride:        Stride between tile origins (default 768 = 256px overlap).
        batch_size:    Requested tiles per GPU batch. Automatically reduced
                       based on image size and VRAM budget.
        num_classes:   Number of segmentation classes.
        vram_budget_gb: VRAM budget for one forward pass (default 12 GB).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: torch.nn.Module,
        device: torch.device,
        tile_size: int = 1024,
        stride: int = 768,
        batch_size: int = 8,
        num_classes: int = 7,
        vram_budget_gb: float = 12.0,
    ) -> None:
        self.model          = model
        self.device         = device
        self.tile_size      = tile_size
        self.stride         = stride
        self.requested_bs   = batch_size
        self.num_classes    = num_classes
        self.vram_budget_gb = vram_budget_gb
        # Keep Hann window on CPU — moved to GPU per-batch only
        self.hann_cpu       = _hann2d(tile_size)   # (T, T) float16 on CPU
        model.eval()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Internal: forward one batch with OOM retry
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _forward_batch(
        self,
        tiles_np: list[np.ndarray],
        current_bs: int,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, int]:
        """
        Run one forward pass. On CUDA OOM, halve the batch and retry.

        Returns:
            (probs_cpu, effective_bs_used)
            probs_cpu: (B, C, T, T) float16 on CPU
        """
        while True:
            try:
                batch = torch.stack(
                    [torch.from_numpy(t) for t in tiles_np]
                ).to(self.device)                          # (B, 9, T, T) float32

                with torch.no_grad():
                    logits    = self.model(batch)          # (B, C, T, T)
                    probs_cpu = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().half()

                # Explicit GPU cleanup — critical for sustained batch runs
                del batch, logits
                if self.device.type == 'cuda':
                    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
                gc.collect()

                return probs_cpu, current_bs

            except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError:
                # Clean up whatever partial allocations were made
                try:
                    del batch
                except NameError:
                    pass
                if self.device.type == 'cuda':
                    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
                gc.collect()

                new_bs = max(1, current_bs // 2)
                if new_bs == current_bs:
                    raise   # already at 1, can't go lower — re-raise
                print(
                    f'\n  [OOM] CUDA OOM at batch_size={current_bs}. '
                    f'Retrying with batch_size={new_bs} ...'
                )
                # Re-split tiles_np to the smaller batch and retry
                # (caller must handle the rest of the original batch)
                tiles_np  = tiles_np[:new_bs]
                current_bs = new_bs

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public: full image inference
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def run(self, img_rgb: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Full tiled inference on one large image.

        Args:
            img_rgb: (H, W, 3) uint8 RGB.

        Returns:
            (H, W) uint8 argmax class map.
        """
        H, W  = img_rgb.shape[:2]
        T     = self.tile_size
        S     = self.stride
        C     = self.num_classes

        # Compute safe batch size based on image dimensions
        bs = _safe_batch_size(H, W, T, self.requested_bs, self.vram_budget_gb)

        # Pad image so tiles cover the edges cleanly
        pad_h = (math.ceil((H - T) / S) * S + T) - H if H > T else T - H
        pad_w = (math.ceil((W - T) / S) * S + T) - W if W > T else T - W
        if pad_h > 0 or pad_w > 0:
            img_rgb = cv2.copyMakeBorder(
                img_rgb, 0, pad_h, 0, pad_w,
                cv2.BORDER_REFLECT_101,
            )
        Hp, Wp = img_rgb.shape[:2]

        # Tile coordinate grid
        ys     = list(range(0, Hp - T + 1, S))
        xs     = list(range(0, Wp - T + 1, S))
        coords = [(y, x) for y in ys for x in xs]
        print(f'  Grid: {len(xs)}×{len(ys)} = {len(coords)} tiles'
              f'  |  batch_size={bs}'
              f'  |  ~{math.ceil(len(coords)/bs)} forward passes')

        # CPU accumulators — never touch the GPU
        prob_acc   = torch.zeros(C, Hp, Wp, dtype=torch.float16)
        weight_acc = torch.zeros(1, Hp, Wp, dtype=torch.float16)

        # Process all tiles in batches
        # OOM inside _forward_batch will halve bs and retry the SAME sub-batch.
        # We therefore use a while-loop with a pointer instead of a for-loop.
        ptr = 0
        with tqdm(total=len(coords), desc='  Tiles', unit='tile',
                  leave=False, ncols=80) as pbar:
            while ptr < len(coords):
                batch_coords = coords[ptr: ptr + bs]
                tiles_np     = [
                    build_9channel(img_rgb[y:y+T, x:x+T])
                    for y, x in batch_coords
                ]

                probs_cpu, bs_used = self._forward_batch(tiles_np, bs)
                # _forward_batch may have reduced bs_used < len(batch_coords)
                # if OOM occurred. In that case only bs_used tiles were processed.
                if bs_used < len(batch_coords):
                    # Only advance by bs_used; remaining tiles re-processed next iter
                    batch_coords = batch_coords[:bs_used]
                    bs = bs_used   # use smaller size for remaining batches

                # Splat probabilities into accumulator
                for i, (y, x) in enumerate(batch_coords):
                    p = probs_cpu[i]                       # (C, T, T) float16
                    w = self.hann_cpu                      # (T, T) float16
                    prob_acc[:, y:y+T, x:x+T]   += p * w.unsqueeze(0)
                    weight_acc[0, y:y+T, x:x+T] += w

                ptr += len(batch_coords)
                pbar.update(len(batch_coords))

        # Normalise probability map
        prob_acc = prob_acc / weight_acc.clamp(min=1e-4)   # (C, Hp, Wp)
        cs       = prob_acc.sum(dim=0, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-4)
        prob_acc = prob_acc / cs

        # Argmax + crop back to original size
        class_map = prob_acc.argmax(dim=0).numpy().astype(np.uint8)
        return class_map[:H, :W]




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/inference/tile_v11.py
================================================================================

"""
Tile-and-stitch inference for large orthomosaic images.

Production-hardened for very large remote sensing imagery (25k+ pixels).

Fixes applied
-------------
1. All accumulators (prob_acc, weight_acc) live on CPU float16.
   Nothing is accumulated on the GPU — only one batch at a time sits there.

2. Dynamic safe batch size: computed from image pixel count to avoid OOM
   before the first forward pass. Can be overridden by the caller.

3. OOM retry loop: if a batch still OOMs (e.g. due to fragmentation), the
   batch is automatically re-tried with half the size, down to minimum 1.

4. Explicit GPU cleanup after every batch: del + empty_cache + gc.collect.
   Prevents VRAM fragmentation across the hundreds of batches in a large image.

5. No model-side changes required — fixes are purely in data movement.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import gc
import math

import cv2
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from tqdm import tqdm

from src.preprocessing.tensor_builder import build_9channel


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dynamic safe batch size
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _safe_batch_size(
    H: int,
    W: int,
    tile_size: int,
    requested: int,
    vram_budget_gb: float = 12.0,
) -> int:
    """
    Compute a safe batch size that is unlikely to OOM on the current GPU.

    Heuristic based on measured GPU usage for SegFormer-B2 at tile_size=1024:
        ~0.22 GB per tile in a batch (input + activations + logits)
    This is conservative — actual usage depends on batch size due to
    batched attention patterns in SegFormer.

    Args:
        H, W:          Image dimensions in pixels.
        tile_size:     Inference tile size.
        requested:     Caller-requested batch size.
        vram_budget_gb: How many GB to budget for one forward pass.
                        Default 12 GB leaves headroom for the model weights
                        (~0.5 GB) on a 70 GB H200.
    """
    pixels_per_tile = tile_size * tile_size
    # Measured ~0.22 GB per tile for SegFormer-B2 at 1024×1024
    # Measured SegFormer-B2 peak VRAM per tile in a batch:
    # ~0.75 GB at 1024×1024 — accounts for input, activations, attention maps,
    # gradients (even with no_grad, PyTorch keeps intermediate tensors).
    gb_per_tile = 9 * pixels_per_tile * 4 / 1e9 * 20.0  # 20× overhead factor

    safe = max(1, int(vram_budget_gb / gb_per_tile))
    result = min(requested, safe)

    total_tiles = math.ceil(H / tile_size) * math.ceil(W / tile_size)
    if result < requested:
        print(
            f'  [tile] Image {W}×{H} ({total_tiles} tiles): '
            f'reducing batch {requested}→{result} to stay within '
            f'{vram_budget_gb:.0f} GB VRAM budget.'
        )
    return result


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Hann window
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _hann2d(size: int) -> torch.Tensor:
    """2D Hann window as CPU float16. Clipped to min 0.01 so corners contribute."""
    h = torch.hann_window(size, periodic=False)
    h = h.clamp(min=0.1)
    return torch.outer(h, h).half()   # (size, size) float16


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TileStitcher
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TileStitcher:
    """
    Production-grade tiled inference over large orthomosaic images.

    All GPU memory is released after every batch.
    Automatically retries with smaller batch on CUDA OOM.
    All accumulator tensors live on CPU to avoid VRAM pressure.

    Args:
        model:         Trained SegFormerB2 on `device`, already in eval mode.
        device:        torch.device
        tile_size:     Tile size in pixels (default 1024).
        stride:        Stride between tile origins (default 768 = 256px overlap).
        batch_size:    Requested tiles per GPU batch. Automatically reduced
                       based on image size and VRAM budget.
        num_classes:   Number of segmentation classes.
        vram_budget_gb: VRAM budget for one forward pass (default 12 GB).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: torch.nn.Module,
        device: torch.device,
        tile_size: int = 1024,
        stride: int = 768,
        batch_size: int = 8,
        num_classes: int = 7,
        vram_budget_gb: float = 12.0,
    ) -> None:
        self.model          = model
        self.device         = device
        self.tile_size      = tile_size
        self.stride         = stride
        self.requested_bs   = batch_size
        self.num_classes    = num_classes
        self.vram_budget_gb = vram_budget_gb
        # Keep Hann window on CPU — moved to GPU per-batch only
        self.hann_cpu       = _hann2d(tile_size)   # (T, T) float16 on CPU
        model.eval()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Internal: forward one batch with OOM retry
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _forward_batch(
        self,
        tiles_np: list[np.ndarray],
        current_bs: int,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, int]:
        """
        Run one forward pass. On CUDA OOM, halve the batch and retry.

        Returns:
            (probs_cpu, effective_bs_used)
            probs_cpu: (B, C, T, T) float16 on CPU
        """
        while True:
            try:
                batch = torch.stack(
                    [torch.from_numpy(t) for t in tiles_np]
                ).to(self.device)                          # (B, 9, T, T) float32

                with torch.no_grad():
                    logits    = self.model(batch)          # (B, C, T, T)
                    logits[:, 6] -= 1.20  # V11 drought calibration
                    logits[:, 5] -= 0.40  # V11 dry-weed calibration
                    probs_cpu = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().float()

                # Explicit GPU cleanup — critical for sustained batch runs
                del batch, logits
                if self.device.type == 'cuda':
                    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
                gc.collect()

                return probs_cpu, current_bs

            except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError:
                # Clean up whatever partial allocations were made
