    cv2.putText(bar, 'Ground truth', (W + 10, 22), font, 0.55, (20,20,20), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    cv2.putText(bar, 'Prediction', (2*W + 10, 22), font, 0.55, (20,20,20), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    composite = np.vstack([bar, composite])

    save_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    cv2.imwrite(str(save_path), cv2.cvtColor(composite, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR),
                [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 88])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Train
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def train(config: dict, resume_path=None) -> None:
    import os
    device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
    print(f'Device: {device}')
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        print(f'GPU:    {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}')
        print(f'VRAM:   {torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory/1e9:.1f} GB')
    slurm_keys = ['SLURM_JOB_ID','SLURM_JOB_NAME','SLURM_NODELIST','SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK']
    slurm_info = {k: os.environ[k] for k in slurm_keys if k in os.environ}
    if slurm_info:
        print('SLURM:', slurm_info)

    paths   = config['paths']
    cfg_seg = config['segmentation']

    out_dir = Path(paths['runs_dir']) / 'segmentation'
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    writer = SummaryWriter(log_dir=out_dir / cfg_seg.get('run_name', 'default') / 'tb_logs')

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Datasets
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    crop_size = cfg_seg['input_size']

    clean_images_dir = Path(paths.get('clean_images_dir',
                                      'data/clean_images'))
    clean_masks_dir  = Path(paths.get('clean_masks_dir',
                                      'data/clean_masks'))

    # Stratified train/val split: ensure drought-rich tiles go to val
    all_clean_masks = sorted(
        m for m in clean_masks_dir.glob('*.png')
        if any(
            (clean_images_dir / (m.stem + ext)).is_file()
            for ext in ('.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.tif', '.tiff',
                        '.PNG', '.JPG', '.JPEG', '.TIF', '.TIFF')
        )
    )
    if len(all_clean_masks) < 10:
        print(f'[WARNING] Only {len(all_clean_masks)} clean masks found. '
              'Validation will be unreliable.')
    val_n = max(3, int(len(all_clean_masks) * 0.2))

    # Compute drought fraction per tile for stratification
    drought_fracs = {}
    for m in all_clean_masks:
        mask_arr = cv2.imread(str(m), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
        valid    = mask_arr != 255
        n_valid  = max(valid.sum(), 1)
        drought_fracs[m.stem] = float((mask_arr == 6).sum()) / n_valid

    # Sort by drought fraction descending, interleave into val/train
    sorted_stems = sorted(drought_fracs, key=drought_fracs.get, reverse=True)
    # Take every (1/val_ratio)-th tile from the top-drought end for val
    val_stems = set()
    step = max(1, len(sorted_stems) // val_n)
    for i in range(0, len(sorted_stems), step):
        if len(val_stems) >= val_n:
            break
        val_stems.add(sorted_stems[i])
    # Fallback: fill val_n if not reached
    for s in sorted_stems:
        if len(val_stems) >= val_n: break
        val_stems.add(s)

    print(f'Clean masks total: {len(all_clean_masks)}, val: {len(val_stems)}')
    print(f'Val tile drought fracs: {[round(drought_fracs[s],3) for s in val_stems]}')

    # Build sub-datasets
    # CleanMaskDataset filters by directory listing — for the train/val split
    # we create two filtered subfolder views using symlinks-or-list
    # Simpler: instantiate one ClanMaskDataset and split via Subset
    full_clean = CleanMaskDataset(
        images_dir=clean_images_dir,
        masks_dir=clean_masks_dir,
        crop_size=crop_size,
        train=True,
    )
    train_idx, val_idx = [], []
    for i, (img, mask) in enumerate(full_clean.pairs):
        if mask.stem in val_stems:
            val_idx.append(i)
        else:
            train_idx.append(i)
    print(f'  Clean train: {len(train_idx)}, val: {len(val_idx)}')

    from torch.utils.data import Subset
    clean_train = Subset(full_clean, train_idx) if train_idx else None

    # Val-mode clean (no augmentation, centre crop)
    full_clean_val = CleanMaskDataset(
        images_dir=clean_images_dir,
        masks_dir=clean_masks_dir,
        crop_size=crop_size,
        train=False,
    )
    val_subset = Subset(full_clean_val, val_idx) if val_idx else None

    pseudo_train = PseudoLabelDataset(
        images_dir=paths['unlabeled_dir'],
        masks_dir=Path(paths.get('pseudo_masks_dir', '/content/v5_data/pseudo_masks')),
        crop_size=crop_size,
        train=True,
    )

    composite_train = CompositeDataset(
        patches_dir=paths['patches_dir'],
        backgrounds_dir=paths['unlabeled_dir'],
        crop_size=crop_size,
        virtual_length=cfg_seg.get('composite_per_epoch', 1000),
        train=True,
        seed=cfg_seg.get('seed', 42),
    )

    mixed = MixedSegmentationDataset(
        clean=clean_train,
        pseudo=pseudo_train,
        composite=composite_train,
        ratio_clean    = cfg_seg.get('batch_manual',    4),
        ratio_pseudo   = cfg_seg.get('batch_pseudo',    4),
        ratio_composite= cfg_seg.get('batch_composites', 8),
    )
    print(f'  Mixed train length: {len(mixed)}')
    print(f'  Composite per epoch: {cfg_seg.get("composite_per_epoch", 1000)}')

    dl_train = DataLoader(
        mixed,
        batch_size=cfg_seg['batch_size'],
        shuffle=True,
        num_workers=cfg_seg.get('num_workers', 4),
        pin_memory=True,
        drop_last=True,
        collate_fn=collate_fn,
    )
    dl_val = (
        DataLoader(val_subset,
                   batch_size=cfg_seg['batch_size'],
                   shuffle=False, num_workers=2,
                   pin_memory=True, collate_fn=collate_fn)
        if val_subset is not None else None
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Class pixel counts (from training clean masks, for weighting)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    print('\nComputing class pixel counts from clean training masks...')
    class_counts = {i: 0 for i in range(NUM_CLASSES)}
    for i in train_idx:
        _, mask_path = full_clean.pairs[i]
        m = cv2.imread(str(mask_path), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
        for c in range(NUM_CLASSES):
            class_counts[c] += int((m == c).sum())
    total = max(sum(class_counts.values()), 1)
    print('  Pixel distribution (training clean):')
    for c in range(NUM_CLASSES):
        pct = class_counts[c] / total * 100
        print(f'    {CLASS_NAMES[c]:14} {pct:5.2f}%')

    drought_idx    = CLASS_TO_IDX.get('drought', 6)
    cotton_idx     = CLASS_TO_IDX.get('cotton_ball', 3)

    # Drought weight override: force high weight so model must learn drought.
    drought_override = cfg_seg.get('drought_override_weight', 30.0)

    # Cotton_ball weight override: auto-computed weight (~32) was too high with
    # 151 clean tiles, causing cotton_ball false positives on bright soil pixels
    # (Balaji v4 showed 15.3% cotton vs 1.1% in v3). Lowering to 12 reduces
    # sensitivity while still giving adequate supervision for the rare class.
    cotton_override = cfg_seg.get('cotton_override_weight', None)

    overrides = {drought_idx: drought_override}
    if cotton_override is not None:
        overrides[cotton_idx] = float(cotton_override)

    class_weights = make_class_weights(
        class_counts, NUM_CLASSES,
        overrides=overrides,
    )
    print('  Computed class weights (drought + cotton overridden):')
    for ci, w in enumerate(class_weights.tolist()):
        print(f'    {CLASS_NAMES[ci]:14} {w:6.2f}')

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Model
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    print('\nBuilding SegFormer-B2 ...')
    model = SegFormerB2(
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
        input_size=crop_size,
        pretrained_id=cfg_seg.get('pretrained_id', 'nvidia/mit-b2'),
        pretrained_local_path=cfg_seg.get('pretrained_local_path'),
        in_channels=9,
    ).to(device)
    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()) / 1e6
    print(f'  parameters: {n_params:.1f}M')

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Loss + optimizer
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    criterion = SegmentationLoss(
        class_weights=class_weights.to(device),
        w_ce=cfg_seg.get('ce_weight', 1.0),
        w_dice=cfg_seg.get('dice_weight', 2.5),
        w_lovasz=cfg_seg.get('boundary_weight', 1.0),
        ignore_index=IGNORE_INDEX,
        focal_gamma=cfg_seg.get('focal_gamma', 2.0),
    ).to(device)

    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        model.parameters(),
        lr=cfg_seg['lr'],
        weight_decay=cfg_seg['weight_decay'],
    )
    scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(
        optimizer, T_max=cfg_seg['epochs'], eta_min=1e-6
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Training loop
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    best_miou   = 0.0
    run_name    = cfg_seg.get('run_name', 'best_v2')
    best_path   = out_dir / f'{run_name}.pt'
    history     = []
    start_epoch = 1

    # Resume from checkpoint if requested
    if resume_path is not None and Path(resume_path).exists():
        print(f'\nResuming from {resume_path} ...')
        ckpt = torch.load(resume_path, map_location=device, weights_only=False)
        model.load_state_dict(ckpt['model_state_dict'])
        best_miou   = 0.0
        start_epoch = 1
        history     = []
        print('  V4 weights loaded; starting V5 from epoch 1.')
        print(f'  Resumed at epoch {start_epoch}, best mIoU so far: {best_miou:.4f}')
    elif resume_path is not None:
        print(f'[WARNING] --resume path not found: {resume_path}. Starting fresh.')


    if resume_path is None or not Path(resume_path).exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError("Provide an existing checkpoint with --resume")

    model.eval()
    t0 = time.time()
    epoch = 0
    # ---- Validation ----
    val_miou = float('nan')
    val_iou  = np.full(NUM_CLASSES, np.nan)
    if dl_val is not None and len(dl_val) > 0:
        model.eval()
        confusion = np.zeros((NUM_CLASSES, NUM_CLASSES), dtype=np.int64)
        with torch.no_grad():
            for batch in tqdm(dl_val, desc=f'Epoch {epoch:3d} [val]  ', leave=False):
                images = batch['image'].to(device, non_blocking=True)
                masks  = batch['mask'].to(device, non_blocking=True)
                logits = model(images)
                preds  = logits.argmax(dim=1)
                update_confusion(confusion, preds, masks, NUM_CLASSES)

        # Save confusion matrix and summarize errors.
        import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

        out_eval = Path("/content/drive/MyDrive/implementation/runs/segmentation")
        out_eval.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        np.save(out_eval / "confusion_v11.npy", confusion)

        normalized = confusion.astype(float) / np.maximum(
            confusion.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True), 1
        )

        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 8))
        im = ax.imshow(normalized, cmap="Blues", vmin=0, vmax=1)
        ax.set_xticks(range(NUM_CLASSES), CLASS_NAMES, rotation=45, ha="right")
        ax.set_yticks(range(NUM_CLASSES), CLASS_NAMES)
        ax.set_xlabel("Predicted class")
        ax.set_ylabel("Ground truth class")
        ax.set_title("V11 Validation Confusion Matrix (row-normalized)")
        fig.colorbar(im, ax=ax)
        fig.tight_layout()
        fig.savefig(out_eval / "confusion_v11.png", dpi=160)
        plt.close(fig)

        print("\\nTop class confusions:")
        errors = []
        for true_idx in range(NUM_CLASSES):
            for pred_idx in range(NUM_CLASSES):
                if true_idx != pred_idx:
                    errors.append((
                        normalized[true_idx, pred_idx],
                        true_idx, pred_idx
                    ))
        for fraction, true_idx, pred_idx in sorted(
            errors, reverse=True
        )[:12]:
            print(
                f"  {CLASS_NAMES[true_idx]} -> "
                f"{CLASS_NAMES[pred_idx]}: {fraction:.1%}"
            )

        val_iou, val_miou = compute_iou_per_class(confusion)


    print(f"V11 evaluation mIoU: {val_miou:.4%}")
    for i, value in enumerate(val_iou):
        print(f"{CLASS_NAMES[i]:14}: {value:.4%}")
    return


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--config', type=Path, default=Path('configs/config.yaml'))
    ap.add_argument('--resume', type=Path, default=None,
                    help='Resume from a previous checkpoint .pt file')
    args = ap.parse_args()

    import yaml
    with open(args.config) as f:
        config = yaml.safe_load(f)

    train(config, resume_path=args.resume)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/segmentation/eval_dryweed_v11.py
================================================================================

"""
SegFormer-B2 training loop.

Usage:
    python -m src.segmentation.train --config configs/config.yaml

What it does:
    1. Builds mixed dataset (clean + pseudo + synthetic).
    2. Loads SegFormer-B2 with 9-channel input adapter.
    3. Computes per-class loss weights from training pixel counts.
    4. Trains with CE + Dice + Lovász, with per-sample loss weights.
    5. Validates each epoch on held-out clean tiles.
    6. Saves best mIoU checkpoint, writes training_report.json.
    7. Dumps val_predictions/ with PNG overlays of best-epoch predictions.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
from torch.utils.tensorboard import SummaryWriter
from tqdm import tqdm

from src.preprocessing.tensor_builder import build_9channel
from src.segmentation.dataset import (
    CleanMaskDataset,
    CompositeDataset,
    MixedSegmentationDataset,
    PseudoLabelDataset,
    _augment_image_and_mask,
)
from src.segmentation.losses import SegmentationLoss, make_class_weights
from src.segmentation.model import SegFormerB2
from src.utils.constants import (
    CLASS_NAMES,
    CLASS_TO_IDX,
    IGNORE_INDEX,
    NUM_CLASSES,
    PALETTE,
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def collate_fn(batch: list[dict]) -> dict:
    """Stack the dicts from PatchDataset → batched dict."""
    images       = torch.stack([b['image'] for b in batch])
    masks        = torch.stack([b['mask']  for b in batch])
    weights      = torch.tensor([b['loss_weight'] for b in batch],
                                dtype=torch.float32)
    sources      = [b['source'] for b in batch]
    return {
        'image':         images,
        'mask':          masks,
        'sample_weight': weights,
        'source':        sources,
    }


def compute_iou_per_class(
    confusion: np.ndarray,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, float]:
    """
    Per-class IoU from a (num_classes, num_classes) confusion matrix
    where confusion[true, pred] = pixel count.
    """
    iou_per = np.zeros(NUM_CLASSES, dtype=np.float64)
    for c in range(NUM_CLASSES):
        tp = confusion[c, c]
        fp = confusion[:, c].sum() - tp
        fn = confusion[c, :].sum() - tp
        denom = tp + fp + fn
        iou_per[c] = tp / denom if denom > 0 else np.nan
    miou = np.nanmean(iou_per)
    return iou_per, miou


def update_confusion(
    confusion: np.ndarray,
    preds: torch.Tensor,
    targets: torch.Tensor,
    num_classes: int,
) -> None:
    """Add to confusion matrix in-place. preds/targets are torch (B, H, W)."""
    valid = (targets != IGNORE_INDEX)
    p = preds[valid].cpu().numpy().astype(np.int32)
    t = targets[valid].cpu().numpy().astype(np.int32)
    # bincount approach is fast
    idx = t * num_classes + p
    counts = np.bincount(idx, minlength=num_classes * num_classes)
    confusion += counts.reshape(num_classes, num_classes)


def save_prediction_overlay(
    img_rgb: np.ndarray,
    true_mask: np.ndarray,
    pred_mask: np.ndarray,
    save_path: Path,
) -> None:
    """Save a 3-panel comparison: original | true mask | predicted mask."""
    H, W = img_rgb.shape[:2]

    def colorize(m):
        out = np.zeros((*m.shape, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        for i, c in enumerate(PALETTE):
            out[m == i] = c
        out[m == IGNORE_INDEX] = (90, 90, 90)
        return out

    true_color = colorize(true_mask)
    pred_color = colorize(pred_mask)

    # Side-by-side
    composite = np.hstack([img_rgb, true_color, pred_color])

    # Label bar at top
    bar = np.full((30, composite.shape[1], 3), 230, dtype=np.uint8)
    font = cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX
    cv2.putText(bar, 'Original', (10, 22), font, 0.55, (20,20,20), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    cv2.putText(bar, 'Ground truth', (W + 10, 22), font, 0.55, (20,20,20), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    cv2.putText(bar, 'Prediction', (2*W + 10, 22), font, 0.55, (20,20,20), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    composite = np.vstack([bar, composite])

    save_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    cv2.imwrite(str(save_path), cv2.cvtColor(composite, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR),
                [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 88])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Train
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def train(config: dict, resume_path=None) -> None:
    import os
    device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
    print(f'Device: {device}')
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        print(f'GPU:    {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}')
        print(f'VRAM:   {torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory/1e9:.1f} GB')
    slurm_keys = ['SLURM_JOB_ID','SLURM_JOB_NAME','SLURM_NODELIST','SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK']
    slurm_info = {k: os.environ[k] for k in slurm_keys if k in os.environ}
    if slurm_info:
        print('SLURM:', slurm_info)

    paths   = config['paths']
    cfg_seg = config['segmentation']

    out_dir = Path(paths['runs_dir']) / 'segmentation'
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    writer = SummaryWriter(log_dir=out_dir / cfg_seg.get('run_name', 'default') / 'tb_logs')

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Datasets
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    crop_size = cfg_seg['input_size']

    clean_images_dir = Path(paths.get('clean_images_dir',
                                      'data/clean_images'))
    clean_masks_dir  = Path(paths.get('clean_masks_dir',
                                      'data/clean_masks'))

    # Stratified train/val split: ensure drought-rich tiles go to val
    all_clean_masks = sorted(
        m for m in clean_masks_dir.glob('*.png')
        if any(
            (clean_images_dir / (m.stem + ext)).is_file()
            for ext in ('.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.tif', '.tiff',
                        '.PNG', '.JPG', '.JPEG', '.TIF', '.TIFF')
        )
    )
    if len(all_clean_masks) < 10:
        print(f'[WARNING] Only {len(all_clean_masks)} clean masks found. '
              'Validation will be unreliable.')
    val_n = max(3, int(len(all_clean_masks) * 0.2))

    # Compute drought fraction per tile for stratification
    drought_fracs = {}
    for m in all_clean_masks:
        mask_arr = cv2.imread(str(m), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
        valid    = mask_arr != 255
        n_valid  = max(valid.sum(), 1)
        drought_fracs[m.stem] = float((mask_arr == 6).sum()) / n_valid

    # Sort by drought fraction descending, interleave into val/train
    sorted_stems = sorted(drought_fracs, key=drought_fracs.get, reverse=True)
    # Take every (1/val_ratio)-th tile from the top-drought end for val
    val_stems = set()
    step = max(1, len(sorted_stems) // val_n)
    for i in range(0, len(sorted_stems), step):
        if len(val_stems) >= val_n:
            break
        val_stems.add(sorted_stems[i])
    # Fallback: fill val_n if not reached
    for s in sorted_stems:
        if len(val_stems) >= val_n: break
        val_stems.add(s)

    print(f'Clean masks total: {len(all_clean_masks)}, val: {len(val_stems)}')
    print(f'Val tile drought fracs: {[round(drought_fracs[s],3) for s in val_stems]}')

    # Build sub-datasets
    # CleanMaskDataset filters by directory listing — for the train/val split
    # we create two filtered subfolder views using symlinks-or-list
    # Simpler: instantiate one ClanMaskDataset and split via Subset
    full_clean = CleanMaskDataset(
        images_dir=clean_images_dir,
        masks_dir=clean_masks_dir,
        crop_size=crop_size,
        train=True,
    )
    train_idx, val_idx = [], []
    for i, (img, mask) in enumerate(full_clean.pairs):
        if mask.stem in val_stems:
            val_idx.append(i)
        else:
            train_idx.append(i)
    print(f'  Clean train: {len(train_idx)}, val: {len(val_idx)}')

    from torch.utils.data import Subset
    clean_train = Subset(full_clean, train_idx) if train_idx else None

    # Val-mode clean (no augmentation, centre crop)
    full_clean_val = CleanMaskDataset(
        images_dir=clean_images_dir,
        masks_dir=clean_masks_dir,
        crop_size=crop_size,
        train=False,
    )
    val_subset = Subset(full_clean_val, val_idx) if val_idx else None

    pseudo_train = PseudoLabelDataset(
        images_dir=paths['unlabeled_dir'],
        masks_dir=Path(paths.get('pseudo_masks_dir', '/content/v5_data/pseudo_masks')),
        crop_size=crop_size,
        train=True,
    )

    composite_train = CompositeDataset(
        patches_dir=paths['patches_dir'],
        backgrounds_dir=paths['unlabeled_dir'],
        crop_size=crop_size,
        virtual_length=cfg_seg.get('composite_per_epoch', 1000),
        train=True,
        seed=cfg_seg.get('seed', 42),
    )

    mixed = MixedSegmentationDataset(
        clean=clean_train,
        pseudo=pseudo_train,
        composite=composite_train,
        ratio_clean    = cfg_seg.get('batch_manual',    4),
        ratio_pseudo   = cfg_seg.get('batch_pseudo',    4),
        ratio_composite= cfg_seg.get('batch_composites', 8),
    )
    print(f'  Mixed train length: {len(mixed)}')
    print(f'  Composite per epoch: {cfg_seg.get("composite_per_epoch", 1000)}')

    dl_train = DataLoader(
        mixed,
        batch_size=cfg_seg['batch_size'],
        shuffle=True,
        num_workers=cfg_seg.get('num_workers', 4),
        pin_memory=True,
        drop_last=True,
        collate_fn=collate_fn,
    )
    dl_val = (
        DataLoader(val_subset,
                   batch_size=cfg_seg['batch_size'],
                   shuffle=False, num_workers=2,
                   pin_memory=True, collate_fn=collate_fn)
        if val_subset is not None else None
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Class pixel counts (from training clean masks, for weighting)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    print('\nComputing class pixel counts from clean training masks...')
    class_counts = {i: 0 for i in range(NUM_CLASSES)}
    for i in train_idx:
        _, mask_path = full_clean.pairs[i]
        m = cv2.imread(str(mask_path), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
        for c in range(NUM_CLASSES):
            class_counts[c] += int((m == c).sum())
    total = max(sum(class_counts.values()), 1)
    print('  Pixel distribution (training clean):')
    for c in range(NUM_CLASSES):
        pct = class_counts[c] / total * 100
        print(f'    {CLASS_NAMES[c]:14} {pct:5.2f}%')

    drought_idx    = CLASS_TO_IDX.get('drought', 6)
    cotton_idx     = CLASS_TO_IDX.get('cotton_ball', 3)

    # Drought weight override: force high weight so model must learn drought.
    drought_override = cfg_seg.get('drought_override_weight', 30.0)

    # Cotton_ball weight override: auto-computed weight (~32) was too high with
    # 151 clean tiles, causing cotton_ball false positives on bright soil pixels
    # (Balaji v4 showed 15.3% cotton vs 1.1% in v3). Lowering to 12 reduces
    # sensitivity while still giving adequate supervision for the rare class.
    cotton_override = cfg_seg.get('cotton_override_weight', None)

    overrides = {drought_idx: drought_override}
    if cotton_override is not None:
        overrides[cotton_idx] = float(cotton_override)

    class_weights = make_class_weights(
        class_counts, NUM_CLASSES,
        overrides=overrides,
    )
    print('  Computed class weights (drought + cotton overridden):')
    for ci, w in enumerate(class_weights.tolist()):
        print(f'    {CLASS_NAMES[ci]:14} {w:6.2f}')

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Model
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    print('\nBuilding SegFormer-B2 ...')
    model = SegFormerB2(
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
        input_size=crop_size,
        pretrained_id=cfg_seg.get('pretrained_id', 'nvidia/mit-b2'),
        pretrained_local_path=cfg_seg.get('pretrained_local_path'),
        in_channels=9,
    ).to(device)
    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()) / 1e6
    print(f'  parameters: {n_params:.1f}M')

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Loss + optimizer
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    criterion = SegmentationLoss(
        class_weights=class_weights.to(device),
        w_ce=cfg_seg.get('ce_weight', 1.0),
        w_dice=cfg_seg.get('dice_weight', 2.5),
        w_lovasz=cfg_seg.get('boundary_weight', 1.0),
        ignore_index=IGNORE_INDEX,
        focal_gamma=cfg_seg.get('focal_gamma', 2.0),
    ).to(device)

    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        model.parameters(),
        lr=cfg_seg['lr'],
        weight_decay=cfg_seg['weight_decay'],
    )
    scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(
        optimizer, T_max=cfg_seg['epochs'], eta_min=1e-6
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Training loop
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    best_miou   = 0.0
    run_name    = cfg_seg.get('run_name', 'best_v2')
    best_path   = out_dir / f'{run_name}.pt'
    history     = []
    start_epoch = 1

    # Resume from checkpoint if requested
    if resume_path is not None and Path(resume_path).exists():
        print(f'\nResuming from {resume_path} ...')
        ckpt = torch.load(resume_path, map_location=device, weights_only=False)
        model.load_state_dict(ckpt['model_state_dict'])
        best_miou   = 0.0
        start_epoch = 1
        history     = []
        print('  V4 weights loaded; starting V5 from epoch 1.')
        print(f'  Resumed at epoch {start_epoch}, best mIoU so far: {best_miou:.4f}')
    elif resume_path is not None:
        print(f'[WARNING] --resume path not found: {resume_path}. Starting fresh.')


    if resume_path is None or not Path(resume_path).exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError("Provide an existing checkpoint with --resume")

    model.eval()
    t0 = time.time()

    biases = [round(-0.6 + i * 0.1, 2) for i in range(13)]
    matrices = {
        b: np.zeros((NUM_CLASSES, NUM_CLASSES), dtype=np.int64)
        for b in biases
    }

    model.eval()
    with torch.inference_mode():
        for batch in tqdm(dl_val, desc="V11 calibration"):
            images = batch["image"].to(device)
            masks = batch["mask"].to(device)
            logits = model(images)

            for bias in biases:
                adjusted = logits.clone()
                adjusted[:, 6] -= 1.20
                adjusted[:, 5] -= bias
                preds = adjusted.argmax(dim=1)
                update_confusion(matrices[bias], preds, masks, NUM_CLASSES)

    print("\\nDrought bias calibration results")
    print(f"{'Bias':>7} {'mIoU':>10} {'Drought IoU':>14}")

    results = []
    for bias in biases:
        ious, miou = compute_iou_per_class(matrices[bias])
        results.append((bias, miou, ious[6]))
        print(f"{bias:7.2f} {miou*100:9.3f}% {ious[6]*100:13.3f}%")


    best = max(results, key=lambda x: x[1])
    print("\nPer-class IoU at best dry-weed bias:")
    ious, _ = compute_iou_per_class(matrices[best[0]])
    for name, iou in zip(CLASS_NAMES, ious):
        print(f"  {name:14}: {iou*100:.3f}%")

    best = max(results, key=lambda x: x[1])
    print(f"\\nBest tested bias: {best[0]:.2f}")
    print(f"Best validation mIoU: {best[1]*100:.4f}%")
    print(f"Baseline mIoU: {results[0][1]*100:.4f}%")
    return


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--config', type=Path, default=Path('configs/config.yaml'))
    ap.add_argument('--resume', type=Path, default=None,
                    help='Resume from a previous checkpoint .pt file')
    args = ap.parse_args()

    import yaml
    with open(args.config) as f:
        config = yaml.safe_load(f)

    train(config, resume_path=args.resume)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/segmentation/eval_source_independent_v11.py
================================================================================

"""
SegFormer-B2 training loop.

Usage:
    python -m src.segmentation.train --config configs/config.yaml

What it does:
    1. Builds mixed dataset (clean + pseudo + synthetic).
    2. Loads SegFormer-B2 with 9-channel input adapter.
    3. Computes per-class loss weights from training pixel counts.
    4. Trains with CE + Dice + Lovász, with per-sample loss weights.
    5. Validates each epoch on held-out clean tiles.
    6. Saves best mIoU checkpoint, writes training_report.json.
    7. Dumps val_predictions/ with PNG overlays of best-epoch predictions.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
from torch.utils.tensorboard import SummaryWriter
from tqdm import tqdm

from src.preprocessing.tensor_builder import build_9channel
from src.segmentation.dataset import (
    CleanMaskDataset,
    CompositeDataset,
    MixedSegmentationDataset,
    PseudoLabelDataset,
    _augment_image_and_mask,
)
from src.segmentation.losses import SegmentationLoss, make_class_weights
from src.segmentation.model import SegFormerB2
from src.utils.constants import (
    CLASS_NAMES,
    CLASS_TO_IDX,
    IGNORE_INDEX,
    NUM_CLASSES,
    PALETTE,
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def collate_fn(batch: list[dict]) -> dict:
    """Stack the dicts from PatchDataset → batched dict."""
    images       = torch.stack([b['image'] for b in batch])
    masks        = torch.stack([b['mask']  for b in batch])
    weights      = torch.tensor([b['loss_weight'] for b in batch],
                                dtype=torch.float32)
    sources      = [b['source'] for b in batch]
    return {
        'image':         images,
        'mask':          masks,
        'sample_weight': weights,
        'source':        sources,
    }


def compute_iou_per_class(
    confusion: np.ndarray,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, float]:
    """
    Per-class IoU from a (num_classes, num_classes) confusion matrix
    where confusion[true, pred] = pixel count.
    """
    iou_per = np.zeros(NUM_CLASSES, dtype=np.float64)
    for c in range(NUM_CLASSES):
        tp = confusion[c, c]
        fp = confusion[:, c].sum() - tp
        fn = confusion[c, :].sum() - tp
        denom = tp + fp + fn
        iou_per[c] = tp / denom if denom > 0 else np.nan
    miou = np.nanmean(iou_per)
    return iou_per, miou


def update_confusion(
    confusion: np.ndarray,
    preds: torch.Tensor,
    targets: torch.Tensor,
    num_classes: int,
) -> None:
    """Add to confusion matrix in-place. preds/targets are torch (B, H, W)."""
    valid = (targets != IGNORE_INDEX)
    p = preds[valid].cpu().numpy().astype(np.int32)
    t = targets[valid].cpu().numpy().astype(np.int32)
    # bincount approach is fast
    idx = t * num_classes + p
    counts = np.bincount(idx, minlength=num_classes * num_classes)
    confusion += counts.reshape(num_classes, num_classes)


def save_prediction_overlay(
    img_rgb: np.ndarray,
    true_mask: np.ndarray,
    pred_mask: np.ndarray,
    save_path: Path,
) -> None:
    """Save a 3-panel comparison: original | true mask | predicted mask."""
    H, W = img_rgb.shape[:2]

    def colorize(m):
        out = np.zeros((*m.shape, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        for i, c in enumerate(PALETTE):
            out[m == i] = c
        out[m == IGNORE_INDEX] = (90, 90, 90)
        return out

    true_color = colorize(true_mask)
    pred_color = colorize(pred_mask)

    # Side-by-side
    composite = np.hstack([img_rgb, true_color, pred_color])

    # Label bar at top
    bar = np.full((30, composite.shape[1], 3), 230, dtype=np.uint8)
    font = cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX
    cv2.putText(bar, 'Original', (10, 22), font, 0.55, (20,20,20), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    cv2.putText(bar, 'Ground truth', (W + 10, 22), font, 0.55, (20,20,20), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    cv2.putText(bar, 'Prediction', (2*W + 10, 22), font, 0.55, (20,20,20), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    composite = np.vstack([bar, composite])

    save_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    cv2.imwrite(str(save_path), cv2.cvtColor(composite, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR),
                [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 88])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Train
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def train(config: dict, resume_path=None) -> None:
    import os
    device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
    print(f'Device: {device}')
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        print(f'GPU:    {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}')
        print(f'VRAM:   {torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory/1e9:.1f} GB')
    slurm_keys = ['SLURM_JOB_ID','SLURM_JOB_NAME','SLURM_NODELIST','SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK']
    slurm_info = {k: os.environ[k] for k in slurm_keys if k in os.environ}
    if slurm_info:
        print('SLURM:', slurm_info)

    paths   = config['paths']
    cfg_seg = config['segmentation']

    out_dir = Path(paths['runs_dir']) / 'segmentation'
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    writer = SummaryWriter(log_dir=out_dir / cfg_seg.get('run_name', 'default') / 'tb_logs')

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Datasets
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    crop_size = cfg_seg['input_size']

    clean_images_dir = Path(paths.get('clean_images_dir',
                                      'data/clean_images'))
    clean_masks_dir  = Path(paths.get('clean_masks_dir',
                                      'data/clean_masks'))

    # Stratified train/val split: ensure drought-rich tiles go to val
    all_clean_masks = sorted(
        m for m in clean_masks_dir.glob('*.png')
        if any(
            (clean_images_dir / (m.stem + ext)).is_file()
            for ext in ('.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.tif', '.tiff',
                        '.PNG', '.JPG', '.JPEG', '.TIF', '.TIFF')
        )
    )
    if len(all_clean_masks) < 10:
        print(f'[WARNING] Only {len(all_clean_masks)} clean masks found. '
              'Validation will be unreliable.')
    val_n = max(3, int(len(all_clean_masks) * 0.2))

    # Compute drought fraction per tile for stratification
    drought_fracs = {}
    for m in all_clean_masks:
        mask_arr = cv2.imread(str(m), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
        valid    = mask_arr != 255
        n_valid  = max(valid.sum(), 1)
        drought_fracs[m.stem] = float((mask_arr == 6).sum()) / n_valid

    # Sort by drought fraction descending, interleave into val/train
    sorted_stems = sorted(drought_fracs, key=drought_fracs.get, reverse=True)
    # Take every (1/val_ratio)-th tile from the top-drought end for val
    val_stems = set()
    step = max(1, len(sorted_stems) // val_n)
    for i in range(0, len(sorted_stems), step):
        if len(val_stems) >= val_n:
            break
        val_stems.add(sorted_stems[i])
    # Fallback: fill val_n if not reached
    for s in sorted_stems:
        if len(val_stems) >= val_n: break
        val_stems.add(s)

    print(f'Clean masks total: {len(all_clean_masks)}, val: {len(val_stems)}')
    print(f'Val tile drought fracs: {[round(drought_fracs[s],3) for s in val_stems]}')

    # Build sub-datasets
    # CleanMaskDataset filters by directory listing — for the train/val split
    # we create two filtered subfolder views using symlinks-or-list
    # Simpler: instantiate one ClanMaskDataset and split via Subset
    full_clean = CleanMaskDataset(
        images_dir=clean_images_dir,
        masks_dir=clean_masks_dir,
        crop_size=crop_size,
        train=True,
    )
    train_idx, val_idx = [], []
    for i, (img, mask) in enumerate(full_clean.pairs):
        if mask.stem in val_stems:
            val_idx.append(i)
        else:
            train_idx.append(i)
    print(f'  Clean train: {len(train_idx)}, val: {len(val_idx)}')

    from torch.utils.data import Subset
    clean_train = Subset(full_clean, train_idx) if train_idx else None

    # Val-mode clean (no augmentation, centre crop)
    full_clean_val = CleanMaskDataset(
        images_dir=clean_images_dir,
        masks_dir=clean_masks_dir,
        crop_size=crop_size,
        train=False,
    )
    val_subset = Subset(full_clean_val, val_idx) if val_idx else None

    pseudo_train = PseudoLabelDataset(
        images_dir=paths['unlabeled_dir'],
        masks_dir=Path(paths.get('pseudo_masks_dir', '/content/v5_data/pseudo_masks')),
        crop_size=crop_size,
        train=True,
    )

    composite_train = CompositeDataset(
        patches_dir=paths['patches_dir'],
        backgrounds_dir=paths['unlabeled_dir'],
        crop_size=crop_size,
        virtual_length=cfg_seg.get('composite_per_epoch', 1000),
        train=True,
        seed=cfg_seg.get('seed', 42),
    )

    mixed = MixedSegmentationDataset(
        clean=clean_train,
        pseudo=pseudo_train,
        composite=composite_train,
        ratio_clean    = cfg_seg.get('batch_manual',    4),
        ratio_pseudo   = cfg_seg.get('batch_pseudo',    4),
        ratio_composite= cfg_seg.get('batch_composites', 8),
    )
    print(f'  Mixed train length: {len(mixed)}')
    print(f'  Composite per epoch: {cfg_seg.get("composite_per_epoch", 1000)}')

    dl_train = DataLoader(
        mixed,
        batch_size=cfg_seg['batch_size'],
        shuffle=True,
        num_workers=cfg_seg.get('num_workers', 4),
        pin_memory=True,
        drop_last=True,
        collate_fn=collate_fn,
    )
    dl_val = (
        DataLoader(val_subset,
                   batch_size=cfg_seg['batch_size'],
                   shuffle=False, num_workers=2,
                   pin_memory=True, collate_fn=collate_fn)
        if val_subset is not None else None
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Class pixel counts (from training clean masks, for weighting)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    print('\nComputing class pixel counts from clean training masks...')
    class_counts = {i: 0 for i in range(NUM_CLASSES)}
    for i in train_idx:
        _, mask_path = full_clean.pairs[i]
        m = cv2.imread(str(mask_path), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
        for c in range(NUM_CLASSES):
            class_counts[c] += int((m == c).sum())
    total = max(sum(class_counts.values()), 1)
    print('  Pixel distribution (training clean):')
    for c in range(NUM_CLASSES):
        pct = class_counts[c] / total * 100
        print(f'    {CLASS_NAMES[c]:14} {pct:5.2f}%')

    drought_idx    = CLASS_TO_IDX.get('drought', 6)
    cotton_idx     = CLASS_TO_IDX.get('cotton_ball', 3)

    # Drought weight override: force high weight so model must learn drought.
    drought_override = cfg_seg.get('drought_override_weight', 30.0)

    # Cotton_ball weight override: auto-computed weight (~32) was too high with
    # 151 clean tiles, causing cotton_ball false positives on bright soil pixels
    # (Balaji v4 showed 15.3% cotton vs 1.1% in v3). Lowering to 12 reduces
    # sensitivity while still giving adequate supervision for the rare class.
    cotton_override = cfg_seg.get('cotton_override_weight', None)

    overrides = {drought_idx: drought_override}
    if cotton_override is not None:
        overrides[cotton_idx] = float(cotton_override)

    class_weights = make_class_weights(
        class_counts, NUM_CLASSES,
        overrides=overrides,
    )
    print('  Computed class weights (drought + cotton overridden):')
    for ci, w in enumerate(class_weights.tolist()):
        print(f'    {CLASS_NAMES[ci]:14} {w:6.2f}')

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Model
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    print('\nBuilding SegFormer-B2 ...')
    model = SegFormerB2(
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
        input_size=crop_size,
        pretrained_id=cfg_seg.get('pretrained_id', 'nvidia/mit-b2'),
        pretrained_local_path=cfg_seg.get('pretrained_local_path'),
        in_channels=9,
    ).to(device)
    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()) / 1e6
    print(f'  parameters: {n_params:.1f}M')

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Loss + optimizer
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    criterion = SegmentationLoss(
        class_weights=class_weights.to(device),
        w_ce=cfg_seg.get('ce_weight', 1.0),
        w_dice=cfg_seg.get('dice_weight', 2.5),
        w_lovasz=cfg_seg.get('boundary_weight', 1.0),
        ignore_index=IGNORE_INDEX,
        focal_gamma=cfg_seg.get('focal_gamma', 2.0),
    ).to(device)

    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        model.parameters(),
        lr=cfg_seg['lr'],
        weight_decay=cfg_seg['weight_decay'],
    )
    scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(
        optimizer, T_max=cfg_seg['epochs'], eta_min=1e-6
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Training loop
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    best_miou   = 0.0
    run_name    = cfg_seg.get('run_name', 'best_v2')
    best_path   = out_dir / f'{run_name}.pt'
    history     = []
    start_epoch = 1

    # Resume from checkpoint if requested
    if resume_path is not None and Path(resume_path).exists():
        print(f'\nResuming from {resume_path} ...')
        ckpt = torch.load(resume_path, map_location=device, weights_only=False)
        model.load_state_dict(ckpt['model_state_dict'])
        best_miou   = 0.0
        start_epoch = 1
        history     = []
        print('  V4 weights loaded; starting V5 from epoch 1.')
        print(f'  Resumed at epoch {start_epoch}, best mIoU so far: {best_miou:.4f}')
    elif resume_path is not None:
        print(f'[WARNING] --resume path not found: {resume_path}. Starting fresh.')


    if resume_path is None or not Path(resume_path).exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError("Provide an existing checkpoint with --resume")

    model.eval()
    t0 = time.time()

    biases = [round(-0.6 + i * 0.1, 2) for i in range(13)]
    matrices = {
        b: np.zeros((NUM_CLASSES, NUM_CLASSES), dtype=np.int64)
        for b in biases
    }



    import re

    def source_id(stem):
        return re.sub(r"_r[0-9]+_c[0-9]+$", "", stem)

    # Reconstruct the actual training/validation split using mask stems.
    train_stems = {
        full_clean.pairs[i][1].stem for i in train_idx
    }
    train_sources = {source_id(s) for s in train_stems}

    independent_stems = {
        s for s in val_stems
        if source_id(s) not in train_sources
    }

    independent_indices = [
        i for i, (_, mask) in enumerate(full_clean_val.pairs)
        if mask.stem in independent_stems
    ]

    print("Training source IDs:", len(train_sources))
    print("Source-independent validation tiles:", len(independent_indices))

    assert len(independent_indices) == 33, (
        f"Expected 33 tiles, found {len(independent_indices)}. "
        "Stopping rather than evaluating the wrong subset."
    )

    dl_val = DataLoader(
        Subset(full_clean_val, independent_indices),
        batch_size=cfg_seg["batch_size"],
        shuffle=False,
        num_workers=2,
        pin_memory=True,
        collate_fn=collate_fn,
    )

    model.eval()
    with torch.inference_mode():
        for batch in tqdm(dl_val, desc="V11 calibration"):
            images = batch["image"].to(device)
            masks = batch["mask"].to(device)
            logits = model(images)

            for bias in biases:
                adjusted = logits.clone()
                adjusted[:, 6] -= 1.20
                adjusted[:, 5] -= bias
                preds = adjusted.argmax(dim=1)
                update_confusion(matrices[bias], preds, masks, NUM_CLASSES)

    print("\\nDrought bias calibration results")
    print(f"{'Bias':>7} {'mIoU':>10} {'Drought IoU':>14}")

    results = []
    for bias in biases:
        ious, miou = compute_iou_per_class(matrices[bias])
        results.append((bias, miou, ious[6]))
        print(f"{bias:7.2f} {miou*100:9.3f}% {ious[6]*100:13.3f}%")


    best = max(results, key=lambda x: x[1])
    print("\nPer-class IoU at best dry-weed bias:")
    ious, _ = compute_iou_per_class(matrices[best[0]])
    for name, iou in zip(CLASS_NAMES, ious):
        print(f"  {name:14}: {iou*100:.3f}%")

    best = max(results, key=lambda x: x[1])
    print(f"\\nBest tested bias: {best[0]:.2f}")
    print(f"Best validation mIoU: {best[1]*100:.4f}%")
    print(f"Baseline mIoU: {results[0][1]*100:.4f}%")
    return


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--config', type=Path, default=Path('configs/config.yaml'))
    ap.add_argument('--resume', type=Path, default=None,
                    help='Resume from a previous checkpoint .pt file')
    args = ap.parse_args()

    import yaml
    with open(args.config) as f:
        config = yaml.safe_load(f)

    train(config, resume_path=args.resume)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/segmentation/losses.py
================================================================================

"""
Segmentation losses — v2 (batch 4.1).

Key change from v1: replaces plain CrossEntropy with FocalLoss.
Focal loss (Lin et al. 2017) multiplies each pixel's CE by (1-p_t)^gamma
where p_t is the model's probability for the correct class.

Effect for drought:
    - Easy soil predictions (p_soil = 0.95): focal weight = (1-0.95)^2 = 0.0025
      → these gradients are suppressed 400x
    - Hard drought misclassified as soil (p_drought = 0.08): focal weight = 0.846
      → these gradients are barely reduced
    - Net ratio easy:hard = 340x larger for hard examples → forces model to
      learn drought instead of ignoring it.

Also added: explicit per-class weight overrides (drought_override_weight) so
drought doesn't rely solely on inverse-frequency weighting which gave only 2.3.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from src.utils.constants import CLASS_TO_IDX, IGNORE_INDEX


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Class weights
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_class_weights(
    class_pixel_counts: dict[int, int],
    num_classes: int,
    floor: float = 0.1,
    cap: float = 50.0,
    overrides: dict[int, float] | None = None,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    """
    Inverse-frequency class weights with optional manual overrides.

    Args:
        class_pixel_counts: {class_idx: pixel_count}
        overrides:          {class_idx: weight} — manual override, bypasses
                            inverse-frequency for specified classes.
                            Example: {6: 30.0} forces drought to weight 30.
    """
    total   = sum(class_pixel_counts.values())
    weights = torch.ones(num_classes, dtype=torch.float32)
    for idx in range(num_classes):
        count = class_pixel_counts.get(idx, 0)
        if count <= 0:
            weights[idx] = 1.0
        else:
            weights[idx] = total / (num_classes * count)
    weights = weights.clamp(min=floor, max=cap)
    # Apply manual overrides AFTER clipping
    if overrides:
        for idx, w in overrides.items():
            if 0 <= idx < num_classes:
                weights[idx] = w
    return weights


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Focal Loss (replaces plain CE)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class FocalLoss(nn.Module):
    """
    Per-pixel focal loss with class weights and ignore_index.

    FL(p_t) = -alpha_t * (1 - p_t)^gamma * log(p_t)

    where:
        alpha_t  = class_weights[class]
        p_t      = softmax probability of the correct class
        gamma    = focusing exponent (2.0 is standard)

    Args:
        class_weights: (C,) alpha values per class.
        gamma:         focusing exponent. Higher → focus more on hard examples.
        ignore_index:  pixels to skip.
        reduction:     'none' (per-pixel) for use in SegmentationLoss.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        class_weights: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        gamma: float = 2.0,
        ignore_index: int = IGNORE_INDEX,
        reduction: str = 'none',
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.gamma        = gamma
        self.ignore_index = ignore_index
        self.reduction    = reduction
        if class_weights is not None:
            self.register_buffer('class_weights', class_weights)
        else:
            self.class_weights = None

    def forward(
        self, logits: torch.Tensor, targets: torch.Tensor
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Args:
            logits:  (B, C, H, W)
            targets: (B, H, W) int64

        Returns:
            (B, H, W) per-pixel focal loss (ignored pixels = 0)
        """
        B, C, H, W = logits.shape
        # Compute log-softmax for numerical stability
        log_probs = F.log_softmax(logits, dim=1)          # (B, C, H, W)
        probs     = torch.exp(log_probs)                  # (B, C, H, W)

        # Mask ignored pixels — clamp target to valid range for indexing
        valid   = (targets != self.ignore_index)          # (B, H, W) bool
        t_safe  = targets.clone()
        t_safe[~valid] = 0

        # Gather log-prob and prob for the correct class
        t_safe_expand = t_safe.unsqueeze(1)                # (B, 1, H, W)
        log_pt = log_probs.gather(1, t_safe_expand).squeeze(1)   # (B, H, W)
        pt     = probs.gather(1, t_safe_expand).squeeze(1)       # (B, H, W)

        # Focal factor: (1 - p_t)^gamma
        focal_factor = (1.0 - pt).pow(self.gamma)         # (B, H, W)

        # Per-pixel CE with focal scaling
        loss = -focal_factor * log_pt                     # (B, H, W)

        # Apply class weights (alpha_t)
        if self.class_weights is not None:
            w = self.class_weights.to(logits.device)
            alpha_t = w[t_safe]                           # (B, H, W)
            loss    = alpha_t * loss

        # Zero out ignored pixels
        loss = loss * valid.float()

        if self.reduction == 'mean':
            return loss.sum() / valid.float().sum().clamp(min=1.0)
        elif self.reduction == 'sum':
            return loss.sum()
        return loss    # 'none'


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dice Loss (unchanged from v1)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class DiceLoss(nn.Module):
    """Multi-class soft Dice loss with ignore-index support."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        smooth: float = 1.0,
        per_image: bool = False,
        ignore_index: int = IGNORE_INDEX,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.smooth       = smooth
        self.per_image    = per_image
        self.ignore_index = ignore_index

    def forward(self, logits: torch.Tensor, targets: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, C, H, W = logits.shape
        probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1)

        valid          = (targets != self.ignore_index)
        t_clamped      = targets.clone()
        t_clamped[~valid] = 0
        one_hot        = F.one_hot(t_clamped, C).permute(0, 3, 1, 2).float()
        one_hot        = one_hot * valid.unsqueeze(1).float()
        probs          = probs   * valid.unsqueeze(1).float()

        if self.per_image:
            inter = (probs * one_hot).sum(dim=(2, 3))
            union = probs.sum(dim=(2, 3)) + one_hot.sum(dim=(2, 3))
            dice  = (2 * inter + self.smooth) / (union + self.smooth)
            return 1.0 - dice.mean()
        else:
            inter = (probs * one_hot).sum(dim=(0, 2, 3))
            union = probs.sum(dim=(0, 2, 3)) + one_hot.sum(dim=(0, 2, 3))
            dice  = (2 * inter + self.smooth) / (union + self.smooth)
            return 1.0 - dice.mean()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Lovász boundary loss (unchanged from v1)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _lovasz_grad(gt_sorted: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    p = len(gt_sorted)
    gts = gt_sorted.sum()
    intersection = gts - gt_sorted.float().cumsum(0)
    union        = gts + (1.0 - gt_sorted.float()).cumsum(0)
    jaccard      = 1.0 - intersection / union
    if p > 1:
        jaccard[1:p] = jaccard[1:p] - jaccard[0:-1]
    return jaccard


def lovasz_softmax_flat(
    probs_flat: torch.Tensor, labels_flat: torch.Tensor
) -> torch.Tensor:
    C      = probs_flat.shape[1]
    losses = []
    for c in range(C):
        fg = (labels_flat == c).float()
        if fg.sum() == 0:
            continue
        errors = (fg - probs_flat[:, c]).abs()
        errors_sorted, perm = torch.sort(errors, descending=True)
        fg_sorted = fg[perm]
        losses.append(torch.dot(errors_sorted, _lovasz_grad(fg_sorted)))
    if not losses:
        return probs_flat.sum() * 0.0
    return torch.stack(losses).mean()


class LovaszSoftmaxLoss(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, ignore_index: int = IGNORE_INDEX) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.ignore_index = ignore_index

    def forward(self, logits: torch.Tensor, targets: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        probs       = F.softmax(logits, dim=1)
        B, C, H, W  = probs.shape
        probs_flat  = probs.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).reshape(-1, C)
        labels_flat = targets.reshape(-1)
        valid       = labels_flat != self.ignore_index
        if not valid.any():
            return logits.sum() * 0.0
        return lovasz_softmax_flat(probs_flat[valid], labels_flat[valid])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Combined loss
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class SegmentationLoss(nn.Module):
    """
    Combined: w_focal * FocalCE  +  w_dice * Dice  +  w_lovasz * Lovász

    Key change from v1: plain CE → FocalLoss with gamma=2.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        class_weights: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        w_ce: float = 1.0,          # weight on focal CE
        w_dice: float = 2.5,        # higher than v1 — Dice rescues rare classes
        w_lovasz: float = 1.0,      # higher than v1 — boundary sharpening
        ignore_index: int = IGNORE_INDEX,
        focal_gamma: float = 2.0,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.w_ce      = w_ce
        self.w_dice    = w_dice
        self.w_lovasz  = w_lovasz
        self.ignore_index = ignore_index

        self.focal  = FocalLoss(
            class_weights=class_weights,
            gamma=focal_gamma,
            ignore_index=ignore_index,
            reduction='none',
        )
        self.dice   = DiceLoss(ignore_index=ignore_index)
        self.lovasz = LovaszSoftmaxLoss(ignore_index=ignore_index)

    def forward(
        self,
        logits: torch.Tensor,
        targets: torch.Tensor,
        sample_weights: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        # Focal CE — per-pixel, then weight by sample importance
        focal_pixel = self.focal(logits, targets)          # (B, H, W), ignored=0
        if sample_weights is not None:
            sw          = sample_weights.view(-1, 1, 1).to(focal_pixel.device)
            focal_pixel = focal_pixel * sw
        valid   = (targets != self.ignore_index).float()
        n_valid = valid.sum().clamp(min=1.0)
        focal_loss = (focal_pixel * valid).sum() / n_valid

        dice_loss   = self.dice(logits, targets)
        lovasz_loss = self.lovasz(logits, targets)

        total = (self.w_ce * focal_loss +
                 self.w_dice * dice_loss +
                 self.w_lovasz * lovasz_loss)

        return {
            'loss':   total,
            'focal':  focal_loss.detach(),
            'dice':   dice_loss.detach(),
            'lovasz': lovasz_loss.detach(),
        }




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/segmentation/model.py
================================================================================

"""
SegFormer-B2 wrapper for the drought segmentation task.

Why SegFormer:
    - Hierarchical (multi-scale) features — natural fit for our spatial
      classes that span small (cotton_ball) to large (whole-field drought).
    - MiT (Mix Transformer) encoder is efficient — fits batch=16 at 512²
      on H200 comfortably.
    - Lightweight MLP decoder — fast inference at orthomosaic scale.

Key adaptations from stock SegFormer:
    - 9-channel input (RGB + 5 vegetation indices + V) instead of 3
    - 7-class output head
    - Pretrained ImageNet weights are loaded into the encoder where possible
      (first conv inflated 3→9 with weight surgery)
    - Output upsampled from H/4 × W/4 to full H × W via bilinear interpolation
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from transformers import (
    SegformerConfig,
    SegformerForSemanticSegmentation,
)

from src.utils.constants import NUM_CLASSES


# SegFormer-B2 architecture specs (from the paper)
_SEGFORMER_B2_HIDDEN  = [64, 128, 320, 512]
_SEGFORMER_B2_DEPTHS  = [3, 4, 6, 3]
_SEGFORMER_B2_DEC_DIM = 768


class SegFormerB2(nn.Module):
    """
    SegFormer-B2 with 9-channel input and configurable num_classes.

    Args:
        num_classes:           Output classes (default 7).
        input_size:            Input resolution (must be divisible by 16).
        pretrained_id:         HF model ID for ImageNet pretrained encoder
                               (e.g., 'nvidia/mit-b2'). None → random init.
        pretrained_local_path: Path to a local .pt/.bin file with state_dict
                               from a previous run. Overrides pretrained_id.
        in_channels:           Input channels (default 9 for our pipeline).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        num_classes: int = NUM_CLASSES,
        input_size: int = 512,
        pretrained_id: str | None = 'nvidia/mit-b2',
        pretrained_local_path: str | None = None,
        in_channels: int = 9,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.num_classes = num_classes
        self.input_size  = input_size
        self.in_channels = in_channels

        # Build config — uses 3 channels for compatibility with pretrained
        # weights, then we surgically replace the first conv with a 9-ch one.
        config_kwargs = dict(
            num_channels=3,
            num_labels=num_classes,
            hidden_sizes=_SEGFORMER_B2_HIDDEN,
            depths=_SEGFORMER_B2_DEPTHS,
            decoder_hidden_size=_SEGFORMER_B2_DEC_DIM,
            semantic_loss_ignore_index=255,
        )
        cfg = SegformerConfig(**config_kwargs)

        # Try to download pretrained encoder. Fall back to random init.
        pretrained_3ch_sd = None
        if pretrained_local_path is not None and os.path.isfile(pretrained_local_path):
            try:
                sd = torch.load(pretrained_local_path, map_location='cpu',
                                weights_only=True)
                if 'model_state_dict' in sd:
                    sd = sd['model_state_dict']
                pretrained_3ch_sd = sd
                print(f'[INFO] Loaded local SegFormer weights from {pretrained_local_path}')
            except Exception as e:
                print(f'[WARNING] Failed local load: {e}')

        if pretrained_3ch_sd is None and pretrained_id:
            try:
                # Force safetensors (avoids torch.load CVE check on older torch)
                hf_model = SegformerForSemanticSegmentation.from_pretrained(
                    pretrained_id, num_labels=num_classes,
                    ignore_mismatched_sizes=True,
                    use_safetensors=True,
                )
                pretrained_3ch_sd = hf_model.state_dict()
                del hf_model
                print(f'[INFO] Loaded SegFormer-B2 from HuggingFace ({pretrained_id})')
            except Exception as e:
                print(f'[WARNING] HF download failed: {e}')
                # Fallback: try loading from local path if available
                if pretrained_local_path and os.path.isfile(pretrained_local_path):
                    try:
                        sd = torch.load(pretrained_local_path, map_location='cpu',
                                        weights_only=True)
                        if isinstance(sd, dict) and 'model_state_dict' in sd:
                            sd = sd['model_state_dict']
                        pretrained_3ch_sd = sd
                        print(f'[INFO] Loaded SegFormer-B2 from local {pretrained_local_path}')
                    except Exception as e2:
                        print(f'[WARNING] Local load also failed: {e2}')
                if pretrained_3ch_sd is None:
                    print('         Encoder will use random init (NOT recommended).')

        # Build the actual model with our channel count
        self.model = SegformerForSemanticSegmentation(cfg)

        # Load pretrained weights (3-channel)
        if pretrained_3ch_sd is not None:
            missing, unexpected = self.model.load_state_dict(
                pretrained_3ch_sd, strict=False
            )
            if len(missing) > 0:
                print(f'[INFO] Missing {len(missing)} keys (head re-init expected)')

        # Now inflate the first patch_embed conv from 3 → in_channels channels
        # Locate: model.segformer.encoder.patch_embeddings[0].proj
        first_conv = self.model.segformer.stages[0].patch_embeddings.proj
        old_w = first_conv.weight.data        # (out_C, 3, kH, kW)
        out_c, _, kh, kw = old_w.shape

        new_conv = nn.Conv2d(
            in_channels, out_c,
            kernel_size=(kh, kw),
            stride=first_conv.stride,
            padding=first_conv.padding,
            bias=first_conv.bias is not None,
        )
        with torch.no_grad():
            new_w = new_conv.weight.data
            # Copy RGB channels (scaled to preserve magnitude)
            new_w[:, :3, :, :] = old_w / 3.0
            # Zero-init the new channels — they learn from scratch but don't
            # interfere with the pretrained RGB signal
            new_w[:, 3:, :, :] = 0.0
            new_conv.weight.data = new_w
            if first_conv.bias is not None:
                new_conv.bias.data = first_conv.bias.data.clone()

        self.model.segformer.stages[0].patch_embeddings.proj = new_conv

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Args:
            x: (B, in_channels, H, W) float32
        Returns:
            (B, num_classes, H, W) logits — upsampled to input resolution
        """
        H, W = x.shape[-2:]
        outputs = self.model(pixel_values=x)
        logits  = outputs.logits           # (B, num_classes, H/4, W/4)
        # Upsample to full input size
        logits = F.interpolate(logits, size=(H, W),
                               mode='bilinear', align_corners=False)
        return logits

"""

    - Hierarchical (multi-scale) features — natural fit for our spatial

                        sd = torch.load(pretrained_local_path, map_location='cpu',
                                        weights_only=True)
                        if isinstance(sd, dict) and 'model_state_dict' in sd:
                            sd = sd['model_state_dict']
                        pretrained_3ch_sd = sd
                        print(f'[INFO] Loaded SegFormer-B3 from local {pretrained_local_path}')

                        print(f'[WARNING] Local load also failed: {e2}')

        Args:

SOURCE FILE: src/segmentation/model_deeplab.py

import torch.nn as nn
import segmentation_models_pytorch as smp


class DeepLabV3Plus(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        num_classes=7,
        input_size=512,
        pretrained_id=None,
        pretrained_local_path=None,
        in_channels=9,
    ):
        super().__init__()

        self.model = smp.DeepLabV3Plus(
            encoder_name="resnet50",
            encoder_weights="imagenet",
            in_channels=in_channels,
            classes=num_classes,
            activation=None,
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.model(x)




================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/segmentation/train.py
================================================================================

"""
SegFormer-B2 training loop.

Usage:
    python -m src.segmentation.train --config configs/config.yaml

What it does:
    1. Builds mixed dataset (clean + pseudo + synthetic).
    2. Loads SegFormer-B2 with 9-channel input adapter.
    3. Computes per-class loss weights from training pixel counts.
    4. Trains with CE + Dice + Lovász, with per-sample loss weights.
    5. Validates each epoch on held-out clean tiles.
    6. Saves best mIoU checkpoint, writes training_report.json.
    7. Dumps val_predictions/ with PNG overlays of best-epoch predictions.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
from torch.utils.tensorboard import SummaryWriter
from tqdm import tqdm

from src.preprocessing.tensor_builder import build_9channel
from src.segmentation.dataset import (
    CleanMaskDataset,
    CompositeDataset,
    MixedSegmentationDataset,
    PseudoLabelDataset,
    _augment_image_and_mask,
)
from src.segmentation.losses import SegmentationLoss, make_class_weights
from src.segmentation.model import SegFormerB2
from src.utils.constants import (
    CLASS_NAMES,
    CLASS_TO_IDX,
    IGNORE_INDEX,
    NUM_CLASSES,
    PALETTE,
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def collate_fn(batch: list[dict]) -> dict:
    """Stack the dicts from PatchDataset → batched dict."""
    images       = torch.stack([b['image'] for b in batch])
    masks        = torch.stack([b['mask']  for b in batch])
    weights      = torch.tensor([b['loss_weight'] for b in batch],
                                dtype=torch.float32)
    sources      = [b['source'] for b in batch]
    return {
        'image':         images,
        'mask':          masks,
        'sample_weight': weights,
        'source':        sources,
    }


def compute_iou_per_class(
    confusion: np.ndarray,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, float]:
    """
    Per-class IoU from a (num_classes, num_classes) confusion matrix
    where confusion[true, pred] = pixel count.
    """
    iou_per = np.zeros(NUM_CLASSES, dtype=np.float64)
    for c in range(NUM_CLASSES):
        tp = confusion[c, c]
        fp = confusion[:, c].sum() - tp
        fn = confusion[c, :].sum() - tp
        denom = tp + fp + fn
        iou_per[c] = tp / denom if denom > 0 else np.nan
    miou = np.nanmean(iou_per)
    return iou_per, miou


def update_confusion(
    confusion: np.ndarray,
    preds: torch.Tensor,
    targets: torch.Tensor,
    num_classes: int,
) -> None:
    """Add to confusion matrix in-place. preds/targets are torch (B, H, W)."""
    valid = (targets != IGNORE_INDEX)
    p = preds[valid].cpu().numpy().astype(np.int32)
    t = targets[valid].cpu().numpy().astype(np.int32)
    # bincount approach is fast
    idx = t * num_classes + p
    counts = np.bincount(idx, minlength=num_classes * num_classes)
    confusion += counts.reshape(num_classes, num_classes)


def save_prediction_overlay(
    img_rgb: np.ndarray,
    true_mask: np.ndarray,
    pred_mask: np.ndarray,
    save_path: Path,
) -> None:
    """Save a 3-panel comparison: original | true mask | predicted mask."""
    H, W = img_rgb.shape[:2]

    def colorize(m):
        out = np.zeros((*m.shape, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        for i, c in enumerate(PALETTE):
            out[m == i] = c
        out[m == IGNORE_INDEX] = (90, 90, 90)
        return out

    true_color = colorize(true_mask)
    pred_color = colorize(pred_mask)

    # Side-by-side
    composite = np.hstack([img_rgb, true_color, pred_color])

    # Label bar at top
    bar = np.full((30, composite.shape[1], 3), 230, dtype=np.uint8)
    font = cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX
    cv2.putText(bar, 'Original', (10, 22), font, 0.55, (20,20,20), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    cv2.putText(bar, 'Ground truth', (W + 10, 22), font, 0.55, (20,20,20), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    cv2.putText(bar, 'Prediction', (2*W + 10, 22), font, 0.55, (20,20,20), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    composite = np.vstack([bar, composite])

    save_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    cv2.imwrite(str(save_path), cv2.cvtColor(composite, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR),
                [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 88])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Train
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def train(config: dict, resume_path=None) -> None:
    import os
    device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
    print(f'Device: {device}')
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        print(f'GPU:    {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}')
        print(f'VRAM:   {torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0).total_memory/1e9:.1f} GB')
    slurm_keys = ['SLURM_JOB_ID','SLURM_JOB_NAME','SLURM_NODELIST','SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK']
    slurm_info = {k: os.environ[k] for k in slurm_keys if k in os.environ}
    if slurm_info:
        print('SLURM:', slurm_info)

    paths   = config['paths']
    cfg_seg = config['segmentation']

    out_dir = Path(paths['runs_dir']) / 'segmentation'
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    writer = SummaryWriter(log_dir=out_dir / cfg_seg.get('run_name', 'default') / 'tb_logs')

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Datasets
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    crop_size = cfg_seg['input_size']

    clean_images_dir = Path(paths.get('clean_images_dir',
                                      'data/clean_images'))
    clean_masks_dir  = Path(paths.get('clean_masks_dir',
                                      'data/clean_masks'))

    # Stratified train/val split: ensure drought-rich tiles go to val
    all_clean_masks = sorted(
        m for m in clean_masks_dir.glob('*.png')
        if any(
            (clean_images_dir / (m.stem + ext)).is_file()
            for ext in ('.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.tif', '.tiff',
                        '.PNG', '.JPG', '.JPEG', '.TIF', '.TIFF')
        )
    )
    if len(all_clean_masks) < 10:
        print(f'[WARNING] Only {len(all_clean_masks)} clean masks found. '
              'Validation will be unreliable.')
    val_n = max(3, int(len(all_clean_masks) * 0.2))

    # Compute drought fraction per tile for stratification
    drought_fracs = {}
    for m in all_clean_masks:
        mask_arr = cv2.imread(str(m), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
        valid    = mask_arr != 255
        n_valid  = max(valid.sum(), 1)
        drought_fracs[m.stem] = float((mask_arr == 6).sum()) / n_valid

    # Sort by drought fraction descending, interleave into val/train
    sorted_stems = sorted(drought_fracs, key=drought_fracs.get, reverse=True)
    # Take every (1/val_ratio)-th tile from the top-drought end for val
    val_stems = set()
    step = max(1, len(sorted_stems) // val_n)
    for i in range(0, len(sorted_stems), step):
        if len(val_stems) >= val_n:
            break
        val_stems.add(sorted_stems[i])
    # Fallback: fill val_n if not reached
    for s in sorted_stems:
        if len(val_stems) >= val_n: break
        val_stems.add(s)

    print(f'Clean masks total: {len(all_clean_masks)}, val: {len(val_stems)}')
    print(f'Val tile drought fracs: {[round(drought_fracs[s],3) for s in val_stems]}')

    # Build sub-datasets
    # CleanMaskDataset filters by directory listing — for the train/val split
    # we create two filtered subfolder views using symlinks-or-list
    # Simpler: instantiate one ClanMaskDataset and split via Subset
    full_clean = CleanMaskDataset(
        images_dir=clean_images_dir,
        masks_dir=clean_masks_dir,
        crop_size=crop_size,
        train=True,
    )
    train_idx, val_idx = [], []
    for i, (img, mask) in enumerate(full_clean.pairs):
        if mask.stem in val_stems:
            val_idx.append(i)
        else:
            train_idx.append(i)
    print(f'  Clean train: {len(train_idx)}, val: {len(val_idx)}')

    from torch.utils.data import Subset
    clean_train = Subset(full_clean, train_idx) if train_idx else None

    # Val-mode clean (no augmentation, centre crop)
    full_clean_val = CleanMaskDataset(
        images_dir=clean_images_dir,
        masks_dir=clean_masks_dir,
        crop_size=crop_size,
        train=False,
    )
    val_subset = Subset(full_clean_val, val_idx) if val_idx else None

    pseudo_train = PseudoLabelDataset(
        images_dir=paths['unlabeled_dir'],
        masks_dir=Path(paths.get('pseudo_masks_dir', '/content/v5_data/pseudo_masks')),
        crop_size=crop_size,
        train=True,
    )

    composite_train = CompositeDataset(
        patches_dir=paths['patches_dir'],
        backgrounds_dir=paths['unlabeled_dir'],
        crop_size=crop_size,
        virtual_length=cfg_seg.get('composite_per_epoch', 1000),
        train=True,
        seed=cfg_seg.get('seed', 42),
    )

    mixed = MixedSegmentationDataset(
        clean=clean_train,
        pseudo=pseudo_train,
        composite=composite_train,
        ratio_clean    = cfg_seg.get('batch_manual',    4),
        ratio_pseudo   = cfg_seg.get('batch_pseudo',    4),
        ratio_composite= cfg_seg.get('batch_composites', 8),
    )
    print(f'  Mixed train length: {len(mixed)}')
    print(f'  Composite per epoch: {cfg_seg.get("composite_per_epoch", 1000)}')

    dl_train = DataLoader(
        mixed,
        batch_size=cfg_seg['batch_size'],
        shuffle=True,
        num_workers=cfg_seg.get('num_workers', 4),
        pin_memory=True,
        drop_last=True,
        collate_fn=collate_fn,
    )
    dl_val = (
        DataLoader(val_subset,
                   batch_size=cfg_seg['batch_size'],
                   shuffle=False, num_workers=2,
                   pin_memory=True, collate_fn=collate_fn)
        if val_subset is not None else None
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Class pixel counts (from training clean masks, for weighting)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    print('\nComputing class pixel counts from clean training masks...')
    class_counts = {i: 0 for i in range(NUM_CLASSES)}
    for i in train_idx:
        _, mask_path = full_clean.pairs[i]
        m = cv2.imread(str(mask_path), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
        for c in range(NUM_CLASSES):
            class_counts[c] += int((m == c).sum())
    total = max(sum(class_counts.values()), 1)
    print('  Pixel distribution (training clean):')
    for c in range(NUM_CLASSES):
        pct = class_counts[c] / total * 100
        print(f'    {CLASS_NAMES[c]:14} {pct:5.2f}%')

    drought_idx    = CLASS_TO_IDX.get('drought', 6)
    cotton_idx     = CLASS_TO_IDX.get('cotton_ball', 3)

    # Drought weight override: force high weight so model must learn drought.
    drought_override = cfg_seg.get('drought_override_weight', 30.0)

    # Cotton_ball weight override: auto-computed weight (~32) was too high with
    # 151 clean tiles, causing cotton_ball false positives on bright soil pixels
    # (Balaji v4 showed 15.3% cotton vs 1.1% in v3). Lowering to 12 reduces
    # sensitivity while still giving adequate supervision for the rare class.
    cotton_override = cfg_seg.get('cotton_override_weight', None)

    overrides = {drought_idx: drought_override}
    if cotton_override is not None:
        overrides[cotton_idx] = float(cotton_override)

    class_weights = make_class_weights(
        class_counts, NUM_CLASSES,
        overrides=overrides,
    )
    print('  Computed class weights (drought + cotton overridden):')
    for ci, w in enumerate(class_weights.tolist()):
        print(f'    {CLASS_NAMES[ci]:14} {w:6.2f}')

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Model
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    print('\nBuilding SegFormer-B2 ...')
    model = SegFormerB2(
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
        input_size=crop_size,
        pretrained_id=cfg_seg.get('pretrained_id', 'nvidia/mit-b2'),
        pretrained_local_path=cfg_seg.get('pretrained_local_path'),
        in_channels=9,
    ).to(device)
    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()) / 1e6
    print(f'  parameters: {n_params:.1f}M')
