================================================================================
SOURCE FILE: src/utils/constants.py
================================================================================

"""Shared constants for the drought detection pipeline."""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Classes (must match configs/config.yaml exactly)
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
CLASS_NAMES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "background",   # 0 — outside valid field mask
    "soil",         # 1 — bare cultivated soil (barren merged here)
    "crop",         # 2 — healthy/maturing vegetation (maize, redgram, cotton)
    "cotton_ball",  # 3 — open cotton bolls (harvest indicator)
    "green_weed",   # 4
    "dry_weed",     # 5
    "drought",      # 6
)

NUM_CLASSES = len(CLASS_NAMES)

# index ↔ name maps
CLASS_TO_IDX: dict[str, int] = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(CLASS_NAMES)}
IDX_TO_CLASS: dict[int, str] = {i: name for i, name in enumerate(CLASS_NAMES)}

# RGB palette (uint8). Matches CLASS_NAMES order.
PALETTE: np.ndarray = np.array(
    [
        [0, 0, 0],          # 0 background — black
        [139, 90, 43],      # 1 soil — brown (barren merged)
        [0, 180, 0],        # 2 crop — green
        [255, 255, 255],    # 3 cotton_ball — white
        [140, 255, 140],    # 4 green_weed — light green
        [210, 170, 90],     # 5 dry_weed — tan
        [220, 30, 30],      # 6 drought — red
    ],
    dtype=np.uint8,
)

# Pairs the model historically confuses. Used by the margin loss in the classifier.
CONFUSION_PAIRS: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = (
    ("drought", "dry_weed"),
    ("drought", "soil"),      # barren merged into soil
    ("drought", "crop"),
    ("soil", "cotton_ball"),
    ("crop", "green_weed"),
)

# Ignore index for pseudo-mask training
IGNORE_INDEX: int = 255

# Folder name → class name (handles patches/ folder mapping)
PATCH_FOLDER_TO_CLASS: dict[str, str] = {
    "crop": "crop",
    "cotton_ball": "cotton_ball",
    "dry_weed": "dry_weed",
    "green_weed": "green_weed",
    "drought": "drought",
    "soil": "soil",
    # barren merged into soil — move barren/ images into soil/ before training
    # 'background' is computed at inference time, never has training patches
}




================================================================================
SUPPORTING SCRIPT: scripts/diagnose_pseudo_labels.py
================================================================================

"""
Pseudo-label diagnostic visualizer.

For each of N images, produces a single composite PNG showing:
    - Original image
    - Final mask overlay (with current confidence threshold)
    - Per-class probability heatmaps (7 separate panels)
    - Confidence map (per-pixel max probability)

This is the right tool to *see what the classifier actually thinks* about each
class, including pixels that fell below the confidence threshold and ended up
as 'ignore' in the final mask.

Usage:
    python scripts/diagnose_pseudo_labels.py --config configs/config.yaml --n 5 --shuffle
    python scripts/diagnose_pseudo_labels.py --config configs/config.yaml --images img_001.jpg,img_055.jpg
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import random
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
import torch
import yaml
from tqdm import tqdm

from src.classifier.model import MultiscalePatchClassifier
from src.pseudo_label.sliding_window import SlidingWindowInference
from src.utils.constants import (
    CLASS_NAMES,
    IGNORE_INDEX,
    NUM_CLASSES,
    PALETTE,
)
from src.pseudo_label.viz import (
    overlay_with_legend,
    add_stat_bar,
    apply_palette,
    draw_legend_bar,
)


def load_image(path: Path) -> np.ndarray | None:
    img = cv2.imread(str(path), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
    if img is None:
        return None
    return cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)


# make_overlay replaced by overlay_with_legend from viz.py


def make_composite(
    img: np.ndarray,
    prob_map: np.ndarray,
    confidence_threshold: float,
    save_path: Path,
    title: str = "",
) -> None:
    """
    Build composite diagnostic figure:
        Row 1: original | final overlay (with legend) | raw argmax | confidence
        Rows 2-3: per-class probability heatmaps (one per non-background class)
    """
    confidence = prob_map.max(axis=2)
    raw_mask   = prob_map.argmax(axis=2).astype(np.uint8)
    final_mask = raw_mask.copy()
    final_mask[confidence < confidence_threshold] = IGNORE_INDEX

    # Use viz.py for the overlay panels (includes legend)
    final_comp  = overlay_with_legend(img, final_mask, alpha=0.55, max_display_size=99999)
    raw_comp    = overlay_with_legend(img, raw_mask,   alpha=0.55, max_display_size=99999)

    # Compute stats for title
    valid      = final_mask != IGNORE_INDEX
    n_valid    = max(valid.sum(), 1)
    fracs_str  = "  ".join(
        f'{CLASS_NAMES[i][:5]}={((final_mask==i).sum()/n_valid):.0%}'
        for i in range(NUM_CLASSES)
        if (final_mask==i).sum()/n_valid > 0.005
    )
    ignore_pct = (final_mask == IGNORE_INDEX).sum() / final_mask.size

    # Layout: 3 rows × 4 cols
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(3, 4, figsize=(22, 13))
    fig.suptitle(
        f'{title}\nconf ≥ {confidence_threshold}: ignore={ignore_pct:.0%}  {fracs_str}',
        fontsize=11, y=0.997)

    # Row 1
    axes[0,0].imshow(img)
    axes[0,0].set_title('Original', fontsize=10); axes[0,0].axis('off')

    axes[0,1].imshow(final_comp)
    axes[0,1].set_title(f'Mask + legend (conf ≥ {confidence_threshold})', fontsize=10)
    axes[0,1].axis('off')

    axes[0,2].imshow(raw_comp)
    axes[0,2].set_title('Raw argmax + legend (no threshold)', fontsize=10)
    axes[0,2].axis('off')

    im_conf = axes[0,3].imshow(confidence, cmap='magma', vmin=0, vmax=1)
    axes[0,3].set_title('Confidence map\n(yellow=high, black=low)', fontsize=10)
    axes[0,3].axis('off')
    plt.colorbar(im_conf, ax=axes[0,3], fraction=0.04, pad=0.02)

    # Rows 2-3: per-class heatmaps
    plot_classes = [i for i, nm in enumerate(CLASS_NAMES) if nm != 'background']
    flat_axes    = list(axes[1].flatten()) + list(axes[2].flatten())
    for idx, cls_idx in enumerate(plot_classes):
        ax       = flat_axes[idx]
        cls_name = CLASS_NAMES[cls_idx]
        color    = tuple(v/255 for v in PALETTE[cls_idx])
        heat     = prob_map[:, :, cls_idx]
        im       = ax.imshow(heat, cmap='viridis', vmin=0, vmax=1)
        ax.set_title(
            f'P({cls_name})  max={heat.max():.2f} mean={heat.mean():.3f}',
            fontsize=9, color=color if max(color) > 0.3 else 'black')
        ax.axis('off')
        plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax, fraction=0.04, pad=0.02)

    for j in range(len(plot_classes), 8):
        flat_axes[j].axis('off')

    plt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0, 1, 0.995])
    save_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    plt.savefig(save_path, dpi=85, bbox_inches='tight')
    plt.close(fig)


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--config', type=Path, default=Path('configs/config.yaml'))
    ap.add_argument('--n', type=int, default=5,
                    help='Number of images to diagnose')
    ap.add_argument('--shuffle', action='store_true',
                    help='Pick random images instead of first N')
    ap.add_argument('--images', type=str, default=None,
                    help='Comma-separated list of specific image filenames')
    ap.add_argument('--seed', type=int, default=42)
    ap.add_argument('--confidence', type=float, default=None,
                    help='Override confidence threshold for visualization')
    ap.add_argument('--out-dir', type=Path, default=Path('runs/pseudo_labels/diagnostics'))
    args = ap.parse_args()

    with open(args.config) as f:
        config = yaml.safe_load(f)

    paths  = config['paths']
    cfg_pl = config['pseudo_label']
    cfg_cls = config['classifier']

    conf_thresh = (args.confidence if args.confidence is not None
                   else cfg_pl['confidence_threshold'])

    # Load classifier
    device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
    print(f'Device: {device}')
    ckpt_path = Path(paths['runs_dir']) / 'classifier' / 'best.pt'
    ckpt = torch.load(ckpt_path, map_location=device, weights_only=False)
    model = MultiscalePatchClassifier(
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES, pretrained=False,
        img_size=cfg_cls['input_size']
    ).to(device)
    model.load_state_dict(ckpt['model_state_dict'])
    model.eval()
    print(f'Loaded classifier (val F1: {ckpt.get("val_macro_f1","?")})')

    engine = SlidingWindowInference(
        model=model, device=device, batch_size=256,
        window_size=cfg_pl.get('window_size', 512),
        stride=cfg_pl.get('stride', 256),
        input_size=cfg_cls['input_size'],
        num_classes=NUM_CLASSES,
    )

    # Pick images
    unlabeled_dir = Path(paths['unlabeled_dir'])
    exts = {'.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.tif', '.tiff'}
    all_images = sorted([p for p in unlabeled_dir.rglob('*')
                         if p.is_file() and p.suffix.lower() in exts])

    if args.images:
        wanted = {n.strip() for n in args.images.split(',')}
        selected = [p for p in all_images if p.name in wanted]
        missing = wanted - {p.name for p in selected}
        if missing:
            print(f'WARNING: not found: {missing}')
    elif args.shuffle:
        random.Random(args.seed).shuffle(all_images)
        selected = all_images[:args.n]
    else:
        selected = all_images[:args.n]

    print(f'\nDiagnosing {len(selected)} image(s):')
    for p in selected:
        print(f'  - {p.name}')
    print(f'Confidence threshold for mask: {conf_thresh}')
    print()

    args.out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for img_path in tqdm(selected, desc='Diagnosing'):
        img = load_image(img_path)
        if img is None:
            print(f'  ⚠ skip {img_path.name}')
            continue
        prob_map = engine.run(img)
        out_path = args.out_dir / f'{img_path.stem}_diagnostic.png'
        make_composite(img, prob_map, conf_thresh, out_path, title=img_path.name)
        tqdm.write(f'  saved: {out_path}')

    print(f'\nDone. {len(selected)} composite figures in {args.out_dir}')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()




================================================================================
SUPPORTING SCRIPT: scripts/pseudo_annotate.py
================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Standalone Segmentation Annotation Tool
========================================
Replaces CVAT. Runs with just Python + OpenCV.
Starts from pseudo-label masks — you only correct what's wrong.

USAGE
-----
On the server (with display):
    python scripts/annotate.py --tiles-zip runs/pseudo_labels/cvat_tiles.zip \
                               --out data/clean_masks/

On your LOCAL machine (download the zip first):
    python scripts/annotate.py --tiles-zip ~/Desktop/cvat_tiles.zip \
                               --out ~/Desktop/clean_masks/

CONTROLS
--------
    1-7         Select class (see legend on right panel)
    0           Select IGNORE (uncertain — leave blank)
    Mouse drag  Paint with current class
    Scroll      Change brush size
    +  /  -     Change brush size (keyboard alternative)
    Z           Undo last stroke
    S           Save current mask and move to next image
    N           Next image without saving
    P           Previous image
    R           Reset to original pseudo-label
    Q / Esc     Quit (saves progress)

OUTPUT
------
    Saves PNG masks to --out directory.
    Pixel value = class index (0-6).  255 = ignore/uncertain.
    Filename matches the tile name (tile_000.png etc).
    These files go directly into data/clean_masks/ on the server.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import os
import sys
import zipfile
from io import BytesIO
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import numpy as np

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Class definitions (must match src/utils/constants.py)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CLASS_NAMES = [
    "background",   # 0  — black
    "soil",         # 1  — brown
    "crop",         # 2  — green
    "cotton_ball",  # 3  — white
    "green_weed",   # 4  — light green
    "dry_weed",     # 5  — tan
    "drought",      # 6  — red
]

# BGR colors for OpenCV display
PALETTE_BGR = [
    (0,   0,   0  ),   # 0 background
    (43,  90,  139),   # 1 soil
    (0,   180, 0  ),   # 2 crop
    (255, 255, 255),   # 3 cotton_ball
    (140, 255, 140),   # 4 green_weed
    (90,  170, 210),   # 5 dry_weed
    (30,  30,  220),   # 6 drought
]

IGNORE_IDX  = 255
NUM_CLASSES = len(CLASS_NAMES)
DISPLAY_W   = 1024   # width of each panel in the side-by-side display
LEGEND_W    = 220    # width of the right legend panel


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Load tiles from zip
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def load_tiles_from_zip(zip_path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    """Load all image+mask pairs from the cvat_tiles.zip."""
    tiles = []
    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path) as zf:
        names = zf.namelist()

        # Find image stems
        img_stems = sorted([
            Path(n).stem for n in names
            if n.startswith('JPEGImages/') and n.endswith('.jpg')
        ])

        if not img_stems:
            # Try fallback folder names from older zip versions
            img_stems = sorted([
                Path(n).stem for n in names
                if n.startswith('images/') and n.endswith('.jpg')
            ])
            img_folder  = 'images'
            mask_folder = 'masks'
        else:
            img_folder  = 'JPEGImages'
            mask_folder = 'SegmentationClass'

        for stem in img_stems:
            # Load image
            img_key = f'{img_folder}/{stem}.jpg'
            if img_key not in names:
                continue
            img_data  = zf.read(img_key)
            img_arr   = np.frombuffer(img_data, dtype=np.uint8)
            img_bgr   = cv2.imdecode(img_arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)

            # Load pseudo-label mask (may not exist if annotation not yet done)
            mask_key = f'{mask_folder}/{stem}.png'
            if mask_key in names:
                mask_data = zf.read(mask_key)
                mask_arr  = np.frombuffer(mask_data, dtype=np.uint8)
                mask      = cv2.imdecode(mask_arr, cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
            else:
                mask = np.full(img_bgr.shape[:2], IGNORE_IDX, dtype=np.uint8)

            # Load overlay for reference if available
            ovl_key = f'overlays/{stem}.jpg'
            if ovl_key in names:
                ovl_data = zf.read(ovl_key)
                ovl_arr  = np.frombuffer(ovl_data, dtype=np.uint8)
                overlay  = cv2.imdecode(ovl_arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
            else:
                overlay = None

            tiles.append({
                'stem':      stem,
                'img':       img_bgr,
                'mask':      mask.copy(),
                'orig_mask': mask.copy(),  # for reset
                'overlay':   overlay,
            })

    print(f"Loaded {len(tiles)} tiles from {zip_path.name}")
    return tiles


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Drawing helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def mask_to_color(mask: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Convert class-index mask to BGR color image."""
    color = np.zeros((*mask.shape, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
    for i, bgr in enumerate(PALETTE_BGR):
        color[mask == i] = bgr
    color[mask == IGNORE_IDX] = (80, 80, 80)  # dark grey = uncertain
    return color


def make_display(
    img: np.ndarray,
    mask: np.ndarray,
    cur_class: int,
    brush_size: int,
    tile_idx: int,
    n_tiles: int,
    stem: str,
    saved: bool,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Build the full display frame:
        [original image | overlay | legend panel]
    """
    H, W = img.shape[:2]
    scale = DISPLAY_W / W
    dH    = int(H * scale)
    dW    = DISPLAY_W

    # Resize for display
    img_disp  = cv2.resize(img, (dW, dH), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
    color     = mask_to_color(mask)
    overlay   = cv2.addWeighted(img_disp, 0.75,
                                cv2.resize(color, (dW, dH)), 0.35, 0)

    # Legend panel
    legend = make_legend(dH, cur_class, brush_size, tile_idx, n_tiles,
                         stem, saved)

    return np.hstack([img_disp, overlay, legend])


def make_legend(
    height: int,
    cur_class: int,
    brush_size: int,
    tile_idx: int,
    n_tiles: int,
    stem: str,
    saved: bool,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Build the right-side legend / info panel."""
    panel = np.full((height, LEGEND_W, 3), 40, dtype=np.uint8)
    font  = cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX
    y     = 30

    def txt(text, color=(210,210,210), scale=0.48, bold=False):
        nonlocal y
        cv2.putText(panel, text, (10, y), font, scale, color,
                    2 if bold else 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
        y += 22

    txt(f'Tile {tile_idx+1}/{n_tiles}', (255,220,80), scale=0.55, bold=True)
    txt(stem, (160,160,160), scale=0.40)
    status_col = (80,220,80) if saved else (80,180,255)
    txt('SAVED' if saved else 'unsaved', status_col, scale=0.45, bold=saved)
    y += 8

    txt('── CLASSES ──', (180,180,180), scale=0.43)
    for i, name in enumerate(CLASS_NAMES):
        bgr     = PALETTE_BGR[i]
        is_cur  = (i == cur_class)
        prefix  = f'[{i}]' if is_cur else f' {i} '
        label   = f'{prefix} {name}'
        col     = (255,255,255) if is_cur else (160,160,160)
        
        # Colour swatch
        sw_y1, sw_y2 = y-14, y+4
        cv2.rectangle(panel, (10, sw_y1), (26, sw_y2), bgr, -1)
        cv2.rectangle(panel, (10, sw_y1), (26, sw_y2), (120,120,120), 1)
        cv2.putText(panel, label, (30, y), font, 0.44, col,
                    2 if is_cur else 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
        if is_cur:
            cv2.rectangle(panel, (8, sw_y1-2), (LEGEND_W-8, sw_y2+2),
                          (255,255,0), 1)
        y += 22

    y += 6
    txt(f'[0] ignore/uncertain', (100,100,100), scale=0.40)
    y += 10
    txt('── CONTROLS ──', (180,180,180), scale=0.43)
    for ctrl in [
        '1-7  select class',
        '0    ignore',
        'drag paint',
        'scroll  brush size',
        '+/-  brush size',
        'Z    undo stroke',
        'S    save + next',
        'N    next (no save)',
        'P    previous',
        'R    reset to pseudo',
        'Q    quit',
    ]:
        if y > height - 20:
            break
        txt(ctrl, (130,130,130), scale=0.38)

    y += 6
    if y < height - 30:
        txt(f'brush: {brush_size}px', (180,180,100), scale=0.42)

    return panel


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Annotation state
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class Annotator:
    def __init__(self, tiles: list[dict], out_dir: Path):
        self.tiles      = tiles
        self.out_dir    = out_dir
        self.idx        = 0
        self.cur_class  = 6    # start on drought
        self.brush_size = 20
        self.drawing    = False
        self.last_pt    = None
        self.strokes: list[tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]] = []  # (pixels, old_vals)
        self.saved_set: set[int] = set()
        self.win        = 'Annotation Tool'
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # ---- properties ----

    @property
    def tile(self) -> dict:
        return self.tiles[self.idx]

    @property
    def mask(self) -> np.ndarray:
        return self.tile['mask']

    # ---- display ----

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        t     = self.tile
        frame = make_display(
            t['img'], t['mask'], self.cur_class, self.brush_size,
            self.idx, len(self.tiles), t['stem'],
            self.idx in self.saved_set,
        )
        cv2.imshow(self.win, frame)

    # ---- painting ----

    def _paint_circle(self, x: int, y: int) -> None:
        """Paint a circle at image coordinates (x, y)."""
        H, W = self.mask.shape
        scale  = self.tile['img'].shape[1] / DISPLAY_W
        img_x  = int(x * scale)
        img_y  = int(y * scale)

        # Build circle mask
        Y, X = np.ogrid[:H, :W]
        dist  = np.sqrt((X - img_x)**2 + (Y - img_y)**2)
        circ  = dist <= self.brush_size

        # Save for undo
        old_vals = self.mask[circ].copy()
        self.strokes.append((np.argwhere(circ), old_vals))

        val = IGNORE_IDX if self.cur_class == 0 else self.cur_class
        self.mask[circ] = val

    def mouse_callback(self, event, x, y, flags, param) -> None:
        if x < DISPLAY_W or x >= 2 * DISPLAY_W:
            return   # only paint in the overlay panel (middle)
        ox = x - DISPLAY_W   # offset into overlay panel

        if event == cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONDOWN:
            self.drawing = True
            self.last_pt = (ox, y)
            self._paint_circle(ox, y)
            self.refresh()
        elif event == cv2.EVENT_MOUSEMOVE and self.drawing:
            self._paint_circle(ox, y)
            self.last_pt = (ox, y)
            self.refresh()
        elif event == cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONUP:
            self.drawing = False
        elif event == cv2.EVENT_MOUSEWHEEL:
            if flags > 0:
                self.brush_size = min(self.brush_size + 5, 120)
            else:
                self.brush_size = max(self.brush_size - 5, 3)
            self.refresh()

    # ---- save ----

    def save_current(self) -> None:
        stem     = self.tile['stem']
        out_path = self.out_dir / f'{stem}.png'
        cv2.imwrite(str(out_path), self.tile['mask'])
        self.saved_set.add(self.idx)
        print(f'  Saved: {out_path}')

    # ---- navigation ----

    def next_tile(self) -> bool:
        if self.idx < len(self.tiles) - 1:
            self.idx += 1
            self.strokes.clear()
            return True
        return False

    def prev_tile(self) -> bool:
        if self.idx > 0:
            self.idx -= 1
            self.strokes.clear()
            return True
        return False

    # ---- run loop ----

    def run(self) -> None:
        # Initialize the window explicitly
        cv2.namedWindow(self.win, cv2.WINDOW_NORMAL)
        
        # Build an initial visual layout frame to show immediately 
        t = self.tile
        init_frame = make_display(
            t['img'], t['mask'], self.cur_class, self.brush_size,
            self.idx, len(self.tiles), t['stem'],
            self.idx in self.saved_set,
        )
        cv2.imshow(self.win, init_frame)
        
        # CRITICAL FIX: Cycle backend event loops to construct the window layout context
        for _ in range(10):
            cv2.waitKey(15)

        # Apply resizing safe window geometries
        cv2.resizeWindow(self.win, DISPLAY_W * 2 + LEGEND_W, 700)
        
        # Extra short cycle window configurations
        for _ in range(5):
            cv2.waitKey(15)

        # Safely bind context actions
        try:
            cv2.setMouseCallback(self.win, self.mouse_callback)
            print("Mouse callback attached successfully.")
        except cv2.error as e:
            print(f"\nFatal: Failed to lock UI mouse binding properties.\n{e}")
            raise

        print('\nAnnotation tool running.')
        print('Controls: 1-7 = select class, S = save+next, N = next, P = prev,')
        print('          Z = undo, R = reset, Q = quit, scroll = brush size\n')

        while True:
            key = cv2.waitKey(20) & 0xFF

            if key in (ord('q'), ord('Q'), 27):   # Q or Esc
                print(f'\nQuitting. Saved {len(self.saved_set)}/{len(self.tiles)} tiles.')
                break

            elif key in (ord('s'), ord('S')):      # Save + next
                self.save_current()
                if not self.next_tile():
                    print('All tiles done!')
                    break
                self.refresh()

            elif key in (ord('n'), ord('N')):      # Next without save
                if not self.next_tile():
                    print('Already at last tile.')
                self.refresh()

            elif key in (ord('p'), ord('P')):      # Previous
                self.prev_tile()
                self.refresh()

            elif key in (ord('z'), ord('Z')):      # Undo
                if self.strokes:
                    pixels, old_vals = self.strokes.pop()
                    for (py, px), v in zip(pixels, old_vals):
                        self.mask[py, px] = v
                    self.refresh()

            elif key in (ord('r'), ord('R')):      # Reset
                self.tile['mask'][:] = self.tile['orig_mask']
                self.strokes.clear()
                self.refresh()

            elif key in (ord('+'), ord('=')):      # Brush larger
                self.brush_size = min(self.brush_size + 5, 120)
                self.refresh()

            elif key in (ord('-'), ord('_')):      # Brush smaller
                self.brush_size = max(self.brush_size - 5, 3)
                self.refresh()

            elif key == ord('0'):                  # Ignore class
                self.cur_class = 0
                self.refresh()

            elif ord('1') <= key <= ord('7'):      # Class selection 1-7
                self.cur_class = key - ord('0')
                self.refresh()

            # Break if window close icon clicked
            if cv2.getWindowProperty(self.win, cv2.WND_PROP_VISIBLE) < 1:
                break

        cv2.destroyAllWindows()
        print(f'\nDone. Masks saved to: {self.out_dir}')


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Segmentation annotation tool')
    ap.add_argument('--tiles-zip', type=Path, required=True,
                    help='Path to cvat_tiles.zip')
    ap.add_argument('--out', type=Path, default=Path('clean_masks'),
                    help='Output directory for corrected masks (default: clean_masks/)')
    ap.add_argument('--start', type=int, default=0,
                    help='Start from tile index N (resume from previous session)')
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if not args.tiles_zip.exists():
        print(f'ERROR: {args.tiles_zip} not found.')
        sys.exit(1)

    tiles = load_tiles_from_zip(args.tiles_zip)
    if not tiles:
        print('ERROR: No tiles found in zip.')
        sys.exit(1)

    ann = Annotator(tiles, args.out)
    ann.idx = min(args.start, len(tiles) - 1)

    # Check for already-saved masks (resume support)
    for i, t in enumerate(tiles):
        if (args.out / f'{t["stem"]}.png').exists():
            ann.saved_set.add(i)
    if ann.saved_set:
        print(f'Resuming: {len(ann.saved_set)} tiles already saved.')

    ann.run()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
